### [1] [Turnpike with Uncertain Measurements: Triangle-Equality ILP with a Deterministic Recovery Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18283)
*C. S. Elder,Guillaume Marçais,Carl Kingsford*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 研究带不确定测量的Turnpike问题：在有界噪声和取整条件下，从无标签的成对距离多重集中重构一维点集。提出了基于三角形等式的组合可实现性特征，并开发了ILP/LP方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Turnpike问题（从成对距离重构点集）在存在测量噪声和取整误差的实际应用中具有挑战性。现有方法通常假设精确测量，而实际测量往往包含不确定性和量化误差。需要开发能够处理有界噪声和取整的鲁棒重构方法。

Method: 1. 提出组合可实现性特征：通过多匹配将区间索引标记为不同距离值，同时满足所有三角形等式。2. 基于三角形等式设计整数线性规划（ILP），其约束结构仅依赖于两分区集$\mathcal{P}_y$。3. 开发具有{0,1}系数约束的自然LP松弛。4. 积分解证明可实现性并输出显式分配矩阵，实现"分配优先，回归其次"的坐标估计流程。

Result: 1. 在有界噪声后取整的条件下，证明了确定性分离条件，在此条件下$\mathcal{P}_y$能够被精确恢复，使得ILP/LP接收与无噪声情况相同的组合输入。2. 实验展示了积分性行为以及在可证明范围之外的性能退化情况。

Conclusion: 该研究为带不确定测量的Turnpike问题提供了理论框架和实用算法。通过组合特征和ILP/LP方法，能够在有界噪声和取整条件下实现鲁棒重构。确定性分离条件为算法性能提供了理论保证，实验验证了方法的有效性及其局限性。

Abstract: We study Turnpike with uncertain measurements: reconstructing a one-dimensional point set from an unlabeled multiset of pairwise distances under bounded noise and rounding. We give a combinatorial characterization of realizability via a multi-matching that labels interval indices by distinct distance values while satisfying all triangle equalities. This yields an ILP based on the triangle equality whose constraint structure depends only on the two-partition set $\mathcal{P}_y=\{(r,s,t): y_r+y_s=y_t\}$ and a natural LP relaxation with $\{0,1\}$-coefficient constraints. Integral solutions certify realizability and output an explicit assignment matrix, enabling an assignment-first, regression-second pipeline for downstream coordinate estimation. Under bounded noise followed by rounding, we prove a deterministic separation condition under which $\mathcal{P}_y$ is recovered exactly, so the ILP/LP receives the same combinatorial input as in the noiseless case. Experiments illustrate integrality behavior and degradation outside the provable regime.

</details>


### [2] [Central Triangulation under Parallel Flip Operations: The CG:SHOP Challenge 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18812)
*Oswin Aichholzer,Joseph Dorfer,Sándor P. Fekete,Phillip Keldenich,Peter Kramer,Stefan Schirra*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 2026年计算几何挑战赛聚焦于在点集三角剖分中寻找平行翻转操作下的中心三角剖分问题，目标是找到与输入三角剖分集合在平行翻转距离上总和最小的中心三角剖分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该挑战赛旨在探索三角剖分中平行翻转操作的几何性质，解决在给定点集的多个三角剖分中寻找中心三角剖分的问题。平行翻转操作在计算几何中具有重要理论意义，研究其距离度量和中心性有助于理解三角剖分空间的结构特性。

Method: 采用平行翻转操作作为基本变换，定义三角剖分之间的距离度量。平行翻转是指同时交换凸四边形中一组边与其相对对角线的操作。挑战目标是设计算法，在给定点集的所有三角剖分集合中，找到最小化到所有输入三角剖分翻转距离总和的中心三角剖分。

Result: 摘要仅提供了挑战赛的问题描述和目标设定，未包含具体的算法结果或性能评估。挑战赛本身旨在激发对该问题的研究，因此结果部分需要参赛者通过算法实现来获得。

Conclusion: 2026年计算几何挑战赛提出了一个具有理论意义和算法挑战性的问题：在平行翻转距离度量下寻找中心三角剖分。该问题涉及三角剖分空间的几何结构和距离计算，为计算几何领域提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: We give an overview of the 2026 Computational Geometry Challenge targeting the problem of finding a Central Triangulation under Parallel Flip Operations in triangulations of point sets. A flip is the parallel exchange of a set of edges in a triangulation with opposing diagonals of the convex quadrilaterals containing them. The challenge objective was, given a set of triangulations of a fixed point set, to determine a central triangulation with respect to parallel flip distances. More precisely, this asks for a triangulation that minimizes the sum of flip distances to all elements of the input

</details>


### [3] [Hardness of High-Dimensional Linear Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19061)
*Alexander Munteanu,Simon Omlor,Jeff M. Phillips*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: 该论文针对最大半空间差异问题建立了新的指数维度下界，填补了现有多项式下界与指数维度上界之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最大半空间差异问题是计算几何和机器学习中的基本问题，但现有下界仅为多项式级别，无法解释已知的指数维度上界（O(n^d) 和 Õ(1/ε^d)）。这种差距需要被填补以更好地理解问题的计算复杂性。

Method: 通过从广泛认可的困难猜想（仿射退化测试和k-Sum问题）进行归约来建立下界。具体包括：1）基于仿射退化测试的归约得到Õ(n^d)和Õ(1/ε^d)下界；2）基于k-Sum假设的归约得到Õ(n^{d/2})和Õ(1/ε^{d/2})下界。

Result: 获得了匹配上界的指数维度下界：基于仿射退化测试得到Õ(n^d)和Õ(1/ε^d)下界；基于k-Sum假设得到Õ(n^{d/2})和Õ(1/ε^{d/2})下界。第一个下界在限制于侧面性查询的计算模型中无条件成立。

Conclusion: 该研究填补了最大半空间差异问题计算复杂性分析中的关键空白，为指数维度依赖提供了理论支持，对计算几何和机器学习算法的设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: We establish new exponential in dimension lower bounds for the Maximum Halfspace Discrepancy problem, which models linear classification. Both are fundamental problems in computational geometry and machine learning in their exact and approximate forms. However, only $O(n^d)$ and respectively $\tilde O(1/\varepsilon^d)$ upper bounds are known and complemented by polynomial lower bounds that do not support the exponential in dimension dependence. We close this gap up to polylogarithmic terms by reduction from widely-believed hardness conjectures for Affine Degeneracy testing and $k$-Sum problems. Our reductions yield matching lower bounds of $\tildeΩ(n^d)$ and respectively $\tildeΩ(1/\varepsilon^d)$ based on Affine Degeneracy testing, and $\tildeΩ(n^{d/2})$ and respectively $\tildeΩ(1/\varepsilon^{d/2})$ conditioned on $k$-Sum. The first bound also holds unconditionally if the computational model is restricted to make sidedness queries, which corresponds to a widely spread setting implemented and optimized in many contemporary algorithms and computing paradigms.

</details>

### [4] [Spin-Charge Groups for Fermions in Fluids and Crystals: General Structures and Physical Consequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18125)
*Arist Zhenyuan Yang,Zheng-Xin Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出自旋-电荷群(SCG)作为描述费米子系统最一般对称性的新框架，涵盖自旋、电荷、时空自由度及其耦合，可应用于多种物理系统并产生新的物相分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知的对称群不足以处理费米子系统中自旋、电荷和空间自由度之间的各种耦合，需要发展更一般的对称性理论框架来填补这一空白。

Method: 引入自旋-电荷群(SCG)，包含自旋和电荷操作作为"内部"对称性，时空操作作为"外部"对称性，它们之间的耦合以及投影扭曲，并研究其一般群结构。

Result: SCG可应用于具体物理系统如³He超流体、电荷-4e超导体、具有自旋流的共线磁体、共存磁序的超导体等，能产生额外的能带简并、陈数和交叉自旋-电荷响应如自旋超流。

Conclusion: SCG为分类和探索新物相提供了途径，即使在强相互作用存在时也适用，扩展了对称性在凝聚态物理中的应用范围。

Abstract: Known symmetry groups are not sufficient to handle various couplings between spin, charge and spatial degrees of freedom for fermions. To fill in this gap, we introduce the spin-charge groups (SCG) to describe the most general symmetries of fermionic systems. These groups are composed of the spin and charge operations as the `internal' symmetries, the spatial and temporal operations as `external' symmetries, couplings between them, and projective twists. After providing the general group structures of SCG, we study their potential applications in concrete physical systems, including $^3$He superfluids, charge-4e superconductors, collinear magnets with spin-fluxes, and superconductors with coexisting magnetic orders. We show that SCG can give rise to extra band degeneracies, Chern numbers and cross spin-charge responses like spin-supercurrents. Hence SCG provide a route to classify and explore new phases of matter even when strong interactions are included.

</details>


### [5] [Disentangling Shear and Compression Phonons: Route to Anomalous Magnetothermal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18137)
*Haoting Xu,Antoine Matar,Hae-Young Kee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究了强自旋轨道耦合下受对称性约束的自旋-晶格耦合如何通过模式选择性的自旋-声子相互作用调控热输运，解释了受挫磁体中磁场依赖的热输运异常现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受挫磁体中的磁热输运表现出与常规磁振子或声子输运不同的显著磁场依赖异常现象，需要从微观机制上解释这些异常。

Method: 在强自旋轨道耦合极限下推导有效自旋-声子哈密顿量，其中不同极化的声子选择性地耦合到不同的自旋算符；结合Landauer输运框架和自旋链与声子浴耦合的精确对角化方法。

Result: 压缩和剪切声子模式在不同磁场区域对自旋热流有不同贡献，产生了热流磁场依赖性的特征峰-谷-峰结构，为自旋轨道耦合Mott绝缘体中的场致输运异常提供了微观解释。

Conclusion: 对称性约束的自旋-晶格耦合通过模式选择性的自旋-声子相互作用控制热输运，这为理解受挫磁体中磁场依赖的热输运异常现象提供了关键机制。

Abstract: Magnetothermal transport in various frustrated magnets exhibits striking field-dependent anomalies that deviate from conventional magnon or phonon transport. Here we show that symmetry-constrained spin-lattice coupling naturally leads to mode-selective spin-phonon interactions that control heat transport. In the strong spin-orbit coupling limit, we derive an effective spin-phonon Hamiltonian in which phonons with different polarizations couple selectively to distinct spin operators. As a result, compression and shear phonon modes contribute to spin heat current across different magnetic-field regimes. Using a Landauer transport framework combined with exact diagonalization of spin chains coupled to a phonon bath, we show that this mechanism produces a characteristic peak-dip-peak structure in the field dependence of heat current, providing a microscopic explanation for field-induced transport anomalies in spin-orbit-coupled Mott insulators.

</details>


### [6] [Removing nodal and support-mismatch pathologies in Variational Monte Carlo via blurred sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18148)
*Zhou-Quan Wan,Roeland Wiersema,Shiwei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出模糊采样方法解决变分蒙特卡洛中因波函数节点导致的统计病理问题，提高VMC和t-VMC计算的鲁棒性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分蒙特卡洛方法在处理具有节点的量子波函数时，随机估计器会出现不稳定或有偏的问题，导致优化轨迹不可靠或变分动力学错误，限制了数值模拟的能力

Method: 引入模糊采样方法，这是一种后处理技术，不需要修改底层采样器，具有最小开销，能够处理波函数节点导致的统计病理问题

Result: 在标准采样方法已知会失效的几个代表性示例中证明了模糊采样的有效性，并将其应用于大规模自旋动力学问题

Conclusion: 模糊采样为鲁棒的VMC和t-VMC计算建立了一个广泛适用的框架，解决了波函数节点导致的统计病理问题

Abstract: Variational Monte Carlo (VMC) is a powerful and fast-growing method for optimizing and evolving parameterized many-body wave functions, especially with modern neural-network quantum states. In practice, however, the stochastic estimators that form the backbone of the method can become unstable or biased due to the presence of nodes, a ubiquitous feature of quantum wave functions. In the continuum, this results in heavy-tailed estimators with potentially divergent variances, while in discrete Hilbert spaces the sampling distribution can miss parts of the support needed to form unbiased estimators. These statistical pathologies lead to unreliable optimization trajectories in stochastic reconfiguration or incorrect variational dynamics in time-dependent Variational Monte Carlo (t-VMC), and severely limit the power of the numerical simulations. We introduce blurred sampling to address these difficulties. The method has a number of rigorous properties that make it well-behaved, effective and efficient. Additionally it is a post-processing approach that can be used without modifying the underlying sampler and incurs only minimal overhead. We demonstrate its effectiveness on several representative examples where standard sampling approaches are known to fail, and apply it to large-scale problems in spin dynamics. This work establishes a broadly applicable framework for robust VMC and t-VMC calculations.

</details>


### [7] [Polaron-driven switching of octupolar order in doped 5d$^2$ double perovskite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18155)
*Dario Fiore Mosca,Lorenzo Celiberti,Leonid V. Pourovskii,Cesare Franchini*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过第一性原理计算，研究了掺杂诱导的小极化子如何影响5d²双钙钛矿Ba₂CaOsO₆的低温多极序，发现极化子反转了主导的八极交换作用，解释了Na掺杂对铁八极序的抑制效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究掺杂诱导的小极化子对5d²双钙钛矿Ba₂CaOsO₆低温多极序的影响，旨在澄清先前实验观测中模糊的现象，探索通过电荷掺杂调控自旋轨道纠缠材料中位点间交换相互作用的新途径。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算5d¹局域空穴极化子与5d²磁性离子之间的位点间交换相互作用，构建相应的有效哈密顿量并进行求解，分析Na掺杂对系统的影响。

Result: 计算表明掺杂诱导的小极化子将主导的八极交换作用从铁磁性反转为反铁磁性，这解释了Na掺杂对铁八极序的渐进抑制和有序温度的降低，澄清了先前实验观测的模糊性。

Conclusion: 小极化子形式的电荷掺杂为调控自旋轨道纠缠材料中的位点间交换相互作用提供了可行途径，能够实现新型量子序的出现，这一发现对理解掺杂对多极序的影响具有重要意义。

Abstract: We investigate how doping-induced small polarons impact the low-temperature multipolar orders of the $5d^2$ double perovskite Ba$_2$CaOsO$_6$. By computing intersite exchange interactions between 5d$^1$ localized hole polarons and 5d$^2$ magnetic ions from first principles, we demonstrate the reversal of the dominant octupolar exchange from ferromagnetic to antiferromagnetic. Solving the corresponding effective Hamiltonian we find this reversal to account for the progressive suppression of the ferro-octupolar order and the reduction of the ordering temperature upon Na doping. These findings clarify previously ambiguous experimental observations and demonstrate that charge doping in the form of small polarons offers a viable route to tuning intersite exchange interactions in spin-orbit-entangled materials, enabling the emergence of novel quantum orders.

</details>


### [8] [Tackling the Sign Problem in the Doped Hubbard Model with Normalizing Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18205)
*Dominic Schuh,Lena Funcke,Janik Kreit,Thomas Luu,Simran Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于归一化流和退火方案的新方法，解决了有限化学势下Hubbard模型模拟中的符号问题和遍历性问题，相比传统方法显著降低了统计不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限化学势下的Hubbard模型是理解掺杂关联系统的基石，但模拟受到符号问题的严重限制。在辅助场表述中，自旋基虽然能缓解符号问题，但严重的遍历性问题限制了其应用。

Method: 将半填充条件下的归一化流方法扩展到有限化学势，引入了退火方案以实现遍历性采样。该方法在自旋基中工作，相比传统的电荷基混合蒙特卡洛方法具有优势。

Result: 与电荷基中最先进的混合蒙特卡洛方法相比，该方法能准确再现精确对角化结果，同时将统计不确定性降低了一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该方法为掺杂关联系统的模拟开辟了新途径，解决了有限化学势下Hubbard模型模拟中的关键挑战。

Abstract: The Hubbard model at finite chemical potential is a cornerstone for understanding doped correlated systems, but simulations are severely limited by the sign problem. In the auxiliary-field formulation, the spin basis mitigates the sign problem, yet severe ergodicity issues have limited its use. We extend recent advances with normalizing flows at half-filling to finite chemical potential by introducing an annealing scheme enabling ergodic sampling. Compared to state-of-the-art hybrid Monte Carlo in the charge basis, our approach accurately reproduces exact diagonalization results while reducing statistical uncertainties by an order of magnitude, opening a new path for simulations of doped correlated systems.

</details>


### [9] [Continuous symmetry analysis and systematic identification of candidate order parameters for interacting fermion models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18285)
*Cheng-Hao He,Yi-Zhuang You,Xiao Yan Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出一个系统框架，用于分析多自由度相互作用费米子系统的连续对称性，并通过Majorana表示和半单李代数理论识别候选序参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有多个内部自由度的相互作用费米子系统中，确定完整的连续对称群和分类可能的序参量仍然具有挑战性，而对称性在现代物理中起着核心作用。

Method: 通过将哈密顿量映射到Majorana表示，从与哈密顿量对易的算符的李代数中获取连续对称性的生成元；利用半单李代数理论识别该李代数的结构；基于表示理论开发系统方法，通过分解Majorana空间上对称代数诱导的外幂表示并结合离散晶格对称性，穷举枚举候选序参量。

Result: 建立了一个系统框架，能够分析连续对称性并识别候选序参量，这些序参量根据它们破坏的对称性进行分类。

Conclusion: 该工作为相互作用费米子系统的对称性分析和序参量分类提供了一个系统框架，结合了Majorana表示、李代数理论和表示理论，有望应用于具体模型研究。

Abstract: Symmetry plays a central role in modern physics, from classifying quantum states to characterizing phases of matter through spontaneous symmetry breaking. In interacting fermionic systems with multiple internal degrees of freedom, however, determining the full continuous symmetry group and classifying possible order parameters remain challenging. In this work, we present a systematic framework for analyzing continuous symmetries and identifying candidate order parameters in such systems. By mapping the Hamiltonian to a Majorana representation, we obtain the generators of continuous symmetries from the Lie algebra of operators that commute with the Hamiltonian. We then identify the structure of this Lie algebra using the theory of semisimple Lie algebras. Building on representation theory, we further develop a systematic method for exhaustively enumerating candidate order parameters. By decomposing the exterior-power representations induced by the symmetry algebra on the Majorana space and incorporating discrete lattice symmetries, we classify these order parameters according to the symmetries they break. (Abridged. Please see the PDF manuscript for the complete abstract and specific model applications.)

</details>


### [10] [Spatially Indirect Exciton Condensation in Two-Dimensional Strongly Correlated Semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18445)
*Yao Zeng,Shi-Cong Mo,Wéi Wú*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究二维三角晶格半金属中间接激子凝聚，发现强在位库仑相互作用U会显著抑制凝聚温度Tc，且多轨道模型中电子-空穴配对通道的竞争进一步抑制Tc，有助于解释过渡金属硫族化合物中强结合能与低转变温度之间的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来识别激子绝缘体基态材料是凝聚态物理的重要研究方向。过渡金属硫族化合物（如1T-TiSe2、Ta2Pd3Te5、Ta2NiSe5）是潜在候选材料，其低能物理由受强在位库仑相互作用的d轨道电子主导。这些材料中强结合能与相对较低的转变温度之间存在显著差异，需要深入理解强关联d壳层电子系统中的激子凝聚机制。

Method: 采用动力学平均场理论（DMFT）与行列式量子蒙特卡洛方法相结合，研究二维三角晶格半金属中的间接激子凝聚。构建了包含强在位相互作用（U）和轨道间相互作用（V）的双轨道和三轨道Hubbard模型，系统分析电子-空穴配对行为。

Result: 1. 在位Hubbard相互作用U会强烈抑制激子凝聚温度Tc，这种效应在较高电子-空穴对密度时尤为显著；2. 与无U情况（Tc随对密度增加而增长）形成鲜明对比；3. 在三轨道模型中，多个电子-空穴配对通道之间存在竞争，进一步抑制激子凝聚的Tc；4. 识别出轨道选择性的电子-空穴配对态。

Conclusion: 强在位库仑相互作用和多轨道配对通道竞争是抑制激子凝聚温度的关键机制，这有助于解释过渡金属硫族化合物材料中间接激子的强结合能与相对较低转变温度之间的巨大差异。研究结果为理解和设计具有强关联d壳层电子的材料中的激子凝聚提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Identifying materials hosting an excitonic insulator ground state has been one of the major pursuits in condensed matter physics in recent years.
  Promising candidates in transition metal chalcogenide compounds (TMC), including $1T-\mathrm{TiSe_2}$, $\mathrm{Ta_2Pd_3Te_5}$, and $\mathrm{Ta_2NiSe_5}$, share a crucial common characteristic: their low-energy physics is governed by electrons in $d-$ orbitals subject to strong on-site Coulomb interactions.
  In this work, we investigate spatially indirect exciton condensation in two-dimensional semimetals on triangular lattice. Using a combination of dynamical mean-field theory and the determinant quantum Monte Carlo method, we study two- and three-orbital Hubbard models incorporating strong on-site ($U$) and inter-orbital interactions ($V$). Our results demonstrate that on-site Hubbard $U$ can strongly suppress the condensation temperature $T_c$, an effect that is particularly pronounced at higher electron-hole pair densities. This behavior contrasts sharply with the case without on-site $U$, where $T_c$ grows with pair density at fixed $V$.
  Moreover, we uncover competition among multiple electron-hole pairing channels in the three-orbital model, which also acts to suppress $T_c$ of exciton condensation. An orbital-selective electron-hole pairing state is identified. These findings may help explain the large discrepancy between strong binding-energy and relative low transition temperature for indirect excitons in TMCs materials, offering important insights for understanding and engineering exciton condensation in materials with strongly correlated $d-$ shell electrons.

</details>

Abstract: The interplay between local and itinerant electrons underpins many correlated and topological quantum states. Kagome lattices provide an ideal platform by hosting both flat (localized states) and dispersive bands (itinerant states), yet direct spectroscopic evidence of their dynamical coupling has remained elusive. Here we report the long-sought flat band resonance in the quasi-two-dimensional kagome bilayer material CsCr6Sb6. Using angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, transport measurements, and combined density functional theory and dynamical mean-field theory, we identify coexisting flat band doublets and dispersive bands near the Fermi energy. Upon cooling, the flat and dispersive bands exhibit a pronounced enhancement of spectral weight and hybridization, directly evidencing flat band resonance. Crucially, this emergence coincides with the onset of short-range antiferromagnetic correlations, contrasting sharply with conventional Kondo lattice behavior. Our findings demonstrate not only the long-sought flat band resonance in kagome materials, but also its unconventional correlation with magnetism.

According to the density functional theory calculations, the orbital characters of the FSs were determined from their shape and orientations. We found that the size of the FS is strongly modified for the d$_{xz}$ orbital, suggesting the orbital-dependent correlation effect. These results provide an electronic basis for exploring the interplay of antiferromagnetic/charge density wave order and possible unconventional superconductivity in this compound.

Abstract: We theoretically study photoemission spectra for photodoped one-dimensional Mott insulators that can host excitons, and show that their spectral characteristics differ qualitatively from those of photodoped semiconductors. In conventional semiconductors, photoemission spectra are well understood; free charge carriers generate spectral weight near the bottom of the conduction band, while the formation of excitons leads to replica features of the valence band appearing inside the band gap. In one-dimensional Mott insulators, on the other hand, strong correlations give rise to fractionalized elementary excitations-spinons, holons, and doublons-which fundamentally modify the photoemission response. We find that when photodoped carriers, i.e., doublons and holons, remain unbound, the photoemission spectrum directly reflects the dispersion of spinons, i.e., magnetic elementary excitations. In contrast, when a doublon and a holon form an excitonic bound state, replica structures of the lower Hubbard band emerge inside the Mott gap, carrying contributions from both spinon and holon excitations. Importantly, the distribution of the in-gap signal depends sensitively on the degree of doublon-holon binding. The origin of these spectral features is clarified through a combination of exact diagonalization and the slave-particle approach. These results indicate that photoemission from photoinduced carriers and excitons in strongly correlated electron systems can provide information on magnetic properties and carrier-binding properties.

</details>

Abstract: The intriguing interplay between competing degrees of freedom in frustrated magnets can lead to non-trivial magnetic phenomena with exotic low-energy excitations that are highly relevant for addressing some of the fundamental questions in quantum condensed matter as well as potential technological applications. Herein, we report the synthesis and thermodynamic results on a frustrated magnet Co3ZnNb2O9. The Co2+ moments constitute buckled AB-type honeycomb layers in the ab-plane. The temperature-dependent magnetic susceptibility shows a sharp anomaly at 14 K, indicating the onset of long-range magnetic ordering. The Curie-Weiss fit of the magnetic susceptibility above 100 K, yields a Curie-Weiss temperature of -70 K, suggesting strong antiferromagnetic (AFM) interactions between the Co2+ spins and an effective magnetic moment of 5.54 muB, indicating the presence of unquenched orbital angular momentum. A field-induced spin-flop-like metamagnetic transition below the ordering temperature is characterized by a critical magnetic field of 1.2 T. The specific heat shows a lambda-type anomaly at 14 K, confirming the presence of long-range magnetic ordering, due to finite interlayer interaction. Interestingly, our study of the magnetocaloric effect near the transition temperature revealed an entropy change of 2.81 J/kg.K, which is ascribed to competing interactions, underlying anisotropy, and reduced net magnetization lead to relatively small isothermal entropy changes that suggest that frustrated honeycomb magnets are promising contenders for field-induced exotic phases and magnetocaloric response.

Conclusion: 该框架为熵计算提供了高效且广泛适用的途径，促进了掺杂哈伯德模型中复杂关联态的热力学表征。

Abstract: We develop a highly efficient framework for computing the thermal entropy in the doped Fermi-Hubbard model within the grand-canonical ensemble. The framework comprises four calculation schemes that express the entropy as path integrals in the parameter space of temperature, interaction strength, and chemical potential. The integrands involve only fundamental observables, including the total energy, fermion density, and double occupancy, which are readily accessible in a wide range of theoretical and numerical methods. We further derive useful Maxwell relations connecting the entropy to other quantities, and present practical formulas for directly evaluating the grand potential. As an application, we compute the entropy of the doped Hubbard model in two and three dimensions, using the numerically unbiased auxiliary-field quantum Monte Carlo method. The test results show excellent agreement across the different schemes and quantitatively verify the Maxwell relations, confirming the reliability of the framework. In two dimensions, we further benchmark our entropy results in physically relevant parameter regimes against diagrammatic Monte Carlo calculations and observe excellent quantitative consistency between the two approaches. By providing an efficient and broadly applicable route for entropy evaluation, our work facilitates the thermodynamic characterization of complex correlated states in the doped Hubbard model.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Erdős问题1148：证明每个足够大的整数n都能表示为n=x²+y²-z²，且满足max(x²,y²,z²)≤n的条件

Method: 将问题转化为在实双曲面b²-4ac=4n的固定相对紧开补丁中寻找正判别式4n的原始二元二次型，然后应用Duke定理的精确点计数形式（来自Einsiedler-Lindenstrauss-Michel-Venkatesh的测度论对偶性），最后通过有限奇偶性修正回到原始三元变量

Result: 证明了每个足够大的整数n都能表示为n=x²+y²-z²，且满足max(x²,y²,z²)≤n，从而完全解决了Erdős问题1148

Conclusion: 通过将数论问题转化为几何问题并应用现代遍历理论和自守形式理论中的深刻结果，成功解决了Erdős提出的一个长期未决的表示问题

Abstract: We prove that every sufficiently large integer $n$ can be written in the form $n=x^2+y^2-z^2$ with $\textrm{max}(x^2,y^2,z^2)\le n$. The proof converts the problem into finding a primitive binary quadratic form of positive discriminant $4n$ inside a fixed relatively compact open patch of the real hyperboloid $b^2-4ac=4n$. This is then supplied by Duke's theorem in the precise point-counting form deduced from the measure-theoretic duality of Einsiedler-Lindenstrauss-Michel-Venkatesh. A finite parity correction returns to the original ternary variables. This settles Erdős Problem 1148.

</details>

### [18] [Why Eight Percent of Benford Sequences Never Converge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18243)
*James M. Hyman*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究Benford序列b^n中多位数字相关性，通过条件互信息(CMI)测量相关性，基于log_10(b)的连分数展开将底数分类为收敛型和持久型，发现5.3%的底数具有持久相关性，证明了CMI偏差的有界性并验证了二次缩放率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Benford序列b^n中多位数字之间的相关性，特别是探索不同底数下相关性的持久性特征，理解数字序列的统计特性及其与底数数学性质的关系。

Method: 使用条件互信息(CMI)测量多位数相关性，基于log_10(b)的连分数展开定义共振比来分类底数，通过大量数值计算(996个底数，样本深度达200,000)验证理论结果，并利用分布误差定理证明CMI偏差的有界性。

Result: 在996个底数中，84个(8.4%)在N=10,000时表现出持久相关性，其中53个(5.3%)在N=200,000时确认为真正持久；证明了CMI偏差有界于分布误差，验证了有效缩放为二次(β=2)；收敛型底数的有效指数为β_eff=1.72±0.19。

Conclusion: Benford序列的相关性可通过log_10(b)的连分数展开分类，持久相关性相对罕见(约5%)；CMI偏差有界且缩放为二次；持久型底数的收敛阈值极高，使得渐近极限在计算范围内观测不到；推测持久率收敛于1/12，与Gauss-Kuzmin分布一致。

Abstract: We study multi-digit correlations in Benford sequences b^n for integer bases 2 <= b <= 1000, measuring dependence via conditional mutual information (CMI). A resonance ratio derived from the continued fraction expansion of log_10(b) classifies bases into convergent and persistent regimes (Theorem 3.13): among 996 bases surveyed, 84 (8.4%) exhibit persistent correlations at sample depth N = 10,000, and extended computation to N = 200,000 confirms 53 (5.3%) as genuinely persistent. We prove that CMI deviation is bounded by the distribution error (Theorem 3.4); exhaustive computation across 2,988 test cases confirms that the effective scaling is quadratic, yielding a two-sided rate beta = 2 for bounded-type bases (conditional on a computationally verified Hessian positivity condition). The observed effective exponent across 774 convergent bases is beta_eff = 1.72 +/- 0.19, consistent with finite-sample corrections to the asymptotic rate. We conjecture that the persistence rate converges to 1/12, a prediction grounded in the Gauss-Kuzmin distribution of partial quotients. For persistent bases, the convergence threshold N_epsilon exceeds 10^6 at standard precision, rendering the asymptotic limit observationally irrelevant within our computational scope.

</details>


### [19] [Norm-trace and Kloosterman sums in finite semi-simple algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18511)
*Daqing Wan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 论文将有限半单代数中给定迹和范数的元素计数渐近公式从交换情形（有限étale代数）推广到非交换情形（有限半单代数），得到带平方根误差项的公式，并应用于半单代数上的Kloosterman和估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将有限étale代数（交换情形）中给定迹和范数的元素计数结果推广到更一般的有限半单代数（非交换情形），建立非交换代数中的类似渐近公式。

Method: 应用Eichler公式处理一般线性群上的Gauss和，结合Hasse-Davenport关系，将问题约化到经典的几何情形（已知结果成立）。

Result: 获得了有限半单代数中给定迹和范数的元素计数的渐近公式，带有平方根误差项；作为约化的应用，得到了半单代数上Kloosterman和的平方根估计。

Conclusion: 成功将迹-范数计数问题从交换代数推广到非交换半单代数，建立了带平方根误差项的渐近公式，并讨论了将范数-迹替换为乘积-迹时的类似平方根估计，提出了有限半单代数上乘积-迹计数的新猜想。

Abstract: An asymptotic formula with a square root error term is obtained for the number of elements with given trace and norm in a finite semisimple algebra over a finite field. This extends previous results from finite etale algebras (commutative case) to finite semi-simple algebras (non-commutative case). The main idea is to apply the Eichler formula for Gauss sums over the general linear group and the Hasse-Davenport relation to reduce the problem to the classical geometric case where the result is known to be true. As an application of this reduction, we also obtain a square root estimate for Kloosterman sums over semi-simple algebras. Similar square root estimates are discussed when norm-trace is replaced by product-trace, leading to a new conjecture on product-trace counting over finite semi-simple algebras.

</details>

Abstract: We prove a geometric local constancy theorem for affine Springer fibers in families of close local fields. Consequently, stable orbital integrals are locally constant in these families, and both the base change fundamental lemma and the standard endoscopic fundamental lemma transfer from characteristic zero to arbitrary positive characteristic.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 1. 构造PGSO(8)的三元性扭椭圆内窥数据；2. 构造G₂的椭圆内窥数据；3. 基于这些构造和迹公式的一般框架建立相关迹公式；4. 利用PGSO(8)的三元性性质分析其自守表示；5. 通过比较PGSO(8)的三元性扭迹公式与G₂的标准迹公式进行推导。

Result: 1. 建立了PGSO(8)的三元性扭迹公式（包括离散部分）；2. 获得了PGSO(8)自守表示的粗分类；3. 通过比较推导出G₂自守表示的相应粗分类。

Abstract: In this paper, we establish the triality twisted trace formula for PGSO(8), including its discrete part, and obtain a coarse classification of its automorphic representations by combining the properties of triality. By comparing the standard trace formula for G_2 with the triality twisted trace formula for PGSO(8), we derive a corresponding coarse classification for automorphic representations of G_2. Specifically, we construct the triality-twisted elliptic endoscopic data for PGSO(8), and the elliptic endoscopic data for G_2. Based on these constructions and the general framework of trace formulas, we establish the relevant trace formulas. Utilizing the triality property of PGSO(8), we obtain a coarse classification of its automorphic representations, which in turn yields a coarse classification for those of G_2.

</details>


### [22] [Simplest cubic fields with small class number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18802)
*Akinari Hoshi,Hiroaki Iida*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文通过计算验证了最简单三次域类数的分布情况，特别关注了指数[O_L_m:Z[α]]为1、3、27时类数h_m≤1000的整数m数量，以及在-1≤m≤4×10^6范围内类数小于16的具体分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究最简单三次域L_m=Q(α)的类数分布规律，其中α是多项式f_m(X)=X^3-mX^2-(m+3)X-1的根。通过计算验证类数与指数[O_L_m:Z[α]]之间的关系，以及在小类数范围内的具体分布情况。

Method: 使用PARI/GP计算工具进行数值计算。首先分析指数[O_L_m:Z[α]]与导子f_m的关系：当m^2+3m+9=f_m时指数为1，等于3f_m时指数为3，等于27f_m时指数为27。然后在这些条件下统计类数h_m≤1000的整数m数量。最后在-1≤m≤4×10^6范围内统计类数小于16的具体分布。

Result: 1. 当[O_L_m:Z[α]]=1时，存在581个整数m≥-1使得h_m≤1000；当指数为3时，存在80个；当指数为27时，存在142个。
2. 在-1≤m≤4×10^6范围内，共有137个整数m的类数h_m<16，具体分布为：h_m=1有26个，h_m=3有31个，h_m=4有10个，h_m=7有10个，h_m=9有36个，h_m=12有21个，h_m=13有3个。

Conclusion: 该研究通过大量计算揭示了最简单三次域类数的分布特征，验证了指数[O_L_m:Z[α]]与类数之间的关系，并给出了在小类数范围内的具体数值分布。这些结果为理解最简单三次域的算术性质提供了重要的计算证据。

Abstract: Let $m\in\mathbb{Z}$ be an integer and $L_m=\mathbb{Q}(α)$ be the simplest cubic field with class number $h_m$ and conductor $\mathfrak{f}_m$ where $α$ is a root of $f_m(X)=X^3-mX^2-(m+3)X-1$. Let $\mathcal{O}_{L_m}$ be the ring of integers of $L_m$. By using PARI/GP, we confirm that if $[\mathcal{O}_{L_m}:\mathbb{Z}[α]]=1$ $($resp. $3$, $27$$)$, i.e. $m^2+3m+9=\mathfrak{f}_m$ $($resp. $3\mathfrak{f}_m$, $27\mathfrak{f}_m$$)$, then there exist exactly $581$ (resp. $80$, $142$) integers $m\geq -1$ such that $h_m\leq 1000$. We also show that if $-1\leq m\leq 4\cdot 10^6$, then $h_m<16$ holds for $137=26+31+10+10+36+21+3$ integers $m$. More precisely, there exist $26$ $($resp. $31$, $10$, $10$, $36$, $21$, $3$$)$ integers $m$ with $-1\leq m\leq 4\cdot 10^6$ such that $h_m=1$ $($resp. $3$, $4$, $7$, $9$, $12$, $13$$)$ which are given explicitly.

</details>


### [23] [Mahler's method and Carlitz logarithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18832)
*Guillaume Estienne*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文为Papanikolas关于Carlitz对数代数独立性定理提供了基于Mahler方法的新证明，替代了原有的t-模理论证明


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Papanikolas在2007年证明了Carlitz对数在有理函数域上线性独立蕴含代数独立，但该证明使用了t-模理论。本文旨在提供基于Mahler方法的新证明，这一方法由Denis在2006年针对Fq(theta)中元素的对数特殊情况发展而来

Method: 采用Mahler方法替代原有的t-模理论，重新审视并扩展Denis在2006年发展的方法。Denis的方法通过Mahler系统证明了Fq(theta)中元素对数的特殊情况，本文将其推广到更一般的代数函数Carlitz对数情形

Result: 成功为Papanikolas定理提供了基于Mahler方法的新证明，表明Carlitz对数在有理函数域上的线性独立性能推导出代数独立性，扩展了Denis在特殊情况下的结果

Conclusion: Mahler方法可以作为t-模理论的有效替代来证明Carlitz对数的代数独立性定理，这为相关领域提供了新的证明工具和视角，扩展了Mahler方法在函数域数论中的应用范围

Abstract: In 2007, Papanikolas established that if Carlitz logarithms of algebraic functions are linearly independent over the rational function field, then they are algebraically independent. The purpose of the present paper is to provide a new proof of this theorem using Mahler s method instead of the theory of t-motives. We revisit and extend the approach developed by Denis, which enabled him in 2006 to prove this result in the particular case of the logarithm of elements in Fq(theta) via a Mahler system.

</details>


### [24] [The non-abelian Leopoldt conjecture and equalities of $\mathcal{L}$-invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18961)
*Daniel Barrera Salazar,Andrew Graham,Chris Williams*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 在非阿贝尔Leopoldt猜想下，证明了Hansen的p-adic过收敛上同调特征簇在特定经典点处是étale的，并由此推导出Fontaine-Mazur和自守L-不变量在某些条件下的相等性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究p-adic过收敛上同调特征簇的局部性质，建立自守L-不变量与Fontaine-Mazur L-不变量之间的关系，验证Gehrmann猜想

Method: 使用Hansen-Thorne的论证方法，在非阿贝尔Leopoldt猜想下分析特征簇的étaleness性质；结合局部-全局兼容性结果，通过切向量假设推导L-不变量等式

Result: 证明了在NALC下，特征簇在非临界经典点处是étale的；当切向量假设满足时，推导出Fontaine-Mazur和自守L-不变量相等；验证了对称幂提升情形的所有假设

Conclusion: 非阿贝尔Leopoldt猜想蕴含Gehrmann猜想：自守L-不变量与上同调度无关；对称幂提升情形验证了所有假设，得到了自守L-不变量的函子性结果

Abstract: Let $G$ be a reductive group quasi-split at $p$. Using arguments of Hansen--Thorne, we show that under the non-abelian Leopoldt conjecture (NALC), Hansen's $p$-adic overconvergent cohomology eigenvariety for $G$ is étale over its image in weight space at any non-critical classical tempered cuspidal point of `cohomological multiplicity one'. This applies to all non-critical classical cuspidal points if $G = \mathrm{Res}_{F/\mathbb{Q}}\mathrm{GL}_n$.
  We then let $π$ be a $p$-ordinary regular algebraic cuspidal automorphic representation of $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{Q}})$ such that $π_p$ is Steinberg. Combining the above étaleness result for the classical point attached to $π$, and a local-global compatibility result from our earlier work, we deduce -- under a tangent vector hypothesis that is true for at least half the simple roots -- the equality of Fontaine--Mazur and automorphic $\mathcal{L}$-invariants for $π$. Where this assumption is satisfied, we deduce the NALC implies a conjecture of Gehrmann: that automorphic $\mathcal{L}$-invariants are independent of cohomological degree. Our approach is inspired by (and generalises) previous work of Gehrmann--Rosso.
  When $π= \operatorname{Sym}^{n-1} π_f$ is the symmetric power lift of a modular form, we verify all assumptions other than the NALC, and deduce a functoriality result for the automorphic $\mathcal{L}$-invariants.

</details>

*Qing Liu*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超椭圆曲线在算术几何中具有重要地位，其极小Weierstrass模型的研究对于理解曲线的约化行为和雅可比行列的性质至关重要。当存在多个极小Weierstrass模型时，这些模型之间的关系及其对正则极小模型和典范模型的影响尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 在离散赋值域上研究亏格g≥2的超椭圆曲线，分析其极小Weierstrass模型。当存在多个此类模型时，研究它们与正则极小模型和典范模型的关系。特别针对亏格2曲线，通过两个特定的极小Weierstrass模型来表征稳定约化的存在性，并计算雅可比行列的Euler因子和Néron模型的体积形式。

Result: 发现了当存在多个极小Weierstrass模型时，正则极小模型和典范模型的有趣性质。对于亏格2曲线，建立了稳定约化存在性与极小Weierstrass模型之间的关系。开发了通过两个特定极小Weierstrass模型计算雅可比行列Euler因子和Néron模型体积形式的方法。

Conclusion: 极小Weierstrass模型的研究为理解超椭圆曲线的算术性质提供了新视角，特别是在存在多个模型的情况下。对于亏格2曲线，该方法能够有效表征稳定约化并计算相关算术不变量，为超椭圆曲线的算术几何研究提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Let $C$ be a hyperelliptic curve of genus $g\ge 2$ over a discrete valuation field $K$ with perfect residue field. We study the minimal Weierstrass models of $C$. When there is more than one such model, we find interesting properties on the minimal regular model and the canonical model of $C$. For curves of genus $2$, we characterize the existence of the stable reduction in terms of the minimal Weierstrass models. When there is more than one such model, we can compute the Euler factor of $\mathrm{Jac}(C)$ and a volume form of the Néron model of $\mathrm{Jac}(C)$, using two specific minimal Weierstrass models.

</details>


### [26] [Multiplication Tables for Integers with Restricted Prime Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19212)
*Jeremy Schlitt*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究具有特定素数集合Q的整数分布，计数在[1,x]中所有素因子属于Q且包含(y,2y]区间内因子的整数数量，建立了对所有相对密度δ∈(0,1]的数量级估计，并发现临界点δ=1/log4处存在相变


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Ford在2008年对δ=1情况的研究，研究更一般的素数集合Q（具有相对密度δ）对整数分布的影响，探索素数分布特征如何影响整数的除数结构

Method: 使用解析数论方法，特别是素数分布理论和除数函数分析，通过建立计数函数的渐近公式来处理具有特定素数集合的整数分布问题

Result: 对所有δ∈(0,1]建立了计数函数的数量级估计，发现临界点δ=1/log4处存在相变现象，并在此临界点处明确确定了计数函数的渐近行为

Conclusion: 素数集合的相对密度δ对整数分布有重要影响，特别是在δ=1/log4处存在相变，这扩展了Ford的经典结果，为理解具有特定素数因子的整数分布提供了完整的理论框架

Abstract: Let $Q$ be a set of primes with relative density $δ$. We count integers in $[1,x]$ with prime factors all in $Q$ that also have a divisor in $(y,2y]$. We establish the order of magnitude for all $δ\in (0,1]$. This generalizes the case $δ= 1$ from the 2008 work of Ford. We also show that there is a phase transition at the critical point $δ= 1/\log 4$, for which we explicitly determine the behaviour.

</details>

### [27] [Final Report for the Workshop on Robotics & AI in Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18130)
*Juan P Wachs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CARE研讨会汇集多方利益相关者，旨在建立国家医疗AI与机器人卓越中心，以协调研究、解决数据、评估、监管和培训等关键障碍，推动智能机器人系统在医疗领域的转化应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗领域对协调性研究工作的需求日益增长，需要弥合工程创新与临床优先事项之间的鸿沟，特别是在安全性、可靠性和转化准备方面。当前存在数据可用性、标准化评估方法、监管路径和劳动力培训等关键障碍，阻碍了智能机器人系统在手术、诊断、康复和辅助等场景的部署。

Method: 通过主题演讲、小组讨论和分组会议的形式，汇集研究人员、临床医生、行业创新者和联邦利益相关者，共同讨论医疗机器人与AI的挑战与机遇，并形成建立国家卓越中心的共识。

Result: 研讨会达成了广泛共识，迫切需要建立国家医疗AI与机器人卓越中心（CARE）。确定了优先研究方向：人机协作、可信自主性、仿真与数字孪生、多模态感知以及生成式AI在临床工作流程中的伦理整合。同时明确了高质量数据集、共享测试平台、自主手术系统、临床基准和跨学科培训机制等需求。

Conclusion: AI赋能的机器人技术具有变革潜力，能够提高精度、减轻医疗负担、扩大专科护理可及性并改善患者预后，特别是在服务不足地区和高风险手术领域。建立CARE中心对于协调国家层面的研究、解决关键障碍、实现医疗机器人与AI的转化应用至关重要。

Abstract: The CARE Workshop on Robotics and AI in Medicine, held on December 1, 2025 in Indianapolis, convened leading researchers, clinicians, industry innovators, and federal stakeholders to shape a national vision for advancing robotics and artificial intelligence in healthcare. The event highlighted the accelerating need for coordinated research efforts that bridge engineering innovation with real clinical priorities, emphasizing safety, reliability, and translational readiness with an emphasis on the use of robotics and AI to achieve this readiness goal.
  Across keynotes, panels, and breakout sessions, participants underscored critical gaps in data availability, standardized evaluation methods, regulatory pathways, and workforce training that hinder the deployment of intelligent robotic systems in surgical, diagnostic, rehabilitative, and assistive contexts. Discussions emphasized the transformative potential of AI enabled robotics to improve precision, reduce provider burden, expand access to specialized care, and enhance patient outcomes particularly in undeserved regions and high risk procedural domains. Special attention was given to austere settings, disaster and relief and military settings.
  The workshop demonstrated broad consensus on the urgency of establishing a national Center for AI and Robotic Excellence in medicine (CARE). Stakeholders identified priority research thrusts including human robot collaboration, trustworthy autonomy, simulation and digital twins, multi modal sensing, and ethical integration of generative AI into clinical workflows. Participants also articulated the need for high quality datasets, shared test beds, autonomous surgical systems, clinically grounded benchmarks, and sustained interdisciplinary training mechanisms.

</details>


### [28] [GoalVLM: VLM-driven Object Goal Navigation for Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18210)
*MoniJesu James,Amir Atef Habel,Aleksey Fedoseev,Dzmitry Tsetserokou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GoalVLM是一个用于零样本开放词汇目标导航的多智能体框架，无需任务特定训练，通过视觉语言模型、文本提示检测分割和空间推理实现自由形式语言目标导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统目标导航方法局限于地面机器人和封闭词汇集，现有多智能体方法依赖预计算概率图且无法泛化到测试时的新目标。需要一种能够处理开放词汇、零样本目标导航的框架。

Method: 集成视觉语言模型到决策循环，使用SAM3进行文本提示检测分割，SpaceOM进行空间推理。每个智能体通过深度投影体素溅射构建BEV语义地图，Goal Projector通过校准深度反投影检测结果进行目标定位。约束引导推理层通过结构化提示链评估前沿区域。

Result: 在GOAT-Bench val_unseen数据集上（360个多子任务场景，1032个顺序目标子任务），使用2个智能体的GoalVLM达到55.8%子任务成功率和18.3%SPL，与最先进方法竞争且无需任务特定训练。消融研究确认了VLM引导前沿推理和深度投影目标定位的贡献。

Conclusion: GoalVLM展示了在零样本开放词汇目标导航中集成视觉语言模型的可行性，通过语义先验和空间推理实现多智能体协作导航，为无需任务特定训练的目标导航提供了新途径。

Abstract: Object-goal navigation has traditionally been limited to ground robots with closed-set object vocabularies. Existing multi-agent approaches depend on precomputed probabilistic graphs tied to fixed category sets, precluding generalization to novel goals at test time.
  We present GoalVLM, a cooperative multi-agent framework for zero-shot, open-vocabulary object navigation. GoalVLM integrates a Vision-Language Model (VLM) directly into the decision loop, SAM3 for text-prompted detection and segmentation, and SpaceOM for spatial reasoning, enabling agents to interpret free-form language goals and score frontiers via zero-shot semantic priors without retraining. Each agent builds a BEV semantic map from depth-projected voxel splatting, while a Goal Projector back-projects detections through calibrated depth into the map for reliable goal localization. A constraint-guided reasoning layer evaluates frontiers through a structured prompt chain (scene captioning, room-type classification, perception gating, multi-frontier ranking), injecting commonsense priors into exploration.
  We evaluate GoalVLM on GOAT-Bench val_unseen (360 multi-subtask episodes, 1032 sequential object-goal subtasks, HM3D scenes), where each episode requires navigating to a chain of 5-7 open-vocabulary targets. GoalVLM with N=2 agents achieves 55.8% subtask SR and 18.3% SPL, competitive with state-of-the-art methods while requiring no task-specific training. Ablation studies confirm the contributions of VLM-guided frontier reasoning and depth-projected goal localization.

</details>


### [29] [ReDAG-RT: Global Rate-Priority Scheduling for Real-Time Multi-DAG Execution in ROS 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18238)
*Md. Mehedi Hasan,Rafid Mostafiz,Bikash Kumar Paul,Md. Abir Hossain,Ziaur Rahman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReDAGRT：一个用于ROS 2的用户空间全局调度框架，通过速率优先级驱动的全局就绪队列实现确定性多DAG执行，解决回调争用、优先级反转和截止期不稳定问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 默认ROS 2执行器采用尽力而为调度，缺乏跨DAG优先级强制执行，导致回调争用、结构性优先级反转和并发工作负载下的截止期不稳定，限制了在时间关键和安全关键系统中的部署。

Method: 提出ReDAGRT框架，引入速率优先级驱动的全局就绪队列，按激活速率排序回调，强制执行每DAG并发边界，缓解跨图优先级反转，无需修改ROS 2 API、执行器接口或底层操作系统调度器。形式化ROS 2回调管道的多DAG任务模型，并在经典速率单调理论下分析跨DAG干扰。

Result: 在ROS 2 Humble环境中实验表明，相比SingleThreadedExecutor和MultiThreadedExecutor，ReDAGRT将截止期错过率降低29.7%，99百分位响应时间减少42.9%，在可比利用率下比MultiThreadedExecutor提升13.7%。非对称每DAG并发边界进一步减少40.8%的干扰。

Conclusion: 确定性可分析的多DAG调度可以在ROS 2用户空间执行层完全实现，为安全关键系统中的实时机器人中间件提供了实用基础。

Abstract: ROS 2 has become a dominant middleware for robotic systems, where perception, estimation, planning, and control pipelines are structured as directed acyclic graphs of callbacks executed under a shared executor. However, default ROS 2 executors use best-effort dispatch without cross-DAG priority enforcement, leading to callback contention, structural priority inversion, and deadline instability under concurrent workloads. These limitations restrict deployment in time-critical and safety-sensitive cyber-physical systems. This paper presents ReDAGRT, a user-space global scheduling framework for deterministic multi-DAG execution in unmodified ROS 2. The framework introduces a Rate-Priority driven global ready queue that orders callbacks by activation rate, enforces per-DAG concurrency bounds, and mitigates cross-graph priority inversion without modifying the ROS 2 API, executor interface, or underlying operating system scheduler. We formalize a multi-DAG task model for ROS 2 callback pipelines and analyze cross-DAG interference under Rate-Priority scheduling. Response-time recurrences and schedulability conditions are derived within classical Rate-Monotonic theory. Experiments in a ROS 2 Humble environment compare ReDAGRT against SingleThreadedExecutor and MultiThreadedExecutor using synthetic multi-DAG workloads. Results show up to 29.7 percent reduction in deadline miss rate, 42.9 percent reduction in 99th percentile response time, and 13.7 percent improvement over MultiThreadedExecutor under comparable utilization. Asymmetric per-DAG concurrency bounds further reduce interference by 40.8 percent. These results demonstrate that deterministic and analyzable multi-DAG scheduling can be achieved entirely in the ROS 2 user-space execution layer, providing a practical foundation for real-time robotic middleware in safety-critical systems.

</details>


### [30] [Rapid Adaptation of Particle Dynamics for Generalized Deformable Object Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18246)
*Bohan Wu,Roberto Martín-Martín,Li Fei-Fei*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RAPiD：一种将快速运动适应（RMA）扩展到可变形物体操作的两阶段方法，通过利用模拟中的粒子位置信息学习动态嵌入，实现真实世界中的可变形物体移动操作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决学习操作未知动态可变形物体的挑战。在可变形物体中，动态参数不仅包括质量和位置，还包括物体形状变化，这对确定成功执行操作任务的最优动作至关重要。现有RMA方法主要处理刚性物体，无法直接应用于可变形物体操作。

Method: RAPiD采用两阶段方法：1）在模拟中学习基于物体动态嵌入的视觉运动策略，动态嵌入从特权信息（如质量、地面真实粒子位置）编码；2）学习使用非特权信息（如机器人视觉观测和动作）推断该嵌入，使学习策略能够迁移到真实世界。

Result: 在具有22自由度的移动机械手上，RAPiD在真实世界中两个基于视觉的可变形物体移动操作任务上实现了超过80%的成功率，适用于各种物体动态、类别和实例。

Conclusion: 通过利用模拟中可变形物体的地面真实粒子位置信息，成功将RMA扩展到可变形物体操作领域，开发出能够处理未知动态并在真实世界中实现高成功率操作的方法。

Abstract: We address the challenge of learning to manipulate deformable objects with unknown dynamics. In non-rigid objects, the dynamics parameters define how they react to interactions -- how they stretch, bend, compress, and move -- and they are critical to determining the optimal actions to perform a manipulation task successfully. In other robotic domains, such as legged locomotion and in-hand rigid object manipulation, state-of-the-art approaches can handle unknown dynamics using Rapid Motor Adaptation (RMA). Through a supervised procedure in simulation that encodes each rigid object's dynamics, such as mass and position, these approaches learn a policy that conditions actions on a vector of latent dynamic parameters inferred from sequences of state-actions. However, in deformable object manipulation, the object's dynamics not only includes its mass and position, but also how the shape of the object changes. Our key insight is that the recent ground-truth particle positions of a deformable object in simulation capture changes in the object's shape, making it possible to extend RMA to deformable object manipulation. This key insight allows us to develop RAPiD, a two-phase method that learns to perform real-robot deformable object mobile manipulation by: 1) learning a visuomotor policy conditioned on the object's dynamics embedding, which is encoded from the object's privileged information in simulation, such as its mass and ground-truth particle positions, and 2) learning to infer this embedding using non-privileged information instead, such as robot visual observations and actions, so that the learned policy can transfer to the real world. On a mobile manipulator with 22 degrees of freedom, RAPiD enables over 80%+ success rates across two vision-based deformable object mobile manipulation tasks in the real world, under various object dynamics, categories, and instances.

</details>


### [31] [Manufacturing Micro-Patterned Surfaces with Multi-Robot Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18260)
*Annalisa T. Taylor,Malachi Landis,Ping Guo,Todd D. Murphey*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 多机器人系统使用遍历控制算法协调制造微图案表面，通过压缩轨迹历史通信实现复杂覆盖目标，降低金属表面摩擦系数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前制造技术无法大规模生产微图案表面，因为高成本设备和低效的覆盖技术（如光栅扫描）限制了规模化应用

Method: 使用配备图案化工具的多机器人系统，采用遍历控制算法协调覆盖任务，机器人通过压缩轨迹历史表示进行通信以分配复杂覆盖目标

Result: 在仿真和实验中都证明机器人能够通过通信协调完成复杂覆盖任务，机器人制造的图案化表面能够降低金属表面的摩擦系数

Conclusion: 分布式多机器人系统能够协调制造以前无法大规模生产的产品，为微图案表面的大规模制造提供了可行方案

Abstract: Applying micro-patterns to surfaces has been shown to impart useful physical properties such as drag reduction and hydrophobicity. However, current manufacturing techniques cannot produce micro-patterned surfaces at scale due to high-cost machinery and inefficient coverage techniques such as raster-scanning. In this work, we use multiple robots, each equipped with a patterning tool, to manufacture these surfaces. To allow these robots to coordinate during the patterning task, we use the ergodic control algorithm, which specifies coverage objectives using distributions. We demonstrate that robots can divide complicated coverage objectives by communicating compressed representations of their trajectory history both in simulations and experimental trials. Further, we show that robot-produced patterning can lower the coefficient of friction of metallic surfaces. This work demonstrates that distributed multi-robot systems can coordinate to manufacture products that were previously unrealizable at scale.

</details>


### [32] [SG-CoT: An Ambiguity-Aware Robotic Planning Framework using Scene Graph Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18271)
*Akshat Rana,Peeyush Agarwal,K. P. S. Rana,Amarjit Malhotra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SG-CoT是一个两阶段框架，通过让LLM迭代查询场景图来检测和澄清机器人规划中的歧义，显著提升规划可靠性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 歧义性是大型语言模型作为机器人规划器面临的主要挑战，需要一种方法来检测和澄清环境中的歧义，以提高规划可靠性

Method: 提出Scene Graph-Chain-of-Thought (SG-CoT)两阶段框架：1) 从输入观察构建结构化场景图表示；2) LLM配备检索函数查询与指令相关的场景图部分，并识别歧义源向用户或其他机器人提出澄清问题

Result: SG-CoT始终优于先前方法，问题准确率至少提升10%，单智能体环境成功率至少提升4%，多智能体环境成功率至少提升15%

Conclusion: SG-CoT通过将LLM推理过程基于观察的场景图表示，有效检测和澄清歧义，显著提高了机器人规划在模糊情况下的可靠性和泛化能力

Abstract: Ambiguity poses a major challenge to large language models (LLMs) used as robotic planners. In this letter, we present Scene Graph-Chain-of-Thought (SG-CoT), a two-stage framework where LLMs iteratively query a scene graph representation of the environment to detect and clarify ambiguities. First, a structured scene graph representation of the environment is constructed from input observations, capturing objects, their attributes, and relationships with other objects. Second, the LLM is equipped with retrieval functions to query portions of the scene graph that are relevant to the provided instruction. This grounds the reasoning process of the LLM in the observation, increasing the reliability of robotic planners under ambiguous situations. SG-CoT also allows the LLM to identify the source of ambiguity and pose a relevant disambiguation question to the user or another robot. Extensive experimentation demonstrates that SG-CoT consistently outperforms prior methods, with a minimum of 10% improvement in question accuracy and a minimum success rate increase of 4% in single-agent and 15% in multi-agent environments, validating its effectiveness for more generalizable robot planning.

</details>


### [33] [Sparse3DTrack: Monocular 3D Object Tracking Using Sparse Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18298)
*Nikhil Gosala,B. Ravi Kiran,Senthil Yogamani,Abhinav Valada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个稀疏监督的单目3D目标跟踪框架，通过2D查询匹配和3D几何估计两个子问题，利用时空一致性从稀疏标注生成高质量伪标签，显著提升跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的单目3D目标跟踪方法完全依赖密集的3D标注，这些标注获取成本高且难以扩展。为了解决这一根本限制，需要开发能够在稀疏标注下有效工作的框架。

Method: 将任务分解为两个顺序子问题：2D查询匹配和3D几何估计。两个组件都利用图像序列的时空一致性来增强稀疏标注样本，学习丰富的2D和3D场景表示。基于这些学习到的线索，模型自动生成整个视频的高质量3D伪标签，将稀疏监督转化为密集的3D轨迹标注。

Result: 在KITTI和nuScenes数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法显著提高了跟踪性能，在每条轨迹最多使用四个真实标注的情况下，性能提升高达15.50个百分点。

Conclusion: 提出的稀疏监督框架成功解决了单目3D目标跟踪中标注成本高的问题，通过将稀疏监督转化为密集伪标签，使现有完全监督跟踪器能够在极端标签稀疏性下有效工作。

Abstract: Monocular 3D object tracking aims to estimate temporally consistent 3D object poses across video frames, enabling autonomous agents to reason about scene dynamics. However, existing state-of-the-art approaches are fully supervised and rely on dense 3D annotations over long video sequences, which are expensive to obtain and difficult to scale. In this work, we address this fundamental limitation by proposing the first sparsely supervised framework for monocular 3D object tracking. Our approach decomposes the task into two sequential sub-problems: 2D query matching and 3D geometry estimation. Both components leverage the spatio-temporal consistency of image sequences to augment a sparse set of labeled samples and learn rich 2D and 3D representations of the scene. Leveraging these learned cues, our model automatically generates high-quality 3D pseudolabels across entire videos, effectively transforming sparse supervision into dense 3D track annotations. This enables existing fully-supervised trackers to effectively operate under extreme label sparsity. Extensive experiments on the KITTI and nuScenes datasets demonstrate that our method significantly improves tracking performance, achieving an improvement of up to 15.50 p.p. while using at most four ground truth annotations per track.

</details>


### [34] [Proprioceptive-only State Estimation for Legged Robots with Set-Coverage Measurements of Learned Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18308)
*Abhijeet M. Kulkarni,Ioannis Poulakakis,Guoquan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于腿式机器人的纯本体感知状态估计框架，使用集合覆盖而非高斯分布来表征测量噪声，提高在真实噪声场景下的鲁棒性和一致性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯本体感知状态估计对腿式机器人具有吸引力，因为它计算成本低且不受感知退化条件影响。现有方法通常假设高斯噪声，但在训练数据有限时这一假设容易失效，导致估计不一致和潜在发散。

Method: 提出一个纯本体感知状态估计框架，使用集合覆盖语句而非分布假设来表征测量噪声。开发了一种实用且计算成本低的方法，将这些集合覆盖测量与高斯滤波器系统性地结合使用。

Result: 在仿真和两个真实世界四足机器人数据集上验证了该方法。与高斯基线相比，所提方法在真实噪声场景下保持一致性且不易漂移。

Conclusion: 提出的基于集合覆盖的测量噪声表征方法能够有效处理真实噪声场景，相比传统高斯假设方法具有更好的鲁棒性和一致性，为腿式机器人的纯本体感知状态估计提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Proprioceptive-only state estimation is attractive for legged robots since it is computationally cheaper and is unaffected by perceptually degraded conditions. The history of joint-level measurements contains rich information that can be used to infer the dynamics of the system and subsequently produce navigational measurements. Recent approaches produce these estimates with learned measurement models and fuse with IMU data, under a Gaussian noise assumption. However, this assumption can easily break down with limited training data and render the estimates inconsistent and potentially divergent. In this work, we propose a proprioceptive-only state estimation framework for legged robots that characterizes the measurement noise using set-coverage statements that do not assume any distribution. We develop a practical and computationally inexpensive method to use these set-coverage measurements with a Gaussian filter in a systematic way. We validate the approach in both simulation and two real-world quadrupedal datasets. Comparison with the Gaussian baselines shows that our proposed method remains consistent and is not prone to drift under real noise scenarios.

</details>


### [35] [DriveVLM-RL: Neuroscience-Inspired Reinforcement Learning with Vision-Language Models for Safe and Deployable Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18315)
*Zilin Huang,Zihao Sheng,Zhengyang Wan,Yansong Qu,Junwei You,Sicong Jiang,Sikai Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DriveVLM-RL：一种神经科学启发的双通路架构，将视觉语言模型集成到强化学习中，用于安全可部署的自动驾驶，通过离线训练和在线部署分离确保实时性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习方法依赖手工设计的奖励或稀疏碰撞信号，无法捕捉安全驾驶所需的丰富上下文理解，且在真实世界环境中不可避免地进行不安全探索。视觉语言模型具有语义理解能力，但高推理延迟和幻觉问题阻碍了其在实时车辆控制中的直接应用。

Method: 提出DriveVLM-RL框架，采用双通路架构：静态通路使用CLIP对比语言目标进行连续空间安全评估；动态通路使用轻量级检测器和大型VLM进行注意力门控多帧语义风险推理。通过分层奖励合成机制融合语义信号与车辆状态，异步训练管道解耦昂贵的VLM推理与环境交互。所有VLM组件仅在离线训练中使用，部署时移除以确保实时可行性。

Result: 在CARLA模拟器中的实验显示，在碰撞避免、任务成功率和多样化交通场景泛化方面均有显著改进，包括在没有显式碰撞惩罚的设置下表现出强大的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DriveVLM-RL为将基础模型集成到自动驾驶中提供了一个实用范式，同时不损害实时可行性，展示了神经科学启发的双通路架构在安全决策中的有效性。

Abstract: Ensuring safe decision-making in autonomous vehicles remains a fundamental challenge despite rapid advances in end-to-end learning approaches. Traditional reinforcement learning (RL) methods rely on manually engineered rewards or sparse collision signals, which fail to capture the rich contextual understanding required for safe driving and make unsafe exploration unavoidable in real-world settings. Recent vision-language models (VLMs) offer promising semantic understanding capabilities; however, their high inference latency and susceptibility to hallucination hinder direct application to real-time vehicle control. To address these limitations, this paper proposes DriveVLM-RL, a neuroscience-inspired framework that integrates VLMs into RL through a dual-pathway architecture for safe and deployable autonomous driving. The framework decomposes semantic reward learning into a Static Pathway for continuous spatial safety assessment using CLIP-based contrasting language goals, and a Dynamic Pathway for attention-gated multi-frame semantic risk reasoning using a lightweight detector and a large VLM. A hierarchical reward synthesis mechanism fuses semantic signals with vehicle states, while an asynchronous training pipeline decouples expensive VLM inference from environment interaction. All VLM components are used only during offline training and are removed at deployment, ensuring real-time feasibility. Experiments in the CARLA simulator show significant improvements in collision avoidance, task success, and generalization across diverse traffic scenarios, including strong robustness under settings without explicit collision penalties. These results demonstrate that DriveVLM-RL provides a practical paradigm for integrating foundation models into autonomous driving without compromising real-time feasibility. Demo video and code are available at: https://zilin-huang.github.io/DriveVLM-RL-website/

</details>


### [36] [ManiDreams: An Open-Source Library for Robust Object Manipulation via Uncertainty-aware Task-specific Intuitive Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18336)
*Gaotian Wang,Kejia Ren,Andrew S. Morgan,Kaiyu Hang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ManiDreams是一个用于不确定性感知操作规划的模块化框架，通过可组合的抽象实现分布状态表示、后端无关的动力学预测和声明式约束规范，在规划循环中集成不确定性而非仅抑制预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界操作本质上是存在不确定性的，而现有的动力学模型主要关注最小化预测误差，未能解决更根本的挑战。鲁棒操作在不确定性下本质上是一个集成问题：需要在规划循环中表示、传播和约束不确定性，而不仅仅是在训练中抑制。

Method: 提出ManiDreams框架，通过三个可组合抽象实现：分布状态表示、后端无关的动力学预测、声明式约束规范的动作优化。框架明确处理三种不确定性来源：感知、参数和结构不确定性。采用样本-预测-约束循环包装任何基础策略，评估候选动作的分布结果，无需重新训练即可增加鲁棒性。

Result: 在ManiSkill任务上的实验表明，ManiDreams在各种扰动下保持鲁棒性能，而RL基线性能显著下降。在推动、抓取、捕捉和真实世界部署的可运行示例展示了框架在不同策略、优化器、物理后端和执行器上的灵活性。

Conclusion: ManiDreams提供了一个模块化框架，将不确定性集成到操作规划循环中，通过分布表示和约束优化实现鲁棒性，无需重新训练基础策略，在模拟和真实世界任务中均表现出色。

Abstract: Dynamics models, whether simulators or learned world models, have long been central to robotic manipulation, but most focus on minimizing prediction error rather than confronting a more fundamental challenge: real-world manipulation is inherently uncertain. We argue that robust manipulation under uncertainty is fundamentally an integration problem: uncertainties must be represented, propagated, and constrained within the planning loop, not merely suppressed during training. We present and open-source ManiDreams, a modular framework for uncertainty-aware manipulation planning over intuitive physics models. It realizes this integration through composable abstractions for distributional state representation, backend-agnostic dynamics prediction, and declarative constraint specification for action optimization. The framework explicitly addresses three sources of uncertainty: perceptual, parametric, and structural. It wraps any base policy with a sample-predict-constrain loop that evaluates candidate actions against distributional outcomes, adding robustness without retraining. Experiments on ManiSkill tasks show that ManiDreams maintains robust performance under various perturbations where the RL baseline degrades significantly. Runnable examples on pushing, picking, catching, and real-world deployment demonstrate flexibility across different policies, optimizers, physics backends, and executors. The framework is publicly available at https://github.com/Rice-RobotPI-Lab/ManiDreams

</details>


### [37] [Shifting Uncertainty to Critical Moments: Towards Reliable Uncertainty Quantification for VLA Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18342)
*Yanchuan Tang,Taowen Wang,Yuefei Chen,Boxuan Zhang,Qiang Guan,Ruixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于VLA机器人策略的改进不确定性量化方法，通过最大滑动窗口池化、运动感知稳定性加权和自由度自适应校准来更准确地预测任务失败


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型缺乏可靠的内省能力，传统基于token级不确定性均值的方法会稀释安全关键但短暂的不确定性峰值，导致无法准确预测任务失败

Method: 1) 使用基于最大值的滑动窗口池化保留瞬态风险信号；2) 应用运动感知稳定性加权强调与不稳定行为相关的高频动作振荡；3) 通过贝叶斯优化进行自由度自适应校准，优先考虑运动学关键轴

Result: 在LIBERO基准测试中，该方法显著提高了失败预测准确率，并产生更可靠的故障检测信号，可支持下游人机交互干预

Conclusion: 提出的统一不确定性量化方法能有效捕捉VLA策略中的瞬态风险信号，改善失败预测，为安全关键机器人应用提供更可靠的内省能力

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable general-purpose robotic policies by mapping visual observations and language instructions to low-level actions, but they often lack reliable introspection. A common practice is to compute a token-level uncertainty signal and take its mean over a rollout. However, mean aggregation can dilute short-lived but safety-critical uncertainty spikes in continuous control. In particular, successful rollouts may contain localized high-entropy segments due to benign noise or non-critical micro-adjustments, while failure rollouts can appear low-entropy for most timesteps and only exhibit brief spikes near the onset of failure. We propose a unified uncertainty quantification approach for predicting rollout success versus failure that (1) uses max-based sliding window pooling to preserve transient risk signals, (2) applies motion-aware stability weighting to emphasize high-frequency action oscillations associated with unstable behaviors, and (3) performs DoF-adaptive calibration via Bayesian Optimization to prioritize kinematically critical axes. Experiments on the LIBERO benchmark show that our method substantially improves failure prediction accuracy and yields more reliable signals for failure detection, which can support downstream human-in-the-loop interventions.

</details>


### [38] [HRI-SA: A Multimodal Dataset for Online Assessment of Human Situational Awareness during Remote Human-Robot Teaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18344)
*Hashini Senaratne,Richard Attfield,Samith Widhanapathirana,David Howard,Cecile Paris,Dana Kulic,Leimin Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HRI-SA数据集，这是首个支持人机团队协作中情境感知（SA）在线评估的公开多模态数据集，包含眼动、生理信号等数据，并展示了使用眼动特征检测感知SA延迟的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高工作负荷和动态环境下，操作员常出现情境感知（SA）缺失，而现有SA测量方法要么干扰任务流程，要么无法捕捉实时波动，缺乏支持在线SA评估的公开数据集。需要开发能够实时检测SA缺失的工具来及时提供操作员协助。

Method: 构建了HRI-SA多模态数据集，包含30名参与者在搜索救援人机协作场景中的眼动、瞳孔直径、生物信号、用户交互和机器人数据。实验协议包含预定义的需要操作员及时协助的事件，通过测量协助需求出现到解决的时间差，系统地获取了两种类型（感知和理解）的SA延迟真实值。

Result: 使用通用眼动特征和上下文特征评估标准机器学习模型检测感知SA延迟的效果。结果显示，仅使用眼动特征就能有效分类感知SA延迟（召回率=88.91%，F1=67.63%），通过上下文数据融合可进一步提升性能（召回率=91.51%，F1=80.38%）。

Conclusion: 本文贡献了首个支持人机团队任务中SA系统评估的公开数据集，同时证明了通用眼动特征在远程人机协作中连续检测感知SA延迟的潜力，为开发在线SA评估工具提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Maintaining situational awareness (SA) is critical in human-robot teams. Yet, under high workload and dynamic conditions, operators often experience SA gaps. Automated detection of SA gaps could provide timely assistance for operators. However, conventional SA measures either disrupt task flow or cannot capture real-time fluctuations, limiting their operational utility. To the best of our knowledge, no publicly available dataset currently supports the systematic evaluation of online human SA assessment in human-robot teaming. To advance the development of online SA assessment tools, we introduce HRI-SA, a multimodal dataset from 30 participants in a realistic search-and-rescue human-robot teaming context, incorporating eye movements, pupil diameter, biosignals, user interactions, and robot data. The experimental protocol included predefined events requiring timely operator assistance, with ground truth SA latency of two types (perceptual and comprehension) systematically obtained by measuring the time between assistance need onset and resolution. We illustrate the utility of this dataset by evaluating standard machine learning models for detecting perceptual SA latencies using generic eye-tracking features and contextual features. Results show that eye-tracking features alone effectively classified perceptual SA latency (recall=88.91%, F1=67.63%) using leave-one-group-out cross-validation, with performance improved through contextual data fusion (recall=91.51%, F1=80.38%). This paper contributes the first public dataset supporting the systematic evaluation of SA throughout a human-robot teaming mission, while also demonstrating the potential of generic eye-tracking features for continuous perceptual SA latency detection in remote human-robot teaming.

</details>


### [39] [Multi-material Direct Ink Writing and Embroidery for Stretchable Wearable Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18354)
*Lukas Cha,Ryman Hashem,Ria Prakash,Tanguy Declety,Wenze Zhang,Liang He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 开发了一种将多材料直写打印与自动刺绣相结合的工作流程，用于制造直接嵌入服装的可拉伸应变传感器，实现了机械固定和电气接口的一体化集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动表现追踪、康复和损伤预防等可穿戴传感系统的需求日益增长，推动了智能服装的发展，需要结合舒适性、耐用性和精确运动检测的解决方案。

Method: 采用纺织兼容的制造工作流程，结合多材料直写打印（硅胶-碳脂-硅胶堆叠）和自动刺绣技术，在单一步骤中实现传感器的机械固定和电气接口。

Result: 混合传感器拉伸性达120%，60%应变内呈近似线性响应（R²=0.99），应变系数31.4，滞后22.9%。80次循环测试显示基线和峰值漂移分别为0.135%和0.236%/循环。机械测试表明硅胶-织物界面在大变形下保持完整。集成到肘部和膝部护套中可监测关节角度。

Conclusion: 通过刺绣集成同时解决机械固定和电气接口问题，为将印刷可拉伸电子器件集成到纺织系统中提供了可重复且可扩展的途径，适用于运动捕捉和软机器人应用。

Abstract: The development of wearable sensing systems for sports performance tracking, rehabilitation, and injury prevention has driven growing demand for smart garments that combine comfort, durability, and accurate motion detection. This paper presents a textile-compatible fabrication workflow that integrates multi-material direct ink writing with automated embroidery to create stretchable strain sensors directly embedded into garments. The process combines sequential multi-material printing of a silicone-carbon grease-silicone stack with automated embroidery that provides both mechanical fixation and electrical interfacing in a single step. The resulting hybrid sensor demonstrates stretchability up to 120% strain while maintaining electrical continuity, with approximately linear behaviour up to 60% strain (R^2 = 0.99), a gauge factor of 31.4, and hysteresis of 22.9%. Repeated loading-unloading tests over 80 cycles show baseline and peak drift of 0.135% and 0.236% per cycle, respectively, indicating moderate cycle-to-cycle stability. Mechanical testing further confirms that the silicone-fabric interface remains intact under large deformation, with failure occurring in the textile rather than at the stitched boundary. As a preliminary proof of concept, the sensor was integrated into wearable elbow and knee sleeves for joint angle monitoring, showing a clear correlation between normalised resistance change and bending angle. By addressing both mechanical fixation and electrical interfacing through embroidery-based integration, this approach provides a reproducible and scalable pathway for incorporating printed stretchable electronics into textile systems for motion capture and soft robotic applications.

</details>


### [40] [Contact Status Recognition and Slip Detection with a Bio-inspired Tactile Hand](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18370)
*Chengxiao He,Wenhui Yang,Hongliang Zhao,Jiacheng Lv,Yuzhe Shao,Longhui Qin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于多模态触觉反馈的机器人手滑移检测方法，通过分布式触觉传感器和接触状态识别实现高精度滑移检测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中稳定可靠的抓取至关重要，特别是对于易碎和光滑物体。现有方法通常假设物体已被牢固抓取，但在非结构化环境中，滑移检测和及时预防是必要的。传统阈值方法存在局限性，需要更智能的滑移检测方法。

Method: 使用五指仿生手的多模态触觉反馈，将24个触觉传感器分布式嵌入软皮肤中。将滑移检测问题转化为接触状态识别，结合分箱技术。对24通道触觉信号进行离散小波变换，从不同时间和频带提取17个特征。使用最优的120个特征进行状态识别，基于识别结果检测滑移起始时间。

Result: 在三种不同滑动速度和六种材料下，测试准确率达到96.39%。应用于四种未见新材料时，仍获得91.95%的高准确率，验证了方法的泛化能力。基于训练好的接触状态识别模型，滑移检测性能得到验证。

Conclusion: 提出的基于多模态触觉反馈和接触状态识别的方法能够有效检测滑移，具有高准确率和良好的泛化能力，为机器人操作中的滑移预防提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Stable and reliable grasp is critical to robotic manipulations especially for fragile and glazed objects, where the grasp force requires precise control as too large force possibly damages the objects while small force leads to slip and fall-off. Although it is assumed the objects to manipulate is grasped firmly in advance, slip detection and timely prevention are necessary for a robot in unstructured and universal environments. In this work, we addressed this issue by utilizing multimodal tactile feedback from a five-fingered bio-inspired hand. Motivated by human hands, the tactile sensing elements were distributed and embedded into the soft skin of robotic hand, forming 24 tactile channels in total. Different from the threshold method that was widely employed in most existing works, we converted the slip detection problem to contact status recognition in combination with binning technique first and then detected the slip onset time according to the recognition results. After the 24-channel tactile signals passed through discrete wavelet transform, 17 features were extracted from different time and frequency bands. With the optimal 120 features employed for status recognition, the test accuracy reached 96.39% across three different sliding speeds and six kinds of materials. When applied to four new unseen materials, a high accuracy of 91.95% was still achieved, which further validated the generalization of our proposed method. Finally, the performance of slip detection is verified based on the trained model of contact status recognition.

</details>


### [41] [Graph-of-Constraints Model Predictive Control for Reactive Multi-agent Task and Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18400)
*Anastasios Manganaris,Jeremy Lu,Ahmed H. Qureshi,Suresh Jagannathan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GoC-MPC是一种用于多智能体任务与运动规划的图约束模型预测控制框架，支持部分有序任务、动态智能体协调和扰动恢复，仅通过视觉观测实现鲁棒的多智能体操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有处理相互依赖几何约束序列的方法难以应对部分有序任务和动态智能体分配，通常假设静态分配且无法在扰动改变任务分配时自适应调整。

Method: 提出图约束模型预测控制（GoC-MPC），将广义约束序列框架与MPC集成，通过跟踪3D关键点定义约束，仅基于视觉观测实现鲁棒的多智能体操作。

Result: 实验表明GoC-MPC相比现有基线方法获得更高的成功率、显著更快的TAMP计算速度和更短的整体路径。

Conclusion: GoC-MPC为现实世界扰动下的多智能体操作提供了高效鲁棒的解决方案，支持部分有序任务、动态协调和在线自适应。

Abstract: Sequences of interdependent geometric constraints are central to many multi-agent Task and Motion Planning (TAMP) problems. However, existing methods for handling such constraint sequences struggle with partially ordered tasks and dynamic agent assignments. They typically assume static assignments and cannot adapt when disturbances alter task allocations. To overcome these limitations, we introduce Graph-of-Constraints Model Predictive Control (GoC-MPC), a generalized sequence-of-constraints framework integrated with MPC. GoC-MPC naturally supports partially ordered tasks, dynamic agent coordination, and disturbance recovery. By defining constraints over tracked 3D keypoints, our method robustly solves diverse multi-agent manipulation tasks-coordinating agents and adapting online from visual observations alone, without relying on training data or environment models. Experiments demonstrate that GoC-MPC achieves higher success rates, significantly faster TAMP computation, and shorter overall paths compared to recent baselines, establishing it as an efficient and robust solution for multi-agent manipulation under real-world disturbances. Our supplementary video and code can be found at https://sites.google.com/view/goc-mpc/home .

</details>


### [42] [Efficient and Versatile Quadrupedal Skating: Optimal Co-design via Reinforcement Learning and Bayesian Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18408)
*Hanwen Wang,Zhenlong Fang,Josiah Hanna,Xiaobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种硬件-控制协同设计方法，使配备被动轮的四足机器人能够高效、多用途地轮滑。通过双层优化框架（上层贝叶斯优化搜索机械设计空间，下层强化学习训练控制策略）实现优于人工设计的性能，并展现出曲棍球式急停、自对齐运动等复杂行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动轮轮滑能减少腿部惯量、提高高速运动时的能量效率，但缺乏直接轮驱动使得机械设计与控制紧密耦合。为充分发挥这种运动模式的潜力，需要系统性地解决硬件与控制协同优化问题。

Method: 采用双层优化框架：上层使用贝叶斯优化搜索机械设计参数空间，下层对每个候选设计使用强化学习训练电机控制策略。这种协同优化方法同时优化硬件设计和控制策略。

Result: 优化得到的设计-策略组合不仅超越了人工设计的基准，还展现出多种复杂行为：曲棍球式急停（通过侧向转弯最大化摩擦力实现快速制动）和自对齐运动（自动调整方向以提高行进方向的能量效率）。

Conclusion: 该研究首次系统性地研究了四足机器人的动态轮滑运动，提出的硬件-控制协同设计方法成功解锁了被动轮轮滑的潜力，为四足机器人高效、多用途运动提供了新方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a hardware-control co-design approach that enables efficient and versatile roller skating on quadrupedal robots equipped with passive wheels. Passive-wheel skating reduces leg inertia and improves energy efficiency, particularly at high speeds. However, the absence of direct wheel actuation tightly couples mechanical design and control. To unlock the full potential of this modality, we formulate a bilevel optimization framework: an upper-level Bayesian Optimization searches the mechanical design space, while a lower-level Reinforcement Learning trains a motor control policy for each candidate design. The resulting design-policy pairs not only outperform human-engineered baselines, but also exhibit versatile behaviors such as hockey stop (rapid braking by turning sideways to maximize friction) and self-aligning motion (automatic reorientation to improve energy efficiency in the direction of travel), offering the first system-level study of dynamic skating motion on quadrupedal robots.

</details>


### [43] [MemoAct: Atkinson-Shiffrin-Inspired Memory-Augmented Visuomotor Policy for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18494)
*Liufan Tan,Jiale Li,Gangshan Jing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MemoAct：受Atkinson-Shiffrin记忆模型启发的分层记忆策略，通过无损短期记忆实现精确任务状态跟踪，压缩长期记忆支持鲁棒长时程记忆，在MemoryRTBench基准上优于现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于记忆的机器人策略通常依赖简单的观察窗口扩展，难以同时实现精确的任务状态跟踪和鲁棒的长时程记忆保留。需要一种更有效的记忆机制来解决这一瓶颈。

Method: 受Atkinson-Shiffrin记忆模型启发，提出MemoAct分层记忆策略：1）无损短期记忆确保精确任务状态跟踪；2）压缩长期记忆实现鲁棒长时程保留。构建MemoryRTBench基准（基于RoboTwin 2.0）专门评估策略的任务状态跟踪和长时程记忆能力。

Result: 在模拟和真实场景的大量实验中，MemoAct相比现有的马尔可夫基线和历史感知策略表现出更优越的性能，特别是在任务状态跟踪和长时程记忆保留方面。

Conclusion: MemoAct通过分层记忆架构有效解决了记忆依赖任务中的状态跟踪和长时程记忆问题，为机器人记忆策略提供了新的解决方案，在构建的MemoryRTBench基准上验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Memory-augmented robotic policies are essential in handling memory-dependent tasks. However, existing approaches typically rely on simple observation window extensions, struggling to simultaneously achieve precise task state tracking and robust long-horizon retention. To overcome these challenges, inspired by the Atkinson-Shiffrin memory model, we propose MemoAct, a hierarchical memory-based policy that leverages distinct memory tiers to tackle specific bottlenecks. Specifically, lossless short-term memory ensures precise task state tracking, while compressed long-term memory enables robust long-horizon retention. To enrich the evaluation landscape, we construct MemoryRTBench based on RoboTwin 2.0, specifically tailored to assess policy capabilities in task state tracking and long-horizon retention. Extensive experiments across simulated and real-world scenarios demonstrate that MemoAct achieves superior performance compared to both existing Markovian baselines and history-aware policies. The project page is \href{https://tlf-tlf.github.io/MemoActPage/}{available}.

</details>


### [44] [Scaling Sim-to-Real Reinforcement Learning for Robot VLAs with Generative 3D Worlds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18532)
*Andrew Choi,Xinjie Wang,Zhizhong Su,Wei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 利用3D世界生成模型和语言驱动场景设计，通过强化学习微调视觉语言动作模型，在保持泛化能力的同时提升性能，实现成功的仿真到真实世界迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在真实世界直接微调视觉语言动作模型会导致过拟合和场景特异性，而仿真训练虽然能提供多样场景但设计成本高昂。需要一种既能保持模型泛化能力又能高效扩展场景多样性的方法。

Method: 结合3D世界生成模型和语言驱动场景设计，生成数百个包含独特物体和背景的交互场景，实现可扩展且高度并行的策略学习。使用预训练模仿基线，通过强化学习微调，并利用领域随机化实现仿真到真实世界的迁移。

Result: 仿真成功率从9.7%提升至79.8%，任务完成时间加速1.25倍。仿真到真实世界迁移成功率从21.7%提升至75%，真实世界任务完成时间加速1.13倍。消融研究表明增加场景多样性直接改善零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 利用3D世界生成模型可以高效生成多样化交互场景，使视觉语言动作模型在强化学习微调过程中保持泛化能力，实现高性能的仿真到真实世界迁移，为机器人学习提供可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The strong performance of large vision-language models (VLMs) trained with reinforcement learning (RL) has motivated similar approaches for fine-tuning vision-language-action (VLA) models in robotics. Many recent works fine-tune VLAs directly in the real world to avoid addressing the sim-to-real gap. While real-world RL circumvents sim-to-real issues, it inherently limits the generality of the resulting VLA, as scaling scene and object diversity in the physical world is prohibitively difficult. This leads to the paradoxical outcome of transforming a broadly pretrained model into an overfitted, scene-specific policy. Training in simulation can instead provide access to diverse scenes, but designing those scenes is also costly. In this work, we show that VLAs can be RL fine-tuned without sacrificing generality and with reduced labor by leveraging 3D world generative models. Using these models together with a language-driven scene designer, we generate hundreds of diverse interactive scenes containing unique objects and backgrounds, enabling scalable and highly parallel policy learning. Starting from a pretrained imitation baseline, our approach increases simulation success from 9.7% to 79.8% while achieving a 1.25$\times$ speedup in task completion time. We further demonstrate successful sim-to-real transfer enabled by the quality of the generated digital twins together with domain randomization, improving real-world success from 21.7% to 75% and achieving a 1.13$\times$ speedup. Finally, we further highlight the benefits of leveraging the effectively unlimited data from 3D world generative models through an ablation study showing that increasing scene diversity directly improves zero-shot generalization.

</details>


### [45] [Inductance-Based Force Self-Sensing in Fiber-Reinforced Pneumatic Twisted-and-Coiled Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18555)
*Yunsong Zhang,Tianlin Li,Mingyang Yang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种集成导电镍线的自感知纤维增强气动扭卷致动器，通过电感反馈实现固有力量估计和间接位移推断，解决了传统FR-PTCAs强迟滞和缺乏固有本体感知的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纤维增强气动扭卷致动器具有高功率密度和柔顺性，但其强迟滞特性和缺乏固有本体感知能力限制了有效的闭环控制应用。

Method: 集成导电镍线构建自感知FR-PTCA，利用电感-力量关系的确定性低迟滞特性，开发参数化自感知模型和非线性混合观测器（结合扩展卡尔曼滤波和约束优化）。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法的力量估计精度与外部负载传感器相当，在不同负载条件下保持鲁棒性能，电感-力量关系在恒定压力下呈现确定性低迟滞特性。

Conclusion: 提出的自感知FR-PTCA通过电感反馈实现了准确的力量估计和状态观测，为具有强迟滞特性的软体致动器提供了有效的闭环控制解决方案。

Abstract: Fiber-reinforced pneumatic twisted-and-coiled actuators (FR-PTCAs) offer high power density and compliance but their strong hysteresis and lack of intrinsic proprioception limit effective closed-loop control. This paper presents a self-sensing FR-PTCA integrated with a conductive nickel wire that enables intrinsic force estimation and indirect displacement inference via inductance feedback. Experimental characterization reveals that the inductance of the actuator exhibits a deterministic, low-hysteresis inductance-force relationship at constant pressures, in contrast to the strongly hysteretic inductance-length behavior. Leveraging this property, this paper develops a parametric self-sensing model and a nonlinear hybrid observer that integrates an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) with constrained optimization to resolve the ambiguity in the inductance-force mapping and estimate actuator states.
