Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves force estimation accuracy comparable to that of external load cells and maintains robust performance under varying load conditions.

</details>


### [46] [TiBCLaG: A Trigger-induced Bistable Compliant Laparoscopic Grasper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18559)
*Joel J Nellikkunnel,Prabhat Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 设计并验证了一种单件式、全柔顺、双稳态的腹腔镜抓钳，通过柔顺机构和双稳态设计减少零件数量，实现无需持续用户输入的稳定抓取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统腹腔镜抓钳采用多连杆刚性机构，制造公差要求严格，导致生产和装配成本高昂。需要开发更简单、成本更低的替代方案。

Method: 采用单件式全柔顺设计，集成柔顺扳机和柔顺夹持末端执行器，通过控制推杆耦合。使用双元件梁约束模型设计V梁类元件控制变形和刚度，实现弹性储能并防止突跳失稳。末端执行器设计为柔顺夹爪，通过弹性变形实现自适应抓取。使用非线性有限元分析验证开合性能，通过熔融沉积3D打印制造原型。

Result: 制造的原型展示了可靠的双稳态驱动，验证了这种柔顺腹腔镜抓钳架构的可行性。设计消除了多个刚性连杆的需求，显著减少了零件数量。

Conclusion: 提出的单件式全柔顺双稳态腹腔镜抓钳设计是可行的，能够减少零件数量、降低制造成本，同时实现无需持续用户输入的稳定抓取，为低成本腹腔镜器械提供了新方案。

Abstract: Industrial laparoscopic graspers use multi-link rigid mechanisms manufactured to tight tolerances, resulting in high manufacturing and assembly costs. This work presents the design and proof-of-concept validation of a monolithic, fully compliant, bistable, laparoscopic grasper that eliminates the need for multiple rigid links, thereby reducing part count. The device integrates a compliant trigger and a compliant gripper end-effector, coupled via a control push-rod, to achieve stable grasping without continuous user input. The trigger mechanism is synthesized using a Two-Element Beam Constraint Model as a design framework to control the deformation and stiffness of V-beam-like elements. This technique enables elastic energy storage while preventing snap-through instability. The end-effector is designed as a compliant gripper to achieve adaptive grasping through elastic deformation. Jaws' opening-and-closing performance is demonstrated using nonlinear finite element analysis. The laparoscopic design presented here is fabricated using fused deposition 3D printing. The fabricated prototype demonstrates reliable bistable actuation, confirming the feasibility of such compliant laparoscopic grasper architectures.

</details>


### [47] [Benchmarking Visual Feature Representations for LiDAR-Inertial-Visual Odometry Under Challenging Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18589)
*Eunseon Choi,Junwoo Hong,Daehan Lee,Sanghyun Park,Hyunyoung Jo,Sunyoung Kim,Changho Kang,Seongsam Kim,Yonghan Jung,Jungwook Park,Seul Koo,Soohee Han*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合视觉里程计方法，将直接光度法与基于描述符的特征匹配相结合，以提升在视觉挑战环境下的定位性能，并在FAST-LIVO2框架基础上进行了扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的精确定位对于环境建图和幸存者搜索等任务至关重要。在低光照、过曝、光照变化和高视差等视觉挑战环境下，传统视觉里程计方法性能显著下降，影响机器人导航的鲁棒性。虽然已有研究提出LiDAR-惯性-视觉里程计（LIVO）框架来应对这些挑战，但仍需进一步提升视觉状态估计的鲁棒性。

Method: 本文扩展了基于FAST-LIVO2的框架，提出了一种混合方法，将直接光度法与基于描述符的特征匹配相结合。对于描述符特征匹配，提出了四种配置：ORB+汉明距离、SuperPoint+SuperGlue、SuperPoint+LightGlue、XFeat+互最近邻。通过准确性、计算成本和特征跟踪稳定性对提出的配置进行基准测试，从而对视觉描述符的适应性和适用性进行定量比较。

Result: 实验结果表明，提出的混合方法优于传统的稀疏直接法。在光照变化导致光度不一致的区域，稀疏直接法经常无法收敛，而提出的方法在相同条件下仍能保持鲁棒性能。此外，基于学习描述符的混合方法能够在挑战性环境中实现鲁棒可靠的视觉状态估计。

Conclusion: 提出的混合视觉里程计方法通过结合直接光度法和描述符特征匹配，显著提升了在视觉挑战环境下的定位性能，特别是在光照变化等传统方法容易失效的场景中表现出更强的鲁棒性。基于学习的描述符进一步增强了系统在复杂环境中的适应能力。

Abstract: Accurate localization in autonomous driving is critical for successful missions including environmental mapping and survivor searches. In visually challenging environments, including low-light conditions, overexposure, illumination changes, and high parallax, the performance of conventional visual odometry methods significantly degrade undermining robust robotic navigation. Researchers have recently proposed LiDAR-inertial-visual odometry (LIVO) frameworks, that integrate LiDAR, IMU, and camera sensors, to address these challenges. This paper extends the FAST-LIVO2-based framework by introducing a hybrid approach that integrates direct photometric methods with descriptor-based feature matching. For the descriptor-based feature matching, this work proposes pairs of ORB with the Hamming distance, SuperPoint with SuperGlue, SuperPoint with LightGlue, and XFeat with the mutual nearest neighbor. The proposed configurations are benchmarked by accuracy, computational cost, and feature tracking stability, enabling a quantitative comparison of the adaptability and applicability of visual descriptors. The experimental results reveal that the proposed hybrid approach outperforms the conventional sparse-direct method. Although the sparse-direct method often fails to converge in regions where photometric inconsistency arises due to illumination changes, the proposed approach still maintains robust performance under the same conditions. Furthermore, the hybrid approach with learning-based descriptors enables robust and reliable visual state estimation across challenging environments.

</details>


### [48] [REST: Receding Horizon Explorative Steiner Tree for Zero-Shot Object-Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18624)
*Shuqi Xiao,Maani Ghaffari,Chengzhong Xu,Hui Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REST提出了一种基于路径树的零样本目标导航框架，通过构建探索性斯坦纳树作为选项空间，利用LLM进行粗到细推理，在保持高成功率的同时实现了最佳路径效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本目标导航的层次化方法主要关注场景理解和高级决策，但忽略了选项设计。传统方法将选项简化为孤立的路径点进行独立评分，这隐藏了沿途的信息增益价值，且非结构化的候选集合模糊了候选路径之间的关系

Method: REST框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 从在线RGB-D流构建显式开放词汇3D地图；(2) 通过基于采样的规划方法，以智能体为中心构建安全且信息丰富的路径树作为选项空间；(3) 将每个分支文本化为空间叙事，通过思维链LLM推理选择最佳路径

Result: 在Gibson、HM3D和HSSD三个基准测试中，REST在成功率方面始终位列前茅，同时实现了最佳或次佳的路径效率，展示了效率与成功率之间的良好平衡

Conclusion: 将选项空间设计为路径树而非孤立路径点，能够暴露沿途信息增益并支持粗到细的LLM推理，从而在零样本目标导航任务中实现更好的效率-成功率平衡

Abstract: Zero-shot object-goal navigation (ZSON) requires navigating unknown environments to find a target object without task-specific training. Prior hierarchical training-free solutions invest in scene understanding (\textit{belief}) and high-level decision-making (\textit{policy}), yet overlook the design of \textit{option}, i.e., a subgoal candidate proposed from evolving belief and presented to policy for selection. In practice, options are reduced to isolated waypoints scored independently: single destinations hide the value gathered along the journey; an unstructured collection obscures the relationships among candidates. Our insight is that the option space should be a \textit{tree of paths}. Full paths expose en-route information gain that destination-only scoring systematically neglects; a tree of shared segments enables coarse-to-fine LLM reasoning that dismisses or pursues entire branches before examining individual leaves, compressing the combinatorial path space into an efficient hierarchy. We instantiate this insight in \textbf{REST} (Receding Horizon Explorative Steiner Tree), a training-free framework that (1) builds an explicit open-vocabulary 3D map from online RGB-D streams; (2) grows an agent-centric tree of safe and informative paths as the option space via sampling-based planning; and (3) textualizes each branch into a spatial narrative and selects the next-best path through chain-of-thought LLM reasoning. Across the Gibson, HM3D, and HSSD benchmarks, REST consistently ranks among the top methods in success rate while achieving the best or second-best path efficiency, demonstrating a favorable efficiency-success balance.

</details>


### [49] [CSSDF-Net: Safe Motion Planning Based on Neural Implicit Representations of Configuration Space Distance Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18669)
*Haohua Chen,Yixuan Zhou,Yifan Zhou,Hesheng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CSSDF-Net：一种在构型空间中学习连续符号距离场的方法，为高维机械臂操作提供可微分的距离和梯度查询，支持零样本泛化到未见环境


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有几何碰撞检测器通常不可微分，而基于工作空间的隐式距离模型受限于高度非线性的工作空间-构型映射，收敛性差，且自碰撞和环境碰撞常作为独立约束处理。需要一种可微分、场景无关的距离查询机制来指导安全运动生成。

Method: 提出CSSDF-Net，直接在构型空间中学习连续符号距离场，提供统一的几何安全概念下的关节空间距离和梯度查询。采用基于空间哈希的数据生成管道编码机器人中心几何先验，支持对任意障碍点集的风险构型高效检索。

Result: 在平面机械臂和7自由度机械臂上的实验表明，该方法具有稳定的梯度，在静态和动态场景中有效避免碰撞，对大规模点云查询具有实用的推理延迟，支持在未见环境中的部署。

Conclusion: CSSDF-Net通过构型空间符号距离场提供了一种可微分、场景无关的碰撞检测和避障方法，能够零样本泛化到新环境，并成功集成到安全约束轨迹优化和滚动时域MPC中，实现离线和在线避障。

Abstract: High-dimensional manipulator operation in unstructured environments requires a differentiable, scene-agnostic distance query mechanism to guide safe motion generation. Existing geometric collision checkers are typically non-differentiable, while workspace-based implicit distance models are hindered by the highly nonlinear workspace--configuration mapping and often suffer from poor convergence; moreover, self-collision and environment collision are commonly handled as separate constraints. We propose Configuration-Space Signed Distance Field-Net (CSSDF-Net), which learns a continuous signed distance field directly in configuration space to provide joint-space distance and gradient queries under a unified geometric notion of safety. To enable zero-shot generalization without environment-specific retraining, we introduce a spatial-hashing-based data generation pipeline that encodes robot-centric geometric priors and supports efficient retrieval of risk configurations for arbitrary obstacle point sets. The learned distance field is integrated into safety-constrained trajectory optimization and receding-horizon MPC, enabling both offline planning and online reactive avoidance. Experiments on a planar arm and a 7-DoF manipulator demonstrate stable gradients, effective collision avoidance in static and dynamic scenes, and practical inference latency for large-scale point-cloud queries, supporting deployment in previously unseen environments.

</details>


### [50] [ADMM-Based Distributed MPC with Control Barrier Functions for Safe Multi-Robot Quadrupedal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19170)
*Yicheng Zeng,Ruturaj S. Sambhus,Basit Muhammad Imran,Jeeseop Kim,Vittorio Pastore,Kaveh Akbari Hamed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种完全去中心化的模型预测控制框架，结合控制屏障函数约束，用于多机器人腿式系统的安全关键轨迹规划，通过ADMM分布式优化实现并行计算，显著降低规划时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人腿式系统中实现安全关键轨迹规划时，控制屏障函数约束引入了显式的智能体间耦合，导致无法直接分解最优控制问题。需要开发一种完全去中心化的方法，在保持安全性和动态可行性的同时实现并行计算。

Method: 采用基于交替方向乘子法的结构化分布式优化框架，通过新颖的节点-边分割公式和共识约束，将全局问题分解为独立的节点局部和边局部二次规划问题，仅需邻居间通信即可并行求解。该方法集成到四足机器人的分层运动控制架构中，包含高层分布式轨迹规划、中层非线性MPC和低层全身控制。

Result: 在两个Unitree Go2四足机器人上进行硬件实验，并在涉及最多四个机器人的数值模拟中验证。结果显示，分布式公式在四智能体情况下将平均每周期规划时间减少高达51%，同时实现与集中式MPC相当的性能，支持高效的实时去中心化实施。

Conclusion: 提出的分布式框架成功解决了安全关键MPC中的耦合问题，实现了完全去中心化的轨迹优化，在保持安全性和动态可行性的同时显著提高了计算效率，为多机器人腿式系统的实时安全导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a fully decentralized model predictive control (MPC) framework with control barrier function (CBF) constraints for safety-critical trajectory planning in multi-robot legged systems. The incorporation of CBF constraints introduces explicit inter-agent coupling, which prevents direct decomposition of the resulting optimal control problems. To address this challenge, we reformulate the centralized safety-critical MPC problem using a structured distributed optimization framework based on the alternating direction method of multipliers (ADMM). By introducing a novel node-edge splitting formulation with consensus constraints, the proposed approach decomposes the global problem into independent node-local and edge-local quadratic programs that can be solved in parallel using only neighbor-to-neighbor communication. This enables fully decentralized trajectory optimization with symmetric computational load across agents while preserving safety and dynamic feasibility. The proposed framework is integrated into a hierarchical locomotion control architecture for quadrupedal robots, combining high-level distributed trajectory planning, mid-level nonlinear MPC enforcing single rigid body dynamics, and low-level whole-body control enforcing full-order robot dynamics. The effectiveness of the proposed approach is demonstrated through hardware experiments on two Unitree Go2 quadrupedal robots and numerical simulations involving up to four robots navigating uncertain environments with rough terrain and external disturbances. The results show that the proposed distributed formulation achieves performance comparable to centralized MPC while reducing the average per-cycle planning time by up to 51% in the four-agent case, enabling efficient real-time decentralized implementation.

</details>


### [51] [Empathetic Motion Generation for Humanoid Educational Robots via Reasoning-Guided Vision--Language--Motion Diffusion Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18771)
*Fuze Sun,Lingyu Li,Lekan Dai,Xinyu Fan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RG-VLMD：一种推理引导的视觉-语言-运动扩散框架，为教育场景中的人形机器人生成指令感知的伴随语音手势，通过多模态情感估计、教学推理和教学行为条件化运动合成实现自适应且语义一致的机器人行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在教育人机交互中，机器人需要生成与教学内容语义一致、具有教学表现力的伴随语音手势。现有方法缺乏对教学情境的推理能力，难以产生适应性强且结构化的手势模式。

Method: 提出RG-VLMD框架：1) 使用门控专家混合模型从文本、视觉和声学特征预测情感效价/唤醒度；2) 通过情感驱动策略映射到离散教学行为类别；3) 采用扩散式运动生成器，通过加性潜在约束和辅助动作组监督，结合片段级意图和帧级教学调度进行条件化生成。

Result: 相比基线扩散模型，RG-VLMD生成更具结构化和区分性的运动模式，经运动统计和成对距离分析验证。生成的运动序列保持物理合理性，可重定向到NAO机器人实时执行。

Conclusion: 推理引导的教学条件化提高了教育人机交互中手势的可控性和教学表现力，为自适应机器人教学行为生成提供了有效框架。

Abstract: This article suggests a reasoning-guided vision-language-motion diffusion framework (RG-VLMD) for generating instruction-aware co-speech gestures for humanoid robots in educational scenarios. The system integrates multi-modal affective estimation, pedagogical reasoning, and teaching-act-conditioned motion synthesis to enable adaptive and semantically consistent robot behavior. A gated mixture-of-experts model predicts Valence/Arousal from input text, visual, and acoustic features, which then mapped to discrete teaching-act categories through an affect-driven policy.These signals condition a diffusion-based motion generator using clip-level intent and frame-level instructional schedules via additive latent restriction with auxiliary action-group supervision. Compared to a baseline diffusion model, our proposed method produces more structured and distinctive motion patterns, as verified by motion statics and pairwise distance analysis. Generated motion sequences remain physically plausible and can be retargeted to a NAO robot for real-time execution. The results reveal that reasoning-guided instructional conditioning improves gesture controllability and pedagogical expressiveness in educational human-robot interaction.

</details>


### [52] [ViTac-Tracing: Visual-Tactile Imitation Learning of Deformable Object Tracing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18784)
*Yongqiang Zhao,Haining Luo,Yupeng Wang,Emmanouil Spyrakos Papastavridis,Yiannis Demiris,Shan Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于视觉-触觉模仿学习的可变形物体追踪方法，通过局部加权损失和全局任务损失实现1D和2D可变形物体的统一追踪，在真实环境中取得了良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可变形物体追踪方法存在局限性：要么缺乏跨类别泛化能力，要么难以在真实世界中可靠完成任务。需要一种既能保持通用性又能在真实环境中有效工作的方法。

Method: 提出视觉-触觉模仿学习方法，从局部和全局两个角度设计：局部引入加权损失强调在触觉图像中心附近保持接触的动作；全局提出追踪任务损失帮助策略调节任务进度。硬件上集成触觉感知到低成本遥操作系统。

Result: 在多样化的1D和2D可变形物体上进行了大量消融和对比实验，平均成功率在已见物体上达到80%，在未见物体上达到65%，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过视觉-触觉融合和局部-全局双重设计，实现了可变形物体追踪的通用性和可靠性，为下游操作任务提供了有效支持。

Abstract: Deformable objects often appear in unstructured configurations. Tracing deformable objects helps bringing them into extended states and facilitating the downstream manipulation tasks. Due to the requirements for object-specific modeling or sim-to-real transfer, existing tracing methods either lack generalizability across different categories of deformable objects or struggle to complete tasks reliably in the real world. To address this, we propose a novel visual-tactile imitation learning method to achieve one-dimensional (1D) and two-dimensional (2D) deformable object tracing with a unified model. Our method is designed from both local and global perspectives based on visual and tactile sensing. Locally, we introduce a weighted loss that emphasizes actions maintaining contact near the center of the tactile image, improving fine-grained adjustment. Globally, we propose a tracing task loss that helps the policy to regulate task progression. On the hardware side, to compensate for the limited features extracted from visual information, we integrate tactile sensing into a low-cost teleoperation system considering both the teleoperator and the robot. Extensive ablation and comparative experiments on diverse 1D and 2D deformable objects demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, achieving an average success rate of 80% on seen objects and 65% on unseen objects.

</details>


### [53] ["You've got a friend in me": Co-Designing a Peer Social Robot for Young Newcomers' Language and Cultural Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18804)
*Neil Fernandes,Cheng Tang,Tehniyat Shahbaz,Alex Hauschildt,Emily Davies-Robinson,Yue Hu,Kerstin Dautenhahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究者与加拿大United for Literacy的导师合作设计了Maple——一款桌面社交辅助机器人，作为新移民儿童英语和文化学习的练习伙伴，以解决社区扫盲项目人手不足和一对一辅导时间有限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加拿大社区扫盲项目面临人员配置有限和一对一辅导时间稀缺的挑战，这限制了为新移民儿童提供个性化的英语和文化学习支持。需要创新解决方案来增强现有导师指导下的学习效果。

Method: 采用协同设计研究方法，通过观察和访谈United for Literacy的导师，提取新移民儿童特定需求，设计并开发了Maple原型系统。该系统采用短篇故事活动、多模态支架（语音、面部反馈、手势）和嵌入式测验，同时生成可供导师使用的形成性信号。

Result: 开发了Maple原型系统，这是一个桌面式、同伴式社交辅助机器人，作为导师指导课程中的练习伙伴。系统整合了新移民特定需求，通过多模态交互支持语言社会化，并为导师提供可操作的形成性评估信号。

Conclusion: 本研究为支持社区环境中语言社会化的导师在环社交辅助机器人系统设计提供了启示，并提出了在真实项目中以儿童为中心的评价方向。Maple展示了如何通过技术增强有限资源下的个性化学习支持。

Abstract: Community literacy programs supporting young newcomer children in Canada face limited staffing and scarce one-to-one time, which constrains personalized English and cultural learning support. This paper reports on a co-design study with United for Literacy tutors that informed Maple, a table-top, peer-like Socially Assistive Robot (SAR) designed as a practice partner within tutor-mediated sessions. From shadowing and co-design interviews, we derived newcomer-specific requirements and added them in an integrated prototype that uses short story-based activities, multi-modal scaffolding (speech, facial feedback, gesture), and embedded quizzes that support attention while producing tutor-actionable formative signals. We contribute system design implications for tutor-in-the-loop SARs supporting language socialization in community settings and outline directions for child-centered evaluation in authentic programs.

</details>


### [54] [V-Dreamer: Automating Robotic Simulation and Trajectory Synthesis via Video Generation Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18811)
*Songjia He,Zixuan Chen,Hongyu Ding,Dian Shao,Jieqi Shi,Chenxu Li,Jing Huo,Yang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: V-Dreamer是一个全自动框架，能够从自然语言指令直接生成开放词汇、仿真就绪的机器人操作环境和可执行的专家轨迹，用于训练通用机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练通用机器人需要大规模、多样化的操作数据，但现实世界数据收集成本高昂，现有仿真器受限于固定的资产库和手动启发式方法，需要自动化生成高质量仿真环境的方法。

Method: 采用生成式流水线：1) 使用大语言模型和3D生成模型构建物理基础的3D场景，通过几何约束验证确保稳定、无碰撞布局；2) 利用视频生成模型作为运动先验进行行为合成；3) 通过Sim-to-Gen视觉-运动学对齐模块将视觉预测映射为可执行的机器人轨迹。

Result: 在桌面操作任务上的广泛评估表明，基于合成轨迹训练的模仿学习策略能够稳健地泛化到仿真中的未见物体，并实现有效的仿真到现实迁移，成功操作真实世界的新物体。

Conclusion: V-Dreamer框架能够自动生成高质量、多样化的机器人操作数据，支持开放词汇场景构建和轨迹生成，为训练通用机器人提供了可扩展的解决方案，实现了从仿真到现实的成功迁移。

Abstract: Training generalist robots demands large-scale, diverse manipulation data, yet real-world collection is prohibitively expensive, and existing simulators are often constrained by fixed asset libraries and manual heuristics. To bridge this gap, we present V-Dreamer, a fully automated framework that generates open-vocabulary, simulation-ready manipulation environments and executable expert trajectories directly from natural language instructions. V-Dreamer employs a novel generative pipeline that constructs physically grounded 3D scenes using large language models and 3D generative models, validated by geometric constraints to ensure stable, collision-free layouts. Crucially, for behavior synthesis, we leverage video generation models as rich motion priors. These visual predictions are then mapped into executable robot trajectories via a robust Sim-to-Gen visual-kinematic alignment module utilizing CoTracker3 and VGGT. This pipeline supports high visual diversity and physical fidelity without manual intervention. To evaluate the generated data, we train imitation learning policies on synthesized trajectories encompassing diverse object and environment variations. Extensive evaluations on tabletop manipulation tasks using the Piper robotic arm demonstrate that our policies robustly generalize to unseen objects in simulation and achieve effective sim-to-real transfer, successfully manipulating novel real-world objects.

</details>


### [55] [Lightweight Model Predictive Control for Spacecraft Rendezvous Attitude Synchronization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18921)
*Peter Stadler,Alexander Meinert,Niklas Baldauf,Alen Turnwald*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出两种基于姿态偏差公式的轻量级模型预测控制方法，用于航天器交会同步期间的反应轮姿态跟踪，在ARM Cortex-M7上验证了实时可行性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对资源受限的航天器交会任务，需要轻量级、计算高效的姿态控制方法，能够在商业现货设备上实现实时控制

Method: 提出新颖的姿态偏差公式，实现角速度的线性约束；开发单环和双环MPC控制器，双环方法在内部嵌入稳定反馈控制器形成线性时不变系统；使用CasADi实现自动代码生成，在Basilisk框架中验证

Result: 实验结果显示跟踪精度提升，计算量和内存消耗减少；在ARM Cortex-M7（代表New Space平台商用设备）上成功部署，证实了实时可行性

Conclusion: 提出的轻量级MPC方法适合资源受限的航天器交会任务中的机载姿态控制，在商用硬件上实现了实时性能

Abstract: This work introduces two lightweight model predictive control (MPC) approaches for attitude tracking with reaction wheels during spacecraft rendezvous synchronization. Both approaches are based on a novel attitude deviation formulation, which enables the use of inherently linear constraints on angular velocity. We develop a single-loop and a dual-loop MPC; the latter embeds a stabilizing feedback controller within the inner loop, yielding a linear time-invariant system. Both controllers are implemented with CasADi - including automatic code generation - evaluated across various solvers, and validated within the Basilisk astrodynamics simulation framework. The experimental results demonstrate improved tracking accuracy alongside reductions in computational effort and memory consumption. Finally, embedded delivery to an ARM Cortex-M7 - representative of commercial off-the-shelf devices used in New Space platforms - confirms the real-time feasibility of these approaches and highlights their suitability for onboard attitude control in resource-constrained spacecraft rendezvous missions.

</details>


### [56] [PRIOR: Perceptive Learning for Humanoid Locomotion with Reference Gait Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18979)
*Chenxi Han,Shilu He,Yi Cheng,Linqi Ye,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRIOR是一个高效可复现的人形机器人运动框架，通过参数化步态生成器、基于GRU的地形状态估计器和地形自适应脚步奖励，实现了在复杂地形上的稳健自然步态行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前训练感知型人形机器人运动策略面临挑战，通常需要多阶段训练流程、对抗性目标或大量真实世界校准。需要一种更简单有效的方法来实现复杂地形上的自然步态行走。

Method: 1. 参数化步态生成器：提供源自动作捕捉的稳定参考轨迹，无需对抗训练；2. GRU状态估计器：通过自监督高度图重建从自我中心深度图像推断地形几何；3. 地形自适应脚步奖励：引导脚步放置到可穿越区域。系统分析了深度图像分辨率权衡，找到实时约束下最大化地形保真度的配置。

Result: 在包括楼梯、箱子和间隙等不同难度地形上的综合实验表明，每个组件都带来互补且必要的性能提升。完整框架实现了100%的穿越成功率。深度图像分辨率优化显著减少了感知开销而不降低穿越性能。

Conclusion: PRIOR提供了一个简单有效的框架，通过三个核心组件实现了稳健的地形穿越和自然步态。该框架将开源，包括训练流程、参数化步态生成器和评估基准，为Isaac Lab上的人形机器人运动研究提供可复现的基础。

Abstract: Training perceptive humanoid locomotion policies that traverse complex terrains with natural gaits remains an open challenge, typically demanding multi-stage training pipelines, adversarial objectives, or extensive real-world calibration. We present PRIOR, an efficient and reproducible framework built on Isaac Lab that achieves robust terrain traversal with human-like gaits through a simple yet effective design: (i) a parametric gait generator that supplies stable reference trajectories derived from motion capture without adversarial training, (ii) a GRU-based state estimator that infers terrain geometry directly from egocentric depth images via self-supervised heightmap reconstruction, and (iii) terrain-adaptive footstep rewards that guide foot placement toward traversable regions. Through systematic analysis of depth image resolution trade-offs, we identify configurations that maximize terrain fidelity under real-time constraints, substantially reducing perceptual overhead without degrading traversal performance. Comprehensive experiments across terrains of varying difficulty-including stairs, boxes, and gaps-demonstrate that each component yields complementary and essential performance gains, with the full framework achieving a 100% traversal success rate. We will open-source the complete PRIOR framework, including the training pipeline, parametric gait generator, and evaluation benchmarks, to serve as a reproducible foundation for humanoid locomotion research on Isaac Lab.

</details>


### [57] [MERGE: Guided Vision-Language Models for Multi-Actor Event Reasoning and Grounding in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18988)
*Joerg Deigmoeller,Nakul Agarwal,Stephan Hasler,Daniel Tanneberg,Anna Belardinelli,Reza Ghoddoosian,Chao Wang,Felix Ocker,Fan Zhang,Behzad Dariush,Michael Gienger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MERGE是一个用于动态人机群体交互中情境感知的系统，通过视觉语言模型和感知流水线结合，实现参与者、物体和事件的一致识别与跟踪，在GROUND数据集上性能提升2倍，运行时间减少4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态人机群体交互需要一致的情境感知，但现有方法存在高成本、高延迟和输出碎片化问题，缺乏合适的多参与者协作基准数据集。

Method: MERGE采用解耦设计：轻量级流式模块持续处理视觉输入检测变化，仅在必要时调用视觉语言模型；通过唯一识别物理实例并构建参与者-动作-对象关系，确保时间一致性。

Result: 在GROUND数据集上，MERGE将平均情境感知分数提升2倍，运行时间减少4倍，优于GPT-4o、GPT-5和Gemini 2.5 Flash等VLM基线模型。

Conclusion: MERGE通过解耦感知与推理的设计，在保持VLM零样本泛化能力的同时提高了效率和一致性，为多参与者协作提供了有效的情境感知解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce MERGE, a system for situational grounding of actors, objects, and events in dynamic human-robot group interactions. Effective collaboration in such settings requires consistent situational awareness, built on persistent representations of people and objects and an episodic abstraction of events. MERGE achieves this by uniquely identifying physical instances of actors (humans or robots) and objects and structuring them into actor-action-object relations, ensuring temporal consistency across interactions. Central to MERGE is the integration of Vision-Language Models (VLMs) guided with a perception pipeline: a lightweight streaming module continuously processes visual input to detect changes and selectively invokes the VLM only when necessary. This decoupled design preserves the reasoning power and zero-shot generalization of VLMs while improving efficiency, avoiding both the high monetary cost and the latency of frame-by-frame captioning that leads to fragmented and delayed outputs. To address the absence of suitable benchmarks for multi-actor collaboration, we introduce the GROUND dataset, which offers fine-grained situational annotations of multi-person and human-robot interactions. On this dataset, our approach improves the average grounding score by a factor of 2 compared to the performance of VLM-only baselines - including GPT-4o, GPT-5 and Gemini 2.5 Flash - while also reducing run-time by a factor of 4. The code and data are available at www.github.com/HRI-EU/merge.

</details>


### [58] [ATG-MoE: Autoregressive trajectory generation with mixture-of-experts for assembly skill learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19029)
*Weihang Huang,Chaoran Zhang,Xiaoxin Deng,Hao Zhou,Zhaobo Xu,Shubo Cui,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ATG-MoE：一种端到端自回归轨迹生成方法，通过专家混合架构从演示中学习装配技能，实现多模态输入到操作轨迹的闭环映射，解决了传统方法的泛化性差、多阶段设计复杂和多技能集成有限的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 柔性制造需要机器人系统能够适应不断变化的任务、物体和环境。传统机器人编程劳动密集且不灵活，现有基于学习的装配方法存在位置泛化能力弱、多阶段设计复杂、多技能集成能力有限等问题。

Method: 提出ATG-MoE方法：1）建立从多模态输入（RGB-D观测、自然语言指令、机器人本体感知）到操作轨迹的闭环映射；2）集成多模态特征融合用于场景和任务理解；3）使用自回归序列建模生成时间一致的轨迹；4）采用专家混合架构实现统一的多技能学习。

Result: 在减压阀装配任务的8个代表性装配技能上进行评估：模拟环境中平均抓取成功率96.3%，平均总体成功率91.8%，表现出强大的泛化能力和有效的多技能集成。真实世界实验进一步验证了其在多技能工业装配中的实用性。

Conclusion: ATG-MoE通过端到端自回归轨迹生成和专家混合架构，成功解决了传统装配方法的局限性，实现了强大的位置泛化、时间一致性轨迹生成和高效的多技能集成，为柔性制造中的机器人装配提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Flexible manufacturing requires robot systems that can adapt to constantly changing tasks, objects, and environments. However, traditional robot programming is labor-intensive and inflexible, while existing learning-based assembly methods often suffer from weak positional generalization, complex multi-stage designs, and limited multi-skill integration capability. To address these issues, this paper proposes ATG-MoE, an end-to-end autoregressive trajectory generation method with mixture of experts for assembly skill learning from demonstration. The proposed method establishes a closed-loop mapping from multi-modal inputs, including RGB-D observations, natural language instructions, and robot proprioception to manipulation trajectories. It integrates multi-modal feature fusion for scene and task understanding, autoregressive sequence modeling for temporally coherent trajectory generation, and a mixture-of-experts architecture for unified multi-skill learning. In contrast to conventional methods that separate visual perception and control or train different skills independently, ATG-MoE directly incorporates visual information into trajectory generation and supports efficient multi-skill integration within a single model. We train and evaluate the proposed method on eight representative assembly skills from a pressure-reducing valve assembly task. Experimental results show that ATG-MoE achieves strong overall performance in simulation, with an average grasp success rate of 96.3% and an average overall success rate of 91.8%, while also demonstrating strong generalization and effective multi-skill integration. Real-world experiments further verify its practicality for multi-skill industrial assembly. The project page can be found at https://hwh23.github.io/ATG-MoE

</details>


### [59] [Fire as a Service: Augmenting Robot Simulators with Thermally and Visually Accurate Fire Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19063)
*Anton R. Wagner,Madhan Balaji Rao,Helge Wrede,Sören Pirk,Xuesu Xiao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FaaS是一个异步协同仿真框架，为现有机器人仿真器添加高保真、计算高效的火灾模拟，解决现有仿真器忽视热学和光学复杂现象的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人仿真器主要关注刚体动力学和逼真渲染，但忽视了真实火灾环境中的热学和光学复杂现象，这阻碍了消防机器人的可靠能力评估和部署前训练数据的生成。

Method: 开发了Fire as a Service (FaaS)异步协同仿真框架，将高保真火灾模拟与现有机器人仿真器集成，实现准确的多组分热力学热传递和视觉一致的体积烟雾，同时不干扰高频刚体控制循环。

Result: 框架能够集成多种机器人仿真器，生成物理准确的火灾行为，评估机器人平台面临的热危害，收集真实的多模态感知数据，并支持实时人机协同操作，通过行为克隆成功训练反应式多模态策略。

Conclusion: FaaS通过将火灾动力学添加到机器人仿真中，为机器人在火灾场景中更安全、更可靠的部署提供了可扩展的途径。

Abstract: Most existing robot simulators prioritize rigid-body dynamics and photorealistic rendering, but largely neglect the thermally and optically complex phenomena that characterize real-world fire environments. For robots envisioned as future firefighters, this limitation hinders both reliable capability evaluation and the generation of representative training data prior to deployment in hazardous scenarios. To address these challenges, we introduce Fire as a Service (FaaS), a novel, asynchronous co-simulation framework that augments existing robot simulators with high-fidelity and computationally efficient fire simulations. Our pipeline enables robots to experience accurate, multi-species thermodynamic heat transfer and visually consistent volumetric smoke without disrupting high-frequency rigid-body control loops. We demonstrate that our framework can be integrated with diverse robot simulators to generate physically accurate fire behavior, benchmark thermal hazards encountered by robotic platforms, and collect realistic multimodal perceptual data. Crucially, its real-time performance supports human-in-the-loop teleoperation, enabling the successful training of reactive, multimodal policies via Behavioral Cloning. By adding fire dynamics to robot simulations, FaaS provides a scalable pathway toward safer, more reliable deployment of robots in fire scenarios.

</details>


### [60] [CAMO: A Conditional Neural Solver for the Multi-objective Multiple Traveling Salesman Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19074)
*Fengxiaoxiao Li,Xiao Mao,Mingfeng Fan,Yifeng Zhang,Yi Li,Tanishq Duhan,Guillaume Sartoretti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CAMO是一个用于多目标多旅行商问题的条件神经求解器，通过条件编码器融合偏好向量，协作解码器协调多智能体，能够泛化到不同目标数、智能体数和偏好向量，生成高质量的帕累托前沿近似解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习方法在单智能体TSP和多目标TSP变体上表现良好，但很少同时处理多智能体协调和多目标权衡这两个复杂挑战。需要开发能够同时解决这两个问题的统一方法。

Method: CAMO包含条件编码器（将偏好向量融合到实例表示中，显式控制多目标权衡）和协作解码器（通过交替智能体选择和节点选择自回归构建多智能体路径）。使用基于REINFORCE的目标在混合问题规模分布上进行训练以提高泛化能力。

Result: CAMO在实验中优于神经和传统启发式方法，实现了更接近帕累托前沿的近似解。消融实验验证了关键组件的贡献，在移动机器人平台上的实际测试证明了其实际适用性。

Conclusion: CAMO成功解决了多目标多旅行商问题的双重挑战，通过条件神经架构实现了对多智能体协调和多目标权衡的统一处理，具有良好的泛化能力和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Robotic systems often require a team of robots to collectively visit multiple targets while optimizing competing objectives, such as total travel cost and makespan. This setting can be formulated as the Multi-Objective Multiple Traveling Salesman Problem (MOMTSP). Although learning-based methods have shown strong performance on the single-agent TSP and multi-objective TSP variants, they rarely address the combined challenges of multi-agent coordination and multi-objective trade-offs, which introduce dual sources of complexity. To bridge this gap, we propose CAMO, a conditional neural solver for MOMTSP that generalizes across varying numbers of targets, agents, and preference vectors, and yields high-quality approximations to the Pareto front (PF). Specifically, CAMO consists of a conditional encoder to fuse preferences into instance representations, enabling explicit control over multi-objective trade-offs, and a collaborative decoder that coordinates all agents by alternating agent selection and node selection to construct multi-agent tours autoregressively. To further improve generalization, we train CAMO with a REINFORCE-based objective over a mixed distribution of problem sizes. Extensive experiments show that CAMO outperforms both neural and conventional heuristics, achieving a closer approximation of PFs. In addition, ablation results validate the contributions of CAMO's key components, and real-world tests on a mobile robot platform demonstrate its practical applicability.

</details>


### [61] [Tendon-Actuated Robots with a Tapered, Flexible Polymer Backbone: Design, Fabrication, and Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19124)
*Harald Minde Hansen,Nandita Gallacher,Nicholas B. Andrews,Kristin Y. Pettersen,Jan Tommy Gravdahl,Mario di Castro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于3D打印的肌腱驱动连续体机器人设计，采用柔性锥形TPU骨架，具有可定制、低成本、快速组装的特性，并建立了考虑锥形几何的广义运动静力学模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统连续体机器人通常为单用途、高成本设计，缺乏可定制性和快速部署能力。本研究旨在开发一种可扩展、低成本、易于定制的连续体机器人平台，通过几何锥形设计实现高曲率和远端柔顺性，适用于广泛的柔顺机器人检查与操作任务。

Method: 1) 设计采用3D打印的柔性锥形TPU骨架和集成电子基座，实现肌腱张力直接控制与传感；2) 基于Cosserat杆理论建立广义前向运动静力学模型，采用牛顿方法扩展现有肌腱驱动Cosserat杆公式，显式考虑空间变化的骨架截面几何；3) 通过参数化iLogic/CAD脚本实现快速几何生成与缩放；4) 使用运动捕捉数据验证模型，通过线搜索校准杨氏模量以最小化建模误差。

Result: 1) 模型验证显示厘米级形状预测精度；2) 系统分析了骨架锥角对机器人构型空间和可操作性的影响；3) 演示了通过6自由度机械臂搭载的连续体机器人实现内窥镜夹持器的遥操作抓取；4) 建立了从参数化设计到受控肌腱驱动的简单、快速、可重复的完整框架。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种基于熔融沉积成型3D打印机制造的锥形肌腱驱动连续体机器人的完整设计、建模与控制框架。通过几何锥形设计实现了增强的柔顺性，建立的广义运动静力学模型能够准确预测机器人形状，为低成本、可定制连续体机器人的快速开发与应用提供了系统化解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents the design, modeling, and fabrication of 3D-printed, tendon-actuated continuum robots featuring a flexible, tapered backbone constructed from thermoplastic polyurethane (TPU). Our scalable design incorporates an integrated electronics base housing that enables direct tendon tension control and sensing via actuators and compression load cells. Unlike many continuum robots that are single-purpose and costly, the proposed design prioritizes customizability, rapid assembly, and low cost while enabling high curvature and enhanced distal compliance through geometric tapering, thereby supporting a broad range of compliant robotic inspection and manipulation tasks. We develop a generalized forward kinetostatic model of the tapered backbone based on Cosserat rod theory using a Newtonian approach, extending existing tendon-actuated Cosserat rod formulations to explicitly account for spatially varying backbone cross-sectional geometry. The model captures the graded stiffness profile induced by the tapering and enables systematic exploration of the configuration space as a function of the geometric design parameters. Specifically, we analyze how the backbone taper angle influences the robot's configuration space and manipulability. The model is validated against motion capture data, achieving centimeter-level shape prediction accuracy after calibrating Young's modulus via a line search that minimizes modeling error. We further demonstrate teleoperated grasping using an endoscopic gripper routed along the continuum robot, mounted on a 6-DoF robotic arm. Parameterized iLogic/CAD scripts are provided for rapid geometry generation and scaling. The presented framework establishes a simple, rapid, and reproducible pathway from parametric design to controlled tendon actuation for tapered, tendon-driven continuum robots manufactured using fused deposition modeling 3D printers.

</details>


### [62] [Introducing M: A Modular, Modifiable Social Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19134)
*Victor Nikhil Antony,Zhili Gong,Yoonjae Kim,Chien-Ming Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: M是一个开源、低成本的社会机器人平台，旨在通过使机器人更易于复制、修改和部署来减少阻碍社会机器人研究的平台摩擦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会机器人研究面临平台摩擦问题，包括机器人复制困难、修改复杂和部署成本高，这阻碍了研究的可重复性和纵向研究发展。

Method: 采用模块化机械设计、多模态传感和表达性但机械简单的驱动架构，结合ROS2原生软件包，清晰分离感知、表达控制和数据管理。平台包含仿真环境，支持快速从仿真到现实的交互行为转移。

Result: 展示了通过额外传感/驱动模块的可扩展性，提供了讲故事和双向对话辅导的交互模板示例。在实际应用中，成功用于参与式设计和为期一周的家庭部署。

Conclusion: M平台可作为纵向、可重复社会机器人研究的实用基础，通过降低平台摩擦促进社会机器人研究的发展。

Abstract: We present M, an open-source, low-cost social robot platform designed to reduce platform friction that slows social robotics research by making robots easier to reproduce, modify, and deploy in real-world settings. M combines a modular mechanical design, multimodal sensing, and expressive yet mechanically simple actuation architecture with a ROS2-native software package that cleanly separates perception, expression control, and data management. The platform includes a simulation environment with interface equivalence to hardware to support rapid sim-to-real transfer of interaction behaviors. We demonstrate extensibility through additional sensing/actuation modules and provide example interaction templates for storytelling and two-way conversational coaching. Finally, we report real-world use in participatory design and week-long in-home deployments, showing how M can serve as a practical foundation for longitudinal, reproducible social robotics research.

</details>


### [63] [Meanings and Measurements: Multi-Agent Probabilistic Grounding for Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19166)
*Swagat Padhan,Lakshya Jain,Bhavya Minesh Shah,Omkar Patil,Thao Nguyen,Nakul Gopalan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MAPG框架解决机器人理解复杂度量-语义语言查询的问题，通过多智能体概率分解和组合实现3D空间中的精确目标定位


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在语义理解方面表现良好，但缺乏对物理空间中度量约束的推理能力，导致处理复杂度量-语义语言查询时效果不佳

Method: 提出MAPG（多智能体概率定位）框架，将语言查询分解为结构化子组件，使用VLM定位每个组件，然后概率性地组合这些定位结果以产生度量一致、可操作的3D空间决策

Result: 在HM-EQA基准测试中表现优于现有基线方法；提出新的MAPG-Bench基准专门评估度量-语义目标定位；真实机器人演示显示MAPG可迁移到实际场景

Conclusion: MAPG框架有效解决了复杂度量-语义语言查询的定位问题，填补了现有语言定位评估的空白，为机器人自然语言交互提供了更精确的解决方案

Abstract: Robots collaborating with humans must convert natural language goals into actionable, physically grounded decisions. For example, executing a command such as "go two meters to the right of the fridge" requires grounding semantic references, spatial relations, and metric constraints within a 3D scene. While recent vision language models (VLMs) demonstrate strong semantic grounding capabilities, they are not explicitly designed to reason about metric constraints in physically defined spaces. In this work, we empirically demonstrate that state-of-the-art VLM-based grounding approaches struggle with complex metric-semantic language queries. To address this limitation, we propose MAPG (Multi-Agent Probabilistic Grounding), an agentic framework that decomposes language queries into structured subcomponents and queries a VLM to ground each component. MAPG then probabilistically composes these grounded outputs to produce metrically consistent, actionable decisions in 3D space. We evaluate MAPG on the HM-EQA benchmark and show consistent performance improvements over strong baselines. Furthermore, we introduce a new benchmark, MAPG-Bench, specifically designed to evaluate metric-semantic goal grounding, addressing a gap in existing language grounding evaluations. We also present a real-world robot demonstration showing that MAPG transfers beyond simulation when a structured scene representation is available.

</details>


### [64] [Sparse Autoencoders Reveal Interpretable and Steerable Features in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19183)
*Aiden Swann,Lachlain McGranahan,Hugo Buurmeijer,Monroe Kennedy,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究应用机制可解释性技术分析VLA模型内部表示，发现大多数特征对应训练演示的记忆序列，但部分特征对应可解释、通用且可操控的运动基元和语义属性，揭示了VLA泛化性的机制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在机器人操作中表现出不一致的泛化能力：在某些场景下表现优异，但在面对新物体、场景和指令时微调版本往往失败。研究旨在通过机制可解释性技术理解VLA模型的内部工作机制，探究其泛化能力的本质

Method: 在VLA模型的隐藏层激活上训练稀疏自编码器（SAEs），学习稀疏字典特征作为模型计算的紧凑可解释基。提出特征分类指标区分通用可转移基元与特定演示记忆。在LIBERO基准上进行操控实验验证，分析不同训练策略（小数据集微调、大规模多样化数据集、知识隔离）对特征性质的影响

Result: 发现大多数SAE特征对应特定训练演示的记忆序列，但部分特征对应可解释、通用且可操控的运动基元和语义属性。单个SAE特征能因果影响机器人行为，操控通用特征可诱导与其语义一致的行为，且能跨任务和场景应用。监督微调小数据集会过度放大记忆，而大规模多样化数据集或知识隔离能促进更通用特征

Conclusion: 该研究首次提供机制证据表明VLA模型能跨任务和场景学习通用特征。稀疏自编码器特征分析揭示了VLA泛化性的内部机制，为理解模型泛化不一致性提供了新视角。大规模多样化数据集训练和知识隔离是促进通用特征学习的关键策略

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have emerged as a promising approach for general-purpose robot manipulation. However, their generalization is inconsistent: while these models can perform impressively in some settings, fine-tuned variants often fail on novel objects, scenes, and instructions. We apply mechanistic interpretability techniques to better understand the inner workings of VLA models. To probe internal representations, we train Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) on hidden layer activations of the VLA. SAEs learn a sparse dictionary whose features act as a compact, interpretable basis for the model's computation. We find that the large majority of extracted SAE features correspond to memorized sequences from specific training demonstrations. However, some features correspond to interpretable, general, and steerable motion primitives and semantic properties, offering a promising glimpse toward VLA generalizability. We propose a metric to categorize features according to whether they represent generalizable transferable primitives or episode-specific memorization. We validate these findings through steering experiments on the LIBERO benchmark. We show that individual SAE features causally influence robot behavior. Steering general features induces behaviors consistent with their semantic meaning and can be applied across tasks and scenes. This work provides the first mechanistic evidence that VLAs can learn generalizable features across tasks and scenes. We observe that supervised fine-tuning on small robotics datasets disproportionately amplifies memorization. In contrast, training on larger, more diverse datasets (e.g., DROID) or using knowledge insulation promotes more general features. We provide an open-source codebase and user-friendly interface for activation collection, SAE training, and feature steering. Our project page is located at http://drvla.github.io

</details>


### [65] [FASTER: Rethinking Real-Time Flow VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19199)
*Yuxiang Lu,Zhe Liu,Xianzhe Fan,Zhenya Yang,Jinghua Hou,Junyi Li,Kaixin Ding,Hengshuang Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FASTER通过自适应优先级调度和流式管道，显著降低VLA模型反应延迟，实现实时响应


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异步推理方法主要优化轨迹平滑性，但忽视了响应环境变化的关键延迟问题。基于流的VLA模型中恒定调度方案效率低下，要求所有采样步骤完成后才能开始运动，形成反应延迟瓶颈。

Method: 提出FASTER方法，引入Horizon-Aware Schedule自适应调度机制，在流采样过程中优先处理近期动作，将立即反应的去噪过程压缩10倍至单步完成，同时保持长时域轨迹质量。结合流式客户端-服务器管道部署。

Result: FASTER显著降低了实际机器人上的有效反应延迟，特别是在消费级GPU上部署时。真实世界实验（包括高度动态的乒乓球任务）证明FASTER为通用策略提供了前所未有的实时响应能力，能够快速生成准确平滑的轨迹。

Conclusion: 通过重新思考动作分块策略中的反应概念，FASTER解决了VLA模型实时执行的关键瓶颈，实现了在物理世界中部署所需的低延迟响应能力，为通用策略的实际应用提供了重要技术突破。

Abstract: Real-time execution is crucial for deploying Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in the physical world. Existing asynchronous inference methods primarily optimize trajectory smoothness, but neglect the critical latency in reacting to environmental changes. By rethinking the notion of reaction in action chunking policies, this paper presents a systematic analysis of the factors governing reaction time. We show that reaction time follows a uniform distribution determined jointly by the Time to First Action (TTFA) and the execution horizon. Moreover, we reveal that the standard practice of applying a constant schedule in flow-based VLAs can be inefficient and forces the system to complete all sampling steps before any movement can start, forming the bottleneck in reaction latency. To overcome this issue, we propose Fast Action Sampling for ImmediaTE Reaction (FASTER). By introducing a Horizon-Aware Schedule, FASTER adaptively prioritizes near-term actions during flow sampling, compressing the denoising of the immediate reaction by tenfold (e.g., in $π_{0.5}$ and X-VLA) into a single step, while preserving the quality of long-horizon trajectory. Coupled with a streaming client-server pipeline, FASTER substantially reduces the effective reaction latency on real robots, especially when deployed on consumer-grade GPUs. Real-world experiments, including a highly dynamic table tennis task, prove that FASTER unlocks unprecedented real-time responsiveness for generalist policies, enabling rapid generation of accurate and smooth trajectories.

</details>


### [66] [OmniVTA: Visuo-Tactile World Modeling for Contact-Rich Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19201)
*Yuhang Zheng,Songen Gu,Weize Li,Yupeng Zheng,Yujie Zang,Shuai Tian,Xiang Li,Ruihai Wu,Ce Hao,Chen Gao,Si Liu,Haoran Li,Yilun Chen,Shuicheng Yan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OmniViTac是一个大规模视觉-触觉-动作数据集，包含21,000+轨迹、86个任务和100+个物体，涵盖六种物理交互模式。基于此提出的OmniVTA框架通过自监督触觉编码器、双流视觉-触觉世界模型、接触感知融合策略和60Hz反射控制器，实现了接触丰富操作的预测性接触建模与高频触觉反馈闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务（如擦拭和装配）需要准确感知接触力、摩擦力变化和状态转换，这些信息无法仅从视觉可靠推断。现有视觉-触觉操作研究面临两个主要限制：数据集规模小、任务覆盖窄；现有方法将触觉信号视为被动观测而非用于建模接触动力学或实现显式闭环控制。

Method: 提出OmniVTA框架，包含四个紧密耦合模块：1) 自监督触觉编码器；2) 双流视觉-触觉世界模型，用于预测短时域接触演化；3) 接触感知融合策略，用于动作生成；4) 60Hz反射控制器，通过闭环校正预测与观测触觉信号之间的偏差。该框架基于OmniViTac大规模数据集（21,000+轨迹，86个任务，100+物体，六种物理交互模式）开发。

Result: 真实机器人实验在所有六种交互类别中显示，OmniVTA优于现有方法，并且能够很好地泛化到未见过的物体和几何配置。这证实了将预测性接触建模与高频触觉反馈相结合对于接触丰富操作的价值。

Conclusion: OmniVTA框架通过整合预测性接触建模与高频触觉反馈闭环控制，显著提升了接触丰富操作任务的性能。大规模数据集OmniViTac为视觉-触觉操作研究提供了重要资源。该方法展示了将触觉信号主动用于接触动力学建模和闭环控制的优势，为复杂操作任务提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation tasks, such as wiping and assembly, require accurate perception of contact forces, friction changes, and state transitions that cannot be reliably inferred from vision alone. Despite growing interest in visuo-tactile manipulation, progress is constrained by two persistent limitations: existing datasets are small in scale and narrow in task coverage, and current methods treat tactile signals as passive observations rather than using them to model contact dynamics or enable closed-loop control explicitly. In this paper, we present \textbf{OmniViTac}, a large-scale visuo-tactile-action dataset comprising $21{,}000+$ trajectories across $86$ tasks and $100+$ objects, organized into six physics-grounded interaction patterns. Building on this dataset, we propose \textbf{OmniVTA}, a world-model-based visuo-tactile manipulation framework that integrates four tightly coupled modules: a self-supervised tactile encoder, a two-stream visuo-tactile world model for predicting short-horizon contact evolution, a contact-aware fusion policy for action generation, and a 60Hz reflexive controller that corrects deviations between predicted and observed tactile signals in a closed loop. Real-robot experiments across all six interaction categories show that OmniVTA outperforms existing methods and generalizes well to unseen objects and geometric configurations, confirming the value of combining predictive contact modeling with high-frequency tactile feedback for contact-rich manipulation. All data, models, and code will be made publicly available on the project website at https://mrsecant.github.io/OmniVTA.

</details>


### [67] [NavTrust: Benchmarking Trustworthiness for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19229)
*Huaide Jiang,Yash Chaudhary,Yuping Wang,Zehao Wang,Raghav Sharma,Manan Mehta,Yang Zhou,Lichao Sun,Zhiwen Fan,Zhengzhong Tu,Jiachen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NavTrust是首个统一评估具身导航系统在真实世界损坏条件下鲁棒性的基准，系统性地对RGB、深度和指令输入进行现实损坏，揭示了现有方法的严重性能下降并提出了缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身导航研究主要在理想条件下评估模型性能，忽略了真实世界中可能出现的各种输入损坏（如传感器噪声、光照变化、指令歧义等），缺乏对这些现实损坏条件下系统鲁棒性的系统性评估。

Method: 提出了NavTrust基准，在统一框架下系统性地对RGB、深度和指令三种输入模态施加现实损坏，包括传感器噪声、光照变化、遮挡、语言变异等。评估了七种最先进的导航方法，并系统性地测试了四种不同的鲁棒性增强策略。

Result: 评估显示所有七种方法在现实损坏条件下都出现显著性能下降，揭示了现有方法的严重鲁棒性缺陷。通过缓解策略，在真实移动机器人上部署的Uni-NaVid和ETPNav模型表现出对损坏的改进鲁棒性。

Conclusion: NavTrust基准填补了具身导航鲁棒性评估的空白，揭示了现有方法的脆弱性，为开发更可靠的具身导航系统提供了路线图，并展示了通过适当策略可以提升系统对现实损坏的鲁棒性。

Abstract: There are two major categories of embodied navigation: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN), where agents navigate by following natural language instructions; and Object-Goal Navigation (OGN), where agents navigate to a specified target object. However, existing work primarily evaluates model performance under nominal conditions, overlooking the potential corruptions that arise in real-world settings. To address this gap, we present NavTrust, a unified benchmark that systematically corrupts input modalities, including RGB, depth, and instructions, in realistic scenarios and evaluates their impact on navigation performance. To our best knowledge, NavTrust is the first benchmark that exposes embodied navigation agents to diverse RGB-Depth corruptions and instruction variations in a unified framework. Our extensive evaluation of seven state-of-the-art approaches reveals substantial performance degradation under realistic corruptions, which highlights critical robustness gaps and provides a roadmap toward more trustworthy embodied navigation systems. Furthermore, we systematically evaluate four distinct mitigation strategies to enhance robustness against RGB-Depth and instructions corruptions. Our base models include Uni-NaVid and ETPNav. We deployed them on a real mobile robot and observed improved robustness to corruptions. The project website is: https://navtrust.github.io.

</details>


### [68] [Not All Features Are Created Equal: A Mechanistic Study of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19233)
*Bryce Grant,Xijia Zhao,Peng Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过激活注入、稀疏自编码器和线性探针分析6个VLA模型，发现视觉通路主导动作生成，语言敏感性取决于任务结构而非模型设计，专家通路编码运动程序而VLM通路编码目标语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型将感知、语言和运动控制整合到单一架构中，但人们对它们如何将多模态输入转化为动作的理解仍然有限。本研究旨在揭示VLA模型内部表示的工作机制。

Method: 使用激活注入、稀疏自编码器（SAEs）和线性探针分析6个参数规模从80M到7B的模型，在4个基准测试上进行了超过394,000次推演。研究了视觉通路和语言通路在不同任务结构下的作用，以及专家通路和VLM通路的分离表示。

Result: 视觉通路在所有架构中都主导动作生成；语言敏感性取决于任务结构而非模型设计；专家通路编码运动程序，VLM通路编码目标语义；稀疏自编码器处理对大多数架构的动作保真度至关重要；识别出82+个操作概念，因果消融显示敏感性范围在28-92%之间。

Conclusion: VLA模型的动作生成主要由视觉通路驱动，语言的作用取决于任务结构。模型内部存在分离的表示空间：专家通路编码具体的运动程序，VLM通路编码抽象的目标语义。这些发现为理解VLA模型的工作机制提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models combine perception, language, and motor control in a single architecture, yet how they translate multimodal inputs into actions remains poorly understood. We apply activation injection, sparse autoencoders (SAEs), and linear probes to six models spanning 80M--7B parameters across 394,000+ rollout episodes on four benchmarks. The visual pathway dominates action generation across all architectures: injecting baseline activations into null-prompt episodes recovers near-identical behavior, while cross-task injection steers robots toward source-task positions (99.8\% of X-VLA episodes align with the source trajectory), exposing spatially bound motor programs tied to scene coordinates rather than abstract task representations. Language sensitivity depends on task structure, not model design: when visual context uniquely specifies the task, language is ignored; when multiple goals share a scene, language becomes essential (X-VLA \texttt{libero\_goal}: 94\%$\to$10\% under wrong prompts vs.\ \texttt{libero\_object}: 60--100\% regardless). In all three multi-pathway architectures (\pizhalf{}, SmolVLA, GR00T), expert pathways encode motor programs while VLM pathways encode goal semantics ($2\times$ greater behavioral displacement from expert injection), and subspace injection confirms these occupy separable activation subspaces. Per-token SAE processing is essential for action fidelity on most architectures, though mean-pooling improves fidelity on X-VLA. Contrastive identification recovers 82+ manipulation concepts, and causal ablation reveals sensitivity spanning 28--92\% zero-effect rates independent of representation width. We release \textbf{Action Atlas} (https://action-atlas.com) for interactive exploration of VLA representations across all six models.

</details>

### [69] [Probabilistic multivariate statistical process control via kernel parameter uncertainty propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19055)
*Zina-Sabrina Duma,Victoria Jorry,Ayesha Safraz,Maria Paola di Crosta,Tuomas Sihvonen,Lassi Roininen,Satu-Pia Reinikainen*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 提出概率核多元统计过程控制框架，量化并传播核参数不确定性到监控统计量，通过贝叶斯推断和MCMC处理核参数不确定性，在田纳西伊斯曼过程上验证效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统K-MSPC中核参数通常通过启发式或确定性优化选择并视为固定值，但实际从有限噪声数据推断而来，这会导致控制限过于自信和报警行为不稳定。需要量化核参数不确定性对监控统计量的影响。

Method: 采用两阶段工作流：1) 使用监督或非监督模型进行确定性核校准；2) 通过马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛进行核参数的贝叶斯推断。将后验样本通过核主成分分析传播，生成概率性T²和平方预测误差控制图，以及不确定性感知贡献图。

Result: 在田纳西伊斯曼过程基准测试中，平方指数核的后验均值监控通常比确定性先验均值图有更好的故障检测效果；控制状态下可信带保持较窄，故障状态下变宽，反映异常状态下认知不确定性的放大。自动相关性确定核减少后验不确定性，性能接近确定性基线；非监督校准产生更宽后验带但仍保持稳健故障检测。

Conclusion: 提出的概率K-MSPC框架成功量化并传播核参数不确定性，提供更可靠的监控统计量和不确定性感知控制图，在核选择不确定时提高监控系统的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Kernel-based multivariate statistical process control (K-MSPC) extends classical monitoring to nonlinear industrial processes. Its performance depends critically on kernel parameters such as lengthscales and variance terms. In current practice these parameters are typically selected by heuristics or deterministic optimisation, and then treated as fixed, despite being inferred from finite and noisy data. This can lead to overconfident control limits and unstable alarm behaviour when the kernel choice is uncertain. This work proposes a probabilistic K-MSPC framework that quantifies and propagates kernel parameter uncertainty to the monitoring statistics. The approach follows a two-stage workflow: (i) deterministic kernel calibration using supervised or unsupervised models, and (ii) Bayesian inference of kernel parameters via Markov chain Monte Carlo. Posterior samples are propagated through kernel Principal Component Analysis to produce probabilistic $T^2$ and squarred prediction error control charts, together with uncertainty-aware contribution plots. The framework is evaluated on the Tennessee Eastman Process benchmark. Results show that posterior-mean monitoring often improves fault detection compared to deterministic prior-mean charts for the squared exponential kernel, while credible bands remain narrow in-control and widen under faults, reflecting amplified epistemic uncertainty in abnormal regimes. The automatic relevance determination kernel reduces posterior uncertainty and yields performance close to the deterministic baseline, whereas unsupervised calibration produces wider posterior bands but still robust fault detection.

</details>


### [70] [The Uncertain Policy Price of Scaling Direct Air Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19143)
*Leonardo Chiani,Pietro Andreoni,Laurent Drouet,Tobias Schmidt,Katrin Sievert,Bjerne Steffen,Massimo Tavoni*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: DACCS部署呈现厚尾分布：多数情景下技术采用有限，但有4-6%概率在2050年前实现十亿吨级碳移除；达到此规模需持续数十年、每吨200-330美元以上的补贴，总成本达9000-30000亿美元，且需配合强减排政策才能实现经济可持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DACCS作为有前景的CO2移除技术，其大规模部署仍具推测性，但技术、经济和政策相关的不确定性在减排路径中常被忽视。本文旨在首次对DACCS进行不确定性量化分析，评估技术、市场、金融和公共支持驱动因素的影响。

Method: 使用详细过程的综合评估模型和新开发的敏感性算法，对DACCS进行首次不确定性量化和全局敏感性分析，涵盖技术、市场、金融和公共支持等多维度驱动因素。

Result: DACCS部署呈现厚尾分布：多数情景显示技术采用有限，但有4-6%概率在2050年前实现十亿吨级碳移除；达到此规模需持续数十年、每吨200-330美元以上的补贴，总公共支持计划成本达9000-30000亿美元；该投资到2050年可收回，但前提是配合强减排政策。

Conclusion: 气候政策在实现稳健且经济可持续的CO2移除策略中发挥关键作用；DACCS的大规模部署需要长期、高额的公共支持，且必须与强减排政策相结合才能实现经济回报和环境效益。

Abstract: Direct air carbon capture and storage (DACCS) is a promising CO2 removal technology, but its deployment at scale remains speculative. Yet, its technological, economic, and policy-related uncertainties have often been overlooked in mitigation pathways. This paper conducts the first uncertainty quantification and global sensitivity analysis of DACCS on technological, market, financial and public support drivers, using a detailed-process Integrated Assessment Model and newly developed sensitivity algorithms. We find that DACCS deployment exhibits a fat-tailed distribution: most scenarios show modest technology uptake, but there is a small but non-zero probability (4-6%) of achieving gigaton-scale removals by mid-century. Scaling DACCS to gigaton levels requires subsidies that always exceed 200-330 USD/tCO2 and are sustained for decades, resulting in a public support programme of 900-3000 USD Billions. Such an effort pays back by mid-century, but only if accompanied by strong emission reduction policies. These findings highlight the critical role of climate policies in enabling a robust and economically sustainable CO2 removal strategy.

</details>

### [71] [Genetic determinism of circadian rhythm of feed intake and relation with feed efficiency evaluated in group-housed growing Large White pigs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18081)
*Lucile Riaboff,Ingrid David*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本研究首次探索了群养自由采食生长猪在育肥阶段采食量昼夜节律的遗传特性，提出了三个反映昼夜节律一致性和建立早晚的新性状，并估计了其遗传力及其与剩余采食量的遗传相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知动物昼夜节律与代谢优化密切相关，但从未研究过群养自由采食生长猪在育肥阶段采食量昼夜节律的遗传决定机制。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探索昼夜节律性状的遗传特性及其与饲料效率的关系。

Method: 对2,297头大白猪的电子饲喂站数据进行时频分析，提出三个反映昼夜节律的新性状：DayCR（昼夜节律一致性）、IndexCR和gCR（节律建立早晚）。使用多性状模型估计这些性状的遗传力及其与剩余采食量的遗传相关性。

Result: 三个昼夜节律性状显示中等遗传力（0.24-0.35），剩余采食量遗传力较高（0.41）。昼夜节律性状间遗传相关性很高（0.87-0.98），表明在育肥期多数天数（特别是后期）表现出24小时周期性的猪更早建立昼夜节律。昼夜节律性状与剩余采食量呈低但有利的遗传相关性（0.18-0.27），表明具有一致且早期24小时周期性的猪饲料效率更高。

Conclusion: 时频分析可有效检测生长猪育肥期间采食模式的24小时周期性。昼夜节律建立的变异性部分受遗传驱动，支持对昼夜节律性状进行遗传选择的可行性。选择具有早期且一致24小时采食周期性的猪可促进代谢稳态，从而提高动物性能和抗逆性。

Abstract: Background Genetic parameters of feeding behaviours traits from electronic feeding stations in relation to feed efficiency have been widely explored. However, genetic determinism of the circadian rhythm of feed intake throughout the fattening phase in group-housed growing pigs fed ad libitum has never been investigated, despite the well-known relationships between animals' circadian rhythms and the optimization of their metabolism. The objective of this study was to (i) propose three new traits derived from time-frequency approach applied to electronic feeding data from 2,297 Large White pigs that reflect the consistency of circadian feed intake rhythm throughout fattening (so called DayCR) and the precocity of its establishment (so called IndexCR and gCR), and then to (ii) estimate the heritability of those traits and their genetic correlations with residual feed intake using a multiple trait model. Results Results highlighted moderate heritability estimates for the three circadian traits (range h2: [0.24; 0.35]) and high heritability for residual feed intake (0.41). High genetic correlations (range of absolute values: [0.87; 0.98]) among circadian traits suggested that pigs exhibiting a 24-hour periodicity in feed intake on most days of fattening, particularly during the final fattening period, establish their circadian rhythm earlier than the other pigs. The low (range of absolute values: [0.18; 0.27]) but favourable genetic correlations between residual feed intake and circadian traits revealed that animals with a consistent and early 24-hour periodicity of feed intake also tend to be more feed efficient. Conclusions This study proposed to apply time-frequency analysis on longitudinal feeding data to detect 24-hour periodicities in the hourly feed intake pattern across days throughout fattening in growing-pigs. Results suggested that part of the variability observed in the establishment of circadian rhythm is genetically driven, further supporting the feasibility of genetic selection on circadian traits. Considering the well-established biological mechanisms underlying circadian feeding rhythm, selecting animals for their ability to exhibit an early and consistent 24-hour periodicity of feed intake could promote metabolic homeostasis, thereby enhancing animal performance and resilience.

</details>


### [72] [Interplay between evolutionary and epidemic time scales challenges the outcome of control policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18801)
*Santiago Lamata-Otín,Alex Arenas,Jesús Gómez-Gardeñes,David Soriano-Paños*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 该研究提出了SIR模型的扩展版本，允许传染性进化，揭示了病毒进化如何导致疫情超指数增长、突变性转变，并削弱控制措施效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典SIR模型假设病原体特性固定，忽略了病毒进化对流行病动态的影响。需要研究病毒进化如何改变疫情特征和控制策略效果。

Method: 提出了SIR模型的最小扩展，允许传染性进化。推导了控制该行为的临界突变率和干预时间的解析表达式。

Result: 病毒进化可导致疫情早期超指数增长、产生突变性转变，并削弱控制政策效果。过早解除干预可能导致比不采取行动更糟的疫情情景。发现了控制策略间的不对称性：缩短传染期仅阻碍传播而不抑制病毒进化，而降低传播既减少病例又减缓病毒进化。

Conclusion: 病毒进化显著改变了流行病动态，使控制策略更加复杂。需要考虑病毒进化因素来设计有效的流行病控制政策，特别是干预时机和策略选择对进化轨迹的影响。

Abstract: The SIR model is the cornerstone model for mathematical epidemiology, explaining key epidemic features such as the second-order transition between disease-free and epidemic states, the initial exponential growth of outbreaks or the short-term benefits of control measures. Nonetheless, the classical SIR model assumes that pathogen traits remain fixed, thus neglecting viral evolution. Here we propose a minimal extension of the SIR model, allowing infectiousness to evolve. We show that such evolution can cause superexponential early growth of outbreaks, create abrupt epidemic transitions, and undermine the effectiveness of control policies, as lifting interventions too early can lead to worse epidemic scenarios than no action. We derive analytical expressions for the critical mutation rate and intervention time governing this behavior, and identify a strong asymmetry between control strategies: while shortening the infectious period hinders transmission without suppressing viral evolution, lowering transmission both reduces cases and slows down viral evolution.

</details>

### [73] [Classifying submodules over monoidal categories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18264)
*Hadi Salmasian,Alistair Savage,Yaolong Shen*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文研究了单子范畴上模范畴子模的分类，扩展了Coulembier关于单子范畴中张量理想分类的思想，建立了子模分类框架，并应用于偏斜绞线范畴作为定向绞线范畴的模范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展Coulembier关于单子范畴中张量理想分类的工作，研究更一般的模范畴子模分类问题，特别是那些具有扭曲柱面扭转结构的模范畴，这类结构与扭曲反射方程和量子对称对密切相关。

Method: 开发了一个适用于具有扭曲柱面扭转结构的模范畴的框架，建立了模范畴M的子模与路径代数模M(1,-)的子模之间的序保持双射，分析了该对应关系在幂等完备化和分拆Grothendieck群下的行为。

Result: 在温和假设下，建立了模范畴子模与路径代数模子模之间的对应关系，该对应与幂等完备化兼容，并给出了通过不可分解对象进行分类的判据。应用方面，完整分类了偏斜绞线范畴作为定向绞线范畴模范畴的所有子模。

Conclusion: 该工作为具有扭曲柱面扭转结构的模范畴提供了系统的子模分类理论，扩展了单子范畴中张量理想分类的框架，并在绞线理论的具体应用中展示了该理论的有效性，为相关代数结构的研究提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study the classification of submodules of module categories over monoidal categories, extending ideas of Coulembier on the classification of tensor ideals in monoidal categories. We develop a framework that applies to module categories equipped with a twisted cylinder twist, a structure closely related to the twisted reflection equation and quantum symmetric pairs. Under mild assumptions, we establish an order-preserving bijection between submodules of a module category $\mathcal{M}$ and submodules of the path-algebra module $\mathcal{M}(1,-)$. We show that this correspondence is compatible with idempotent completion and analyze its behavior under decategorification to the split Grothendieck group, giving criteria for classification in terms of indecomposable objects. As an application, we study the disoriented skein category as a module category over the oriented skein category, describe its indecomposable objects, and obtain a complete classification of its submodules.

</details>


### [74] [Silting theory and derived base change](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18790)
*Riku Fushimi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该论文将Koenig-Yang关于倾斜复形与简单心集合之间的双射从域上的有限维代数推广到交换完全局部诺特环上的代数


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Koenig和Yang在域上的有限维代数中建立了倾斜复形与有界导出范畴中简单心集合之间的双射，许多作者在各种设置中对此做出了进一步贡献。本文旨在将这一结果推广到更一般的交换完全局部诺特环上的代数

Method: 在交换完全局部诺特环$(R,\m,k)$上工作，而不是在域上工作，并在此更一般的设置中建立双射。利用这一推广，构造了诺特$R$-代数$Λ$上的倾斜复形与$Λ\ten^\LL_RS$上的倾斜复形之间的双射，其中$(R,\m,k)\to(S,\n,k)$是交换完全局部诺特环的任意态射

Result: 成功建立了交换完全局部诺特环上代数中倾斜复形与简单心集合之间的双射。作为应用，构造了诺特$R$-代数$Λ$上的倾斜复形与$Λ\ten^\LL_RS$上的倾斜复形之间的双射，推广了诺特代数上倾斜复形的一些已知结果

Conclusion: 该研究将Koenig-Yang双射从域上的有限维代数推广到交换完全局部诺特环上的代数，为倾斜复形理论提供了更一般的框架，并获得了关于倾斜复形在环扩张下行为的重要应用

Abstract: For finite-dimensional algebras over a field, Koenig and Yang established a bijection between silting complexes and simple-minded collections in the bounded derived category, with further contributions by many authors in various settings. In this paper, we work over a commutative complete local noetherian ring $(R,\m,k)$ rather than over a field and establish a bijection in this more general setting.
  As an application of this generalization, we construct a bijection between silting complexes over a noetherian $R$-algebra $Λ$ and silting complexes over $Λ\ten^\LL_RS$ for any morphism of commutative complete local noetherian rings $(R,\m,k)\to(S,\n,k)$. This result generalizes some known results on silting complexes over noetherian algebras.

</details>


### [75] [Pascal, Catalan, Motzkin triangles and tensor product multiplicities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19069)
*L. Poulain d'Andecy*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文通过sl_2表示论视角，对Catalan三角形和Motzkin三角形及其推广进行初等讨论，揭示这些整数三角形可通过Pascal规则从特定初始条件生成，并解释其中数字作为sl_2表示张量积中重数出现的现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在从初等角度探讨Catalan三角形和Motzkin三角形等整数三角形，特别关注它们与sl_2表示论的联系。作者希望展示这些三角形可以通过经典的Pascal规则从精心选择的初始条件生成，并解释其中数字作为表示论中重数出现的现象。

Method: 采用初等方法分析整数三角形，通过Pascal规则从特定初始条件构造Catalan三角形和Motzkin三角形及其推广。利用sl_2表示论框架，证明这些三角形中的数字对应于sl_2表示张量积中的重数，并表示为广义二项式系数的差。同时通过表示论视角讨论这些三角形中的"平方和"现象。

Result: 成功建立了整数三角形与sl_2表示论之间的明确联系，证明Catalan三角形和Motzkin三角形中的数字确实作为sl_2表示张量积中的重数出现。这些数字可以表示为广义二项式系数的差，并通过表示论框架解释了三角形中的平方和现象。

Conclusion: 本文提供了整数三角形与sl_2表示论之间联系的初等阐述，展示了如何通过Pascal规则和精心选择的初始条件生成这些三角形，并成功解释了其中数字作为表示论重数的现象。这种联系不仅加深了对整数三角形的理解，也为表示论提供了具体的组合实现。

Abstract: The main purpose of this note is to provide an elementary discussion of some simple triangles of integer numbers in particular through their connections with representation theory of $sl_2$. The triangles under consideration are the Catalan triangle and the Motzkin triangle together with their generalisations that we introduce here. We advocate the point of view that these triangles are given by the well-known and classical Pascal rule starting from a well-chosen initial condition. We give an elementary derivation of the fact that the numbers in these triangles are multiplicities appearing in tensor products of $sl_2$-representations and that they are simply expressed as a difference of generalised binomial coefficients. We also take the opportunity to discuss the ``sum of squares'' phenomenon that happens in these triangles through the lense of representation theory.

</details>


### [76] [The variety of Lie algebra representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19123)
*Bruna Mariana Braido da Silva Percinotti*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 研究李代数表示仿射簇的能量泛函临界点结构，证明半单对全局最小化轨道能量，获得半单同态刚性新证明和Mostow定理新证法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究任意n维李代数到固定实半单李代数的所有同态构成的仿射簇，利用实几何不变量理论分析其结构，特别是能量泛函的临界点

Method: 使用实几何不变量理论，通过GL(n,ℝ)×Inn(𝔤)作用引入自然矩映射和能量泛函，分析能量泛函临界点结构

Result: 证明每个半单对（半单李代数的表示）在其轨道中全局最小化能量，获得半单同态刚性的初等证明，以及半单子代数相容Cartan对合存在的Mostow定理新证明

Conclusion: 通过能量最小化方法为李代数表示簇提供了新的几何视角，建立了半单表示的特殊性质，并获得了经典结果的新证明

Abstract: We study the affine variety $L_{n}(\mathfrak{g})$ of Lie algebra representations, the collection of all homomorphisms from an arbitrary $n$-dimensional Lie algebra into a fixed real semi-simple Lie algebra $\mathfrak{g}$. Using techniques from real Geometric Invariant Theory, we equip this variety with a natural moment map and associated energy functional arising from the action of the real reductive group $GL(n,\mathbb{R}) \times \text{Inn}(\mathfrak{g})$.
  We analyze the critical points of the energy functional and describe their structure. In particular, we prove that every semi-simple pair, that is representations of semi-simple Lie algebras, will globally minimize the energy in its orbit. As consequences, we obtain an elementary proof of the rigidity of semi-simple homomorphisms and derive a new proof of the Mostow theorem on the existence of compatible Cartan involutions for semi-simple subalgebras. Subsequent results concerning the structure of critical points of higher energy are also obtained.

</details>

### [77] [Fluxes of Generic Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals with a Spinning Secondary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18075)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端质量比旋进系统是未来空间引力波观测站的关键目标，将次级天体的自旋纳入波形模型不仅能提高测量精度，还能深入了解恒星质量天体的自旋分布。

Method: 在Kerr背景时空下，采用线性自旋近似，使用辐射处方（半推迟场减半超前场）推导了轨道平均演化方程，包括能量、角动量、类Carter常数和平行自旋分量的演化。

Result: 构建了包含次级天体自旋的极端质量比旋进系统的通量和波形模型，提供了一个可处理的框架来生成包含次级自旋的波形。

Conclusion: 该框架为生成包含次级自旋的波形提供了可行途径，并具有在未来工作中进一步简化的潜力。

Abstract: Extreme mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs), comprising a stellar-mass compact object (CO) orbiting a supermassive black hole (BH), are key targets for future space-based gravitational-wave (GW) observatories. Incorporating the spin of the secondary body into waveform models not only enhances measurement precision but also offers insight into the spin distribution of stellar-mass objects. In this work, we construct the flux and waveform for an EMRI with a spinning secondary in a Kerr background under the linear-spin approximation. Using the radiative prescription (half-retarded minus half-advanced field), we derive orbit-averaged evolution equations for the fundamental constants of motion, including the energy, angular momentum, Carter-like constant, and the parallel spin component. This framework provides a tractable route to generating waveforms that incorporate the secondary spin, with the potential for further simplification in future work.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究快速射电暴FRB 20240114A中上漂移爆发簇的漂移率分布特征，探索是否存在不同的物理发射机制或空间区域。

Conclusion: 漂移率分布的双峰结构表明可能存在两个空间分离的磁层发射区域，各自产生具有不同物理特性的上漂移爆发簇，但需要更多观测数据来确认这一假设。

Abstract: We report the discovery of bimodal structure in the drift rate distribution of upward-drifting burst clusters from the hyperactive repeating fast radio burst FRB 20240114A. Using unsupervised machine learning (UMAP dimensionality reduction combined with HDBSCAN density-based clustering) applied to 233 upward-drifting burst clusters from the FAST telescope dataset, we identify a distinct subpopulation of 45 burst clusters (Cluster C1) with mean drift rates 2.5x higher than typical upward-drifting burst clusters (245.6 vs 98.1 MHz/ms). Gaussian mixture modeling reveals strong evidence for bimodality (delta-BIC = 296.6), with clearly separated modes (Ashman's D = 2.70 > 2) and a statistically significant gap in the distribution (11.3 sigma). Crucially, we demonstrate that this bimodality persists when restricting the analysis to single-component (U1) burst clusters only (delta-BIC = 19.9, Ashman's D = 2.71), confirming that the result is not an artifact of combining single- and multi-component burst clusters with different drift rate definitions. The extreme-drift subpopulation also exhibits systematically lower peak frequencies (-7%), shorter durations (-29%), and distinct clustering in multi-dimensional feature space. These findings are suggestive of two spatially separated emission regions in the magnetosphere, each producing upward-drifting burst clusters with distinct physical characteristics, although confirmation requires observations from additional epochs and sources.

</details>


### [79] [Probing Kerr black hole in a uniform Bertotti-Robinson magnetic field through astrophysical quasi-periodic oscillations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18129)
*Hamza Rehman,Sanjar Shaymatov,Saddam Hussain,Tao Zhu*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 研究在均匀Bertotti-Robinson磁场中的Kerr黑洞周围高频准周期振荡行为，通过贝叶斯推断和MCMC分析约束黑洞参数，发现磁场参数虽小但不可忽略

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 推导Kerr黑洞在均匀磁场中的测地线方程，计算轨道、径向和垂直回旋频率，建立基于参数共振和强迫共振机制的QPO理论模型，使用贝叶斯推断和MCMC分析处理多个黑洞X射线双星的观测数据

Result: 对于GRO J1655-40、GRS 1915+105、H1743-322和M82~X-1，在参数共振模型中获得了非零的磁场参数b（68%置信水平），而其他源只获得90%置信水平的上限；在强迫共振模型中，所有源都只获得90%置信区间的磁场参数上限

Conclusion: 磁场参数虽小但不可忽略，对粒子动力学和回旋频率产生轻微修正，磁场影响还体现在最内稳定圆轨道性质和薄吸积盘的辐射特性上

Abstract: In this study, the behavior of high-frequency quasi-periodic oscillations (QPOs) is investigated around a Kerr black hole immersed in a uniform Bertotti-Robinson magnetic field. The motion of the test particle is analyzed by determining the geodesic equations and evaluating the corresponding orbital, radial, and vertical epicyclic frequencies. These fundamental frequencies are used to construct the theoretical framework of QPO models based on parametric and forced resonance mechanisms. Observational data obtained from several black hole X-ray binaries (GRO J1655-40, XTE J1550-564, XTE J1859+226, GRS 1915+105, H1743-322, M82~X-1, and Sgr~A$^{*}$) are used to constrain the black hole parameters through Bayesian inference and Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) analyses. For the X-ray binaries GRO J1655-40, GRS 1915+105, H1743-322, and M82~X-1, nonzero values of the dimensionless parameter $b=Bm$ are obtained at the $68\%$ confidence level within the framework of the parametric resonance model, while only upper bounds at the $90\%$ confidence level are obtained for the remaining sources. In contrast, in the case of the forced resonance model, only an upper bound at the $90\%$ confidence interval is obtained for the magnetic field parameter for all considered X-ray binary sources. The analysis indicates that the value of the magnetic field parameter is small but not negligible, producing minor modifications to particle dynamics and epicyclic frequencies. The influence of the magnetic field is further examined through the properties of the innermost stable circular orbit and the radiative properties of the thin accretion disk, including the energy flux and temperature profiles, within the allowed parameter range inferred from the MCMC analysis.

</details>


### [80] [Spectral Hardness as the Primary Discriminator: Unveiling the Collapsar--Merger Boundary with a Gold-Standard Gamma-Ray Burst Sample](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18150)
*Xue Zhang,Yan-Kun Qu,Shuang-Xi Yi,Yu-Peng Yang,Fen Lyu,Fa-Yin Wang,Zhong-Xiao Man*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 基于24个有光谱确认前身星的GRB金标准样本，使用支持向量机(SVM)建立了区分并合与塌缩的物理分类方法，发现谱硬度和能量是主要区分特征而非持续时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上使用T90持续时间区分短暴(并合)和长暴(塌缩)存在重叠区域，需要更可靠的物理分类方法，特别是为高红移GRB宇宙学应用提供清洁样本。

Method: 使用SVM在24个有光谱确认前身星(超新星或千新星)的GRB金标准样本上训练，通过隔离主尖峰排除延展辐射污染，推导出定量分类指数I_SVM = 5.01 log_10 E_p,i - 1.25 log_10 E_iso - 0.34 log_10 T_90,z - 12.90。

Result: 分类器权重分析显示E_p,i的区分能力约为T_90,z的5倍，E_iso贡献与E_p,i相当，表明谱硬度和能量是主要物理特征。I_SVM>0分类为并合，成功识别历史案例如超长暴GRB 111209A(塌缩)和短暴GRB 050709(并合)。

Conclusion: 该方法为清理档案和高红移GRB样本提供了物理基础分类工具，支持精确宇宙学研究，并揭示了塌缩暴存在严格的硬度上限，而并合暴表现为相对于能量预算的异常硬度。

Abstract: In this Letter, we establish a robust, physically motivated classification method using a Support Vector Machine (SVM) trained on a "gold-standard" sample of 24 GRBs with spectroscopically confirmed progenitors (associated SNe or KNe). By isolating the prompt main spike to excise contamination from extended emission, we derive a quantitative classification index, I_SVM = 5.01 log_10 E_p,i - 1.25 log_10 E_iso - 0.34 log_10 T_90,z - 12.90 (units: keV, 10^52 erg, s). Events with I_SVM > 0 are classified as mergers. Analysis of the standardized classification weights reveals that the discriminative power of E_p,i is approximately 5 times that of T_90,z, while E_iso contributes a weight comparable to E_p,i. This quantitatively demonstrates that spectral hardness and energetics, rather than duration, are the primary physical signatures distinguishing mergers from collapsars. The derived boundary implies a stringent hardness ceiling for collapsars, while mergers are identified as outliers with excessive hardness relative to their energy budget. The classifier successfully identifies the nature of historic test cases, including the ultra-long GRB 111209A (collapsar) and the short GRB 050709 (merger), independent of instrumental eras. This tool paves the way for cleaning archival and future high-redshift GRB samples for precision cosmology.

</details>

*S. Peluso,G. Lanzuisi,A. Comastri,M. Brusa,M. Giustini,G. Miniutti,S. Bianchi,V. E. Gianolli,R. Middei,P-O. Petrucci,L. Borrelli,E. Amenta,E. Bertola,B. De Marco,A. De Rosa,S. Kraemer,G. Kriss,Y. TL;DR: 通过X射线分析23个射电宁静AGN的日冕性质，发现高光度AGN具有更冷、光学厚度更大的日冕，高能截止能量与爱丁顿比和光度呈显著反相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高光度类星体中热日冕的性质，通过硬X射线波段发射分析，包括软X射线波段的适当处理，以了解不同光度AGN中日冕特性的差异。

Method: 使用来自XMM-Newton的高质量X射线光谱，辅以NuSTAR数据（在静止框架下延伸至30-40 keV），分析23个红移0.1<z<0.4、光度2×10^44<L_bol<2×10^46 erg/s的AGN样本。软X射线波段采用暖日冕模型拟合，硬X射线波段采用热日冕模型拟合。

Result: 软X射线波段最佳拟合为暖日冕模型，中值温度0.40 keV，光学深度τ=20-40。硬X射线波段热日冕模型中值高能截止为87 keV，低于塞弗特星系的典型值（100-200 keV），光学深度τ=1-5表明中等光学厚度日冕。高能截止能量与爱丁顿比和光度呈显著反相关，从低吸积率/光度时的250-300 keV降至高吸积率/光度时的90-100 keV。

Conclusion: 高光度AGN相比低光度塞弗特星系具有更冷、光学厚度更大的日冕，高能截止能量的降低与增强的日冕冷却（可能由正负电子对产生驱动）一致，支持日冕性质随吸积参数变化的物理模型。

Abstract: We present the X-ray analysis of coronal properties in a statistically representative sample of 23 mostly radio-quiet AGN from the SUBWAYS campaign (SUpermassive Black holes Winds in XrAYs), focusing on quasars at redshifts $0.1 < z < 0.4 $ and bolometric luminosities $2 \times 10^{44} <L_{bol}(erg/s) < 2 \times 10^{46}$. The main aim of this work is to investigate the properties of the hot corona through the study of the hard X-ray band emission, including a proper treatment of the soft X-ray band. High-quality X-ray spectra from XMM-Newton, complemented by NuSTAR data extending up to 30-40 keV in the rest frame, are available for this sample. The soft X-ray band (0.3-2 keV) spectrum is best fitted by a warm corona model with a median temperature of 0.40 keV, and an optical depth in the range $τ$=20 - 40, consistent with previous results on lower luminosity sources. The hard X-ray band is well described using a hot corona model, with a median high-energy cut-off of 87 keV, at the lower end of the distribution of typical values found in Seyfert galaxies (100 - 200 keV). The derived median value of the optical depth ($τ$ = 1 - 5) suggests the presence of a moderately optically thick corona. Combining the SUBWAYS results with literature samples at low and high redshift, we assemble the largest sample to date of AGN with E$_{cut}$ and accretion parameter measurements, finding a significant anticorrelation of E$_{cut}$ with both $λ_{Edd}$ and $L_{bol}$ with the median E$_{cut}$ decreasing from 250 - 300 keV at low accretion rates and luminosities to 90 - 100 keV at high accretion rates and luminosities - consistent with enhanced coronal cooling, possibly driven by pair-production. These results favor cooler, optically thicker coronae in luminous AGN compared to those in lower-luminosity Seyfert galaxies.

</details>


### [82] [Neutrinos and gamma rays from Seyfert galaxies constrain the properties of coronal turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18214)
