### [126] [On the equivalence of semi-discrete Active Flux and Discontinuous Galerkin methods and a comparison of their performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18160)
*Wasilij Barsukow,Christian Klingenberg,Simon Krotsch*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: Active Flux方法比DG方法更经济，在相同自由度下达到更高阶精度，但研究发现对于线性问题两者本质相同，存在自由度映射关系


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Active Flux方法与Discontinuous Galerkin方法之间的数学关系，解释为什么AF方法在相同自由度下能达到更高阶精度，以及DG方法的超收敛现象

Method: 通过建立AF和DG自由度之间的映射关系，证明对于线性问题（以及某种意义上的非线性问题），半离散的AF和DG是相同的方法。利用Radau多项式和其零点分析映射关系

Result: 发现AF和DG存在自由度映射关系，在此映射下两种方法的自由度更新完全一致。AF在相同误差下更经济，DG的超收敛现象可以通过AF作为背景高阶格式来自然理解

Conclusion: AF方法在相同自由度下比DG方法精度更高，但两者本质相关。Radau多项式的零点揭示了AF作为DG背后的高阶格式，这为理解DG的超收敛提供了自然框架

Abstract: The Active Flux (AF) method employs a globally continuous approximation, like continuous Finite Element methods. This is achieved through the placement of point values at cell interfaces which are shared between adjacent cells. With, on average, K+1 degrees of freedom per cell, Active Flux achieves a polynomial approximation of degree K+1, while the Discontinuous Galerkin (DG) method uses only polynomials of degree K, i.e. one degree less with the same number of degrees of freedom. Despite all the differences, in this paper we show, however, that for linear problems in one and several dimensions as well as -- in some sense -- for nonlinear ones, semi-discrete AF and DG are the same method. We identify a mapping between their respective degrees of freedom, upon which the updates of these degrees of freedom turn out to agree. On the one hand, AF therefore seems more economical then DG for a given value of the error, and we confirm this in numerical experiments. On the other hand, this is a way to understand superconvergence of DG in a natural way, and we show how Radau polynomials and their zeros appear in the mapping between DG and AF: In the Radau points, AF "shines through" as the background high-order scheme behind DG.

</details>


### [127] [Splitting-strategies for arbitrary-order fully mixed finite element discretizations of the Biot equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18241)
*Fleurianne Bertrand,Jakub Wiktor Both,Tugay Dağlı*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 论文研究了Biot方程的全混合公式，该公式具有流动与变形之间的对称耦合结构，允许使用稳定的混合有限元而不需要强兼容条件，通过弱强制弹性应力张量对称性来保持守恒结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Biot方程的全混合公式，利用其对称耦合结构，允许对每个子问题使用稳定的混合有限元，而不需要在两个子物理之间施加强兼容条件，同时保持两个子问题的守恒结构。

Method: 采用全混合有限元方法，其中弹性应力张量的对称性被弱强制实施。得到的混合公式具有鞍点结构，其稳定性由适当的inf-sup条件决定。研究了任意阶离散空间族的inf-sup稳定性，并针对全混合公式专门研究了基于经典固定应力分裂的迭代分裂策略，带有额外调优。

Result: 建立了多个离散空间族的inf-sup稳定性，得到了最优先验误差估计。证明了迭代分裂策略的收敛性，收敛速率取决于耦合强度。理论上证明了负稳定化的有效性，与Schur补思想一致。基于解析解和经典Mandel问题的数值结果支持了理论分析。

Conclusion: Biot方程的全混合公式具有对称耦合结构，允许灵活选择稳定的混合有限元而不需要强兼容条件。通过弱强制应力对称性，该方法保持了守恒结构，建立了inf-sup稳定性和最优误差估计。迭代分裂策略有效，负稳定化被证明有效，数值实验验证了理论结果。

Abstract: We study the fully mixed formulation of the Biot equations, which is characterized by a symmetric coupling between flow and deformation. This structure enables the use of stable mixed finite elements for each subproblem without a strong compatibility condition across the two subphysics. To exploit this flexibility while preserving the conservation structure of both subproblems, we consider fully mixed finite element methods in which the symmetry of the elastic stress tensor is enforced weakly. The resulting mixed formulation exhibits a saddle-point structure whose stability is determined by suitable inf--sup conditions. Inf--sup stability is established for several families of discrete spaces of arbitrary order, leading to optimal a priori error estimates. Iterative splitting strategies following the classical fixed-stress split with additional tuning are specifically investigated for the fully mixed formulation, with proof of convergence and rates depending on the coupling strength. Contrary to previous analyses on coupled problems with a symmetric structure, we theoretically prove the efficacy of negative stabilization, consistent with Schur-complement ideas. Numerical results based on analytical solutions and the classical Mandel problem support the theory.

</details>


### [128] [Christoffel Adaptive Sampling for Sparse Random Feature Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18251)
*Ben Adcock,Khiem Can,Xuemeng Wang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 将主动学习与稀疏随机特征展开相结合，利用Christoffel函数指导自适应采样，在数据稀缺场景下提高采样效率和近似精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机特征模型已成为近似多元函数和求解偏微分方程的有力工具，稀疏随机特征展开通过引入稀疏性进一步提升了传统随机特征模型的性能，特别适用于数据稀缺场景。然而，如何更有效地选择采样点以提高采样效率仍是一个重要问题。

Method: 将主动学习与稀疏随机特征近似相结合，利用Christoffel函数指导自适应采样过程。该方法动态选择信息量丰富的采样点，基于这些点对函数空间的贡献程度进行选择。通过利用稀疏恢复求解器迭代选择的基函数所关联的Christoffel函数，优化采样点的分布。

Result: 通过数值实验比较了自适应和非自适应采样策略在稀疏随机特征展开框架下的性能，检验了各种函数近似任务的准确性。结果表明，自适应采样在保持高精度的同时显著降低了采样复杂度。

Conclusion: 自适应采样方法在稀疏随机特征展开中具有明显优势，能够在减少样本复杂度的同时保持高精度，这对于数据获取成本较高的科学计算任务具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Random Feature Models (RFMs) have become a powerful tool for approximating multivariate functions and solving partial differential equations efficiently. Sparse Random Feature Expansions (SRFE) improve traditional RFMs by incorporating sparsity, making it particularly effective in data-scarce settings. In this work, we integrate active learning with sparse random feature approximations to improve sampling efficiency. Specifically, we incorporate the Christoffel function to guide an adaptive sampling process, dynamically selecting informative sample points based on their contribution to the function space. This approach optimizes the distribution of sample points by leveraging the Christoffel function associated with an iteratively-chosen basis obtained by the sparse recovery solver. We conduct numerical experiments comparing adaptive and nonadaptive sampling strategies with the SRFE framework and examine their accuracy for various function approximation tasks. Overall, our results demonstrate the advantages of adaptive sampling in maintaining high accuracy while reducing sample complexity for SRFE, highlighting its potential for scientific computing tasks where data is expensive to acquire.

</details>


### [129] [Convergence of entropy-stable continuous summation-by-parts discretizations of symmetric hyperbolic conservation laws](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18369)
*Zelalem Arega Worku,David C. Del Rey Fernández,David W. Zingg*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在光滑解假设下，熵稳定分裂形式离散化对具有齐次通量函数的标量和对称双曲系统的收敛性，突破了传统Lax等价定理对非线性问题的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对于非线性双曲偏微分方程，稳定性和一致性通常不能保证收敛性，特别是对于广泛使用的熵稳定离散化，目前缺乏一般性的收敛理论。现有收敛结果要么基于投影误差分解，要么基于线性化论证，无法直接扩展到熵稳定分裂形式离散化。

Method: 在连续求和分部(C-SBP)框架内，针对具有齐次通量函数的标量和对称双曲系统，采用稳定性-一致性论证方法。分析基于非线性误差演化不等式，其解提供了数值误差的显式上界。对于d维空间中p次C-SBP离散化，要求p>1+d/2。

Result: 证明了在足够小的网格间距下，对于满足p>1+d/2的C-SBP离散化，该误差上界在任何有限时间区间内保持有限，并随着网格细化趋于零，从而保证了收敛性，即使存在局部线性不稳定性。以标量无粘性Burgers方程作为典型示例。

Conclusion: 该工作为非线性双曲问题的熵稳定分裂形式离散化提供了收敛性证明，阐明了非线性双曲问题离散化中一致性、熵稳定性、非线性误差增长和收敛性之间的关系，填补了该领域的重要理论空白。

Abstract: The Lax equivalence theorem guarantees convergence of stable and consistent discretizations for linear hyperbolic partial differential equations (PDEs). For nonlinear problems, however, stability and consistency alone do not generally guarantee convergence, even for smooth solutions, and existing convergence results typically rely either on projection-based error decompositions or on linearization arguments that do not directly extend to entropy-stable split-form discretizations. In particular, general convergence results for entropy-stable discretizations of hyperbolic PDEs are currently lacking, despite their widespread use. In this work, we prove convergence under smoothness assumptions on the exact solution and fluxes for entropy-stable split-form discretizations of scalar and symmetric hyperbolic systems with homogeneous flux functions within the continuous summation-by-parts (C-SBP) framework. The scalar inviscid Burgers equation is presented as a canonical example. The analysis is based on a stability-consistency argument that yields a nonlinear error evolution inequality whose solution provides an explicit upper bound on the numerical error. We show that, for sufficiently small mesh spacing, and for degree-$p$ C-SBP discretizations in $d$ spatial dimensions with $p>1+d/2$, this bound remains finite on any finite time interval and tends to zero as the mesh is refined, implying convergence despite the presence of local linear instabilities. The results help clarify the relationship between consistency, entropy stability, nonlinear error growth, and convergence for discretizations of nonlinear hyperbolic problems.

</details>


### [130] [Wavelet-based grid adaptation with consistent treatment of high-order sharp immersed geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18403)
*Changxiao Nigel Shen,Wim M. van Rees*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种高阶插值小波变换自适应策略，兼容尖锐浸入边界和界面离散化，解决了传统方法在非网格对齐边界附近的一致性丢失问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于小波的自适应网格方法在求解偏微分方程时提供数学严谨的误差估计，但应用于具有浸入几何的PDE离散化时面临挑战：标准插值小波变换在非网格对齐边界交叉附近失去一致性。

Method: 提出高阶插值小波变换自适应策略，使用一维多项式外推技术在窄区间上执行一致的高阶小波变换。该技术结合边界值和导数，这些值通过类似于高阶浸入有限差分离散化中使用的多元插值多项式进行评估。

Result: 该方法能在任意光滑多维域（包括凹几何部分附近）保持小波阶数，在复杂域中实现网格自适应，并通过手动设置的细化阈值稳健地限制数值误差。算法在静态和动态问题（包括具有移动边界和时间自适应网格分辨率的Navier-Stokes方程）上得到验证。

Conclusion: 所提方法实现了有效的网格自适应，即使在具有复杂移动边界的问题中，也能在用户定义的细化阈值和整体解误差之间建立稳健、可预测的关系。

Abstract: Wavelet-based grid adaptation methods use multiresolution analysis for error estimation, offering a mathematically rigorous approach to adaptive grid refinement when solving Partial Differential Equations (PDEs). However, applying these methods to PDE discretizations with immersed geometries is challenging, as standard interpolating wavelet transforms lose consistency near non-grid-aligned boundary intersections. To address this, we propose a high-order interpolating wavelet transform adaptation strategy compatible with sharp immersed boundary and interface discretizations. The approach performs consistent high-order wavelet transforms on narrow intervals using a 1D polynomial extrapolation technique. To maintain high order, the technique incorporates boundary values and derivatives, which are evaluated from multivariate interpolating polynomials similar to those used in high order immersed finite difference discretizations. Consequently, the proposed approach maintains the wavelet order on any arbitrary smooth multidimensional domain, including near concave geometry sections. This approach enables grid adaptation in complex domains while robustly bounding the numerical error via a manually set refinement threshold. The algorithm's performance is validated on both static and dynamic problems, including the Navier-Stokes equations with moving boundaries and temporally adapting grid resolutions. The results demonstrate that the proposed method enables effective grid adaptation, establishing a robust, predictable relationship between a user-defined refinement threshold and the overall solution error, even for problems with complex, moving boundaries.

</details>


### [131] [Born-Series-Inspired Residual Metric for Learning-based Preconditioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18527)
*Juntao Wang,Xinliang Liu,Jiwei Jia*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出神经预条件Born级数(NPBS)方法，通过基于Riesz映射的残差度量优化学习型PDE预条件器，改善高频Helmholtz方程等不定算子的求解效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习型PDE预条件器使用未预条件的欧几里得残差范数作为损失函数，这种默认度量对于高频Helmholtz方程等不定算子过于敏感，而经典预条件方法通过重塑残差几何结构获得成功。需要将经典预条件思想融入学习框架

Method: 基于Born级数和移位Laplacian左预条件的关系，推导出参考Green算子G_η作为自然Riesz映射残差度量R_η=G_η*G_η。提出神经预条件Born级数(NPBS)迭代，用残差驱动的神经算子替换标量CBS松弛，并设计度量匹配的Born级数启发损失函数L_bs^{R_η}。框架支持FFT/DST/DCT实现O(NlogN)快速评估

Result: 在异质Helmholtz问题上进行数值实验，证明方法有效且随着系统病态程度增加优势更明显。框架扩展到其他PDE类，包括对流-扩散-反应方程和非线性PDE的线性化Newton系统，均获得显著的迭代次数减少

Conclusion: 通过将经典预条件思想与学习型方法结合，提出基于Riesz映射残差度量的神经预条件Born级数框架，显著改善不定PDE算子的求解效率，为学习型预条件器设计提供了新的度量视角

Abstract: Loss functions for learning-based PDE preconditioners implicitly choose a \emph{metric} in which residuals are matched, yet most approaches still optimize an unpreconditioned Euclidean residual norm. For indefinite operators such as the high-frequency Helmholtz equation, this default metric can make both learning and iterative correction overly sensitive to near-resonant spectral components, while classical preconditioning succeeds precisely by reshaping the residual geometry. We show that the Born Series and shifted-Laplacian left preconditioning are linked by the identity $
I-G_ηV_η= G_ηA = L_η^{-1}A, $
which turns the reference Green operator $G_η$ into a natural Riesz-map residual metric $
R_η= G_η^\ast G_η$
and suggests measuring the physical residual via $
\|r\|_{R_η}=\|G_ηr\|_2. $
Building on this viewpoint, we propose a \emph{Neural Preconditioned Born Series} (NPBS) iteration that replaces the scalar CBS relaxation with a residual-driven neural operator, together with a metric-matched Born-series-inspired loss $\mathcal{L}_{\mathrm{bs}}^{R_η}$. The framework is architecture-agnostic and supports fast $\mathcal{O}(N\log N)$ evaluation via FFT/DST/DCT. Numerical experiments on heterogeneous Helmholtz problems demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, and its advantage becomes more pronounced as the systems grow more ill-conditioned; we then extend the framework to other PDE classes, including convection--diffusion--reaction equations and linearized Newton systems for nonlinear PDEs, where it also yields substantial iteration reductions.

</details>


### [132] [A stable and fast method for solving multibody scattering problems via the method of fundamental solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19113)
*Yunhui Cai,Joar Bagge,Per-Gunnar Martinsson*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种求解声学多体散射问题的数值方法，通过局部计算每个散射体的高精度散射算子，然后组合成全局线性系统，该系统具有良好的条件数且适用于迭代求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多体声学散射问题中传统方法难以处理大量散射体、计算复杂度高的问题，特别是当使用简单但可能病态的方法（如基本解法）时，需要保持全局求解器的可扩展性和数值稳定性。

Method: 首先为每个散射体通过局部计算构建高精度散射矩阵，然后组合这些散射矩阵形成全局线性系统。该方法允许使用可能病态的基本解法（MFS）构建局部散射矩阵，同时通过全局系统设计保持数值稳定性。系统可通过快速多极子方法等加速矩阵向量乘法。

Result: 该方法产生的系数矩阵具有良好的条件数，即使对于包含大量散射体的问题也是如此。线性系统适用于迭代求解器，并能通过快速算法有效加速。算法实现简单，因为MFS比基于Nyström或Galerkin离散化边界积分方程的方法更易实现。

Conclusion: 该方法为求解二维和三维声学多体散射问题提供了一种简单、可扩展且数值稳定的数值方法，能够有效处理大量散射体，同时允许使用实现简单的局部计算方法。

Abstract: The paper describes a numerical method for solving acoustic multibody scattering problems in two and three dimensions. The idea is to compute a highly accurate approximation to the scattering operator for each body through a local computation, and then use these scattering matrices to form a global linear system. The resulting coefficient matrix is relatively well-conditioned, even for problems involving a very large number of scatterers. The linear system is amenable to iterative solvers, and can readily be accelerated via fast algorithms for the matrix-vector multiplication such as the fast multipole method. The key point of the work is that the local scattering matrices can be constructed using potentially ill-conditioned techniques such as the method of fundamental solutions (MFS), while still maintaining scalability and numerical stability of the global solver. The resulting algorithm is simple, as the MFS is far simpler to implement than alternative techniques based on discretizing boundary integral equations using Nyström or Galerkin.

</details>


### [133] [Reconstructions of Single Pixel X-Ray Transforms with Applications in Nuclear-Disarmament Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18728)
*Christopher Fichtlscherer,R. Scott Kemp,Christina Brandt*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文研究了单像素X射线变换的数学特性及其在核武器验证中的应用，提出了一种基于Douglas-Rachford分裂和全变差去噪的重建方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在核军控和裁军过程中，需要在不泄露敏感信息的情况下确定物体是否为核武器。现有的单像素X射线变换方法在数学特性分析和物体重建方面研究有限。

Method: 1. 分析单像素X射线变换的数学特性（非线性、连续性、Fréchet可微性、凸性）；2. 提出基于Douglas-Rachford分裂和全变差去噪的重建方法；3. 针对旋转对称物体实现一维直接全变差去噪算法。

Result: 证明了单像素X射线变换具有非线性、连续性、Fréchet可微性和凸性等数学特性，并成功实现了基于有限数量含噪测量值的物体重建方法。

Conclusion: 单像素X射线变换在核验证中具有重要应用价值，提出的重建方法为基于有限测量值的物体识别提供了有效解决方案，特别适用于旋转对称物体的验证场景。

Abstract: In nuclear arms control and disarmament processes, it is crucial to determine whether an object is a nuclear weapon or not without revealing sensitive information about it. At the MIT: Laboratory for Nuclear Security and Policy, such a nuclear verification method was developed, showcasing a transmission-based approach [1]. This method's essential part rests on a mathematical operation, the Single-Pixel X-Ray Transform: a cone of X-rays transmits an object and the remaining intensity is measured with a single-pixel detector. This transformation and the recovery of objects from dimensionless single-pixel measurements more generally has only been analyzed to a limited extent. In this work, we investigate some of the Single Pixel X-Ray Transform's mathematical properties. More specifically, we show that the Single Pixel X-ray transform is non-linear, continuous, Fréchet-differentiable and convex. We also introduce a method of reconstructing an object based only on a finite number of dimensionless, noisy Single Pixel X-Ray Transform measurement values. This method is based on Douglas-Rachford splitting and uses total variation denoising. We present an implementation for this method, focusing on rotational symmetric objects, as they allow the use of a one-dimensional direct total variation denoising algorithm [2].

</details>


### [134] [Analysis of Convergence for the IPA-AC Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18777)
*Xiuzhu Yang,Xiaobo Yin*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: IPA-AC方法在固定非局部模型下具有二阶收敛性，但对非局部长度尺度敏感，且不满足渐近兼容条件


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管IPA-AC方法在抑制边界几何误差方面经验上成功，但缺乏对其在不同缩放极限下收敛行为的系统理论分析

Method: 基于Lax等价定理建立统一收敛框架，推导误差估计，分析三个关键极限机制下的性能

Result: 1) 固定δ时，对网格尺寸h具有二阶收敛性O(h²)；2) 固定网格时，离散误差按O(δ⁻²)缩放；3) 方法不满足渐近兼容条件

Conclusion: IPA-AC方法在模拟固定非局部模型时精度优越，但在逼近局部极限时需要足够大的视域-网格比来缓解固有离散误差

Abstract: The Improved Partial Area-Analytical Calculation (IPA-AC) method represents a leading meshfree discretization strategy for peridynamic models, distinguished by its rigorous geometric treatment of boundary intersections via dual corrections of integration weights and quadrature points. Despite its empirical success in suppressing boundary-induced geometric errors, a systematic theoretical characterization of its convergence behaviors under distinct scaling limits has remained elusive. This work establishes a unified convergence framework for the IPA-AC method applied to both scalar and tensor kernels. By leveraging the Lax Equivalence Theorem, we explicitly derive error estimates that reveal the method's performance across three critical limiting regimes. The theoretical analysis, substantiated by numerical validation, demonstrates that: (1) for a fixed horizon $δ$, the method achieves robust second-order convergence $\mathcal{O}(h ^{2})$ with respect to the mesh size $h$; (2) for a fixed mesh, the discretization error scales as $\mathcal{O}(δ^{-2})$, indicating a sensitivity to the nonlocal length scale; and (3) the method does not satisfy the Asymptotic Compatibility (AC) condition. These findings clarify that while the IPA-AC method offers superior accuracy for simulating fixed nonlocal models, it requires a sufficiently large horizon-to-mesh ratio to mitigate intrinsic discretization errors when approximating the local limit.

</details>


### [135] [Model Order Reduction of Cerebrovascular Hemodynamics Using POD_Galerkin and Reservoir Computing_based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18837)
*Rahul Halder,Arash Hajisharifi,Kabir Bakhshaei,Gianluigi Rozza*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文对比了两种模型降阶方法（物理驱动的POD-Galerkin和数据驱动的POD-Reservoir Computing）在脑血管系统非定常血流动力学模拟中的应用，两种方法均实现了10^2-10^3倍的计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是开发高效准确的模型降阶方法，用于模拟脑血管系统中的非定常血流动力学，以克服传统三维计算流体动力学模拟的高计算成本问题。

Method: 方法包括：1）使用本征正交分解将高保真3D CFD快照压缩到低维潜在空间；2）对比两种模型降阶方法：POD-Galerkin（将Navier-Stokes方程投影到降阶基上）和POD-Reservoir Computing（通过循环架构学习系数的时间演化）；3）引入多谐波多振幅训练信号提高训练效率。

Result: 两种方法均实现了相对于全阶模拟10^2到10^3倍的计算加速，能够高效准确地预测壁面剪切应力等流动量，证明了它们作为高效替代模型的潜力。

Conclusion: 物理驱动的POD-Galerkin和数据驱动的POD-Reservoir Computing两种模型降阶方法都能有效应用于脑血管血流动力学模拟，提供计算效率显著提升的准确替代模型，为临床血流动力学评估提供了实用工具。

Abstract: We investigate model order reduction (MOR) strategies for simulating unsteady hemodynamics within cerebrovascular systems, contrasting a physics-based intrusive approach with a data-driven non-intrusive framework. High-fidelity 3D Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) snapshots of an idealised basilar artery bifurcation are first compressed into a low-dimensional latent space using Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD). We evaluate the performance of a POD-Galerkin (POD-G) model, which projects the Navier-Stokes equations onto the reduced basis, against a POD-Reservoir Computing (POD-RC) model that learns the temporal evolution of coefficients through a recurrent architecture. A multi-harmonic and multi-amplitude training signal is introduced to improve training efficiency. Both methodologies achieve computational speed-ups on the order of 10^2 to 10^3 compared to full-order simulations, demonstrating their potential as efficient and accurate surrogates for predicting flow quantities such as wall shear stress.

</details>


### [136] [A divided difference identity for a class of multiple integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18867)
*Michael S. Floater*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文推导了一个将涉及范德蒙多项式的多重积分与差商联系起来的恒等式，提供了差商的积分公式表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究范德蒙多项式与差商之间的关系，探索多重积分与差商之间的数学联系，为差商提供新的积分表示形式。

Method: 推导了一个恒等式，将涉及范德蒙多项式的多重积分与差商联系起来。证明过程中展示了范德蒙多项式的纯偏导数和混合偏导数之和均为零。

Result: 得到了一个连接范德蒙多项式多重积分与差商的恒等式，该恒等式也可视为差商的积分公式。同时证明了范德蒙多项式的纯偏导数和混合偏导数之和为零的数学性质。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了范德蒙多项式多重积分与差商之间的数学联系，提供了差商的积分表示方法，同时发现的偏导数性质可能具有独立的研究价值。

Abstract: We derive an identity that relates a class of multiple integrals involving Vandermonde polynomials to divided differences. Alternatively the identity can be viewed as an integral formula for divided differences. As part of the derivation we show that both sums of pure partial derivatives and mixed partial derivatives of Vandermonde polynomials are zero, which might be of independent interest.

</details>


### [137] [A Stabilized Mortar Method for Discontinuities in Geological Media with Non-Conforming Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18905)
*Daniele Moretto,Andrea Franceschini,Massimiliano Ferronato*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于mortar方法的断层/裂缝力学数值模拟方法，使用拉格朗日乘子表示界面牵引力，通过牵引力跳跃稳定项解决inf-sup稳定性问题，适用于非匹配网格的工业地质应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 断层和裂缝力学的精确数值模拟对地下系统性能和安全评估至关重要，但间断面的离散表示和摩擦接触行为的鲁棒模拟仍是主要挑战。

Method: 采用mortar方法施加接触约束，允许间断面的非匹配性；使用拉格朗日乘子表示界面牵引力；位移采用分段线性，乘子采用分段常数（定义在非mortar侧）；为稳定违反inf-sup条件的公式，开发了牵引力跳跃稳定项；通过宏单元分析自动评估稳定化缩放，无需用户选择参数。

Result: 提出的公式不仅恢复了inf-sup稳定性条件，而且在更细的非mortar侧情况下也能恢复稳定的牵引力分布，而其他inf-sup稳定公式在此情况下会失效；成功模拟了工业地质应用中典型非匹配角点网格的非线性接触条件。

Conclusion: 该方法为断层和裂缝力学的高效数值模拟提供了稳定、实用的解决方案，特别适用于工业地质应用中的非匹配网格，通过自动稳定化策略避免了参数选择的困难。

Abstract: Accurate numerical simulation of fault and fracture mechanics is critical for the performance and safety assessment of many subsurface systems. The discretized representation of discontinuity surfaces and the robust simulation of their frictional contact behavior still represent major challenges. In this work, we use the mortar method to enforce the contact constraints and allow for non-conformity around the discontinuity surface, with a set of Lagrange multipliers playing the role of interface tractions. The formulation combines piecewise linear displacements with piecewise constant multipliers defined on one side of the fault interface (the non-mortar side). This choice for the Lagrange multipliers has a number of advantages from practical and computational viewpoints, but violates the inf-sup stability constraint. In order to stabilize the proposed formulation, we develop a traction-jump stabilization term to be added to the constraint equations. We use a macro-element analysis to derive an algorithmic strategy that automatically evaluates the proper scaling of the stabilization, without requiring any additional user-selected parameter. Numerical experiments demonstrate that the proposed formulation not only restores the inf-sup stability condition, but also recovers stable traction profiles in the presence of finer non-mortar sides, where other inf-sup-stable formulations fail. The proposed method is finally used to simulate non-linear contact conditions in non-conforming corner-point grids typically used in industrial geological applications.

</details>


### [138] [Non-asymptotic uniform in time error bounds for new and old numerical schemes for SPDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18944)
*Can Huang,Michela Ottobre,Gideon Simpson*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文研究随机偏微分方程(SPDEs)的数值方法，提出了一种证明数值格式在非渐近、时间一致误差界的通用方法，特别关注非全局Lipschitz非线性SPDEs，如随机Allen-Cahn方程，并分析了三种避免有限时间爆炸的数值格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究SPDEs数值方法的动机在于：经典半隐式欧拉时间离散化对于Allen-Cahn型SPDEs可能出现有限时间爆炸问题，需要开发能够准确捕捉瞬态和长期动力学且避免爆炸的数值格式。

Method: 1. 提出证明非渐近、时间一致误差界的通用准则；2. 针对非全局Lipschitz非线性SPDEs，分析三种数值格式：完全隐式格式和两种驯化格式；3. 考虑完整的时空离散化方案；4. 利用通用准则证明这些格式的误差界。

Result: 1. 证明了经典半隐式欧拉格式对Allen-Cahn型SPDEs可能出现有限时间爆炸；2. 对完全隐式格式和两种驯化格式证明了非渐近、时间一致的误差界；3. 提供了数值比较结果；4. 所提方法能够确保数值格式准确捕捉瞬态和长期动力学。

Conclusion: 该论文为SPDEs数值分析提供了通用框架，特别解决了非全局Lipschitz非线性问题中的数值稳定性问题。提出的完全隐式格式和驯化格式能够避免经典方法的爆炸问题，并保证长期动力学行为的准确模拟，为复杂SPDEs的可靠数值模拟提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study numerical schemes for Stochastic Partial Differential Equations (SPDEs). We introduce a general method of proof of non-asymptotic uniform in time error bounds on numerical integrators for SPDEs, ensuring the schemes capture both the transient and the long term dynamics faithfully. We then consider SPDEs with non-globally Lipshitz nonlinearities, which include for example the stochastic Allen-Cahn equation and some stochastic advection-diffusion equations. For the case of Allen-Cahn type SPDEs we show that the classic semi-implicit Euler time-discretization can exhibit finite time blow up. This motivates analysing other schemes which do not suffer from this blow-up problem. We consider three numerical schemes for SPDEs with non globally Lipshitz nonlinearity: a fully implicit scheme and two tamed schemes. For these schemes we prove non-asymptotic uniform in time error bounds by leveraging our general criterion, and provide numerical comparisons. While the main emphasis in this paper is on the properties of the time-discretization, the schemes we consider are full space-time discretization of the SPDE.

</details>


### [139] [On Affordable High-Order Entropy-Conservative/Stable and Well-Balanced Methods for Nonconservative Hyperbolic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18978)
*Marco Artiano,Hendrik Ranocha*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了非保守双曲系统的熵保持波动形式，建立了熵保持三点法的完整特征，并将其扩展到多维SBP方法中，推导出多个新的熵保持格式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有熵保持方法主要针对保守系统，而非保守系统的熵保持方法需要更复杂的波动形式。论文旨在提出简单易解释的熵保持波动形式，为算法化构建非保守双曲系统的熵保持方法提供基础。

Method: 提出特定的熵保持波动形式，分析必要和充分条件，获得有限体积框架下熵保持三点法的完整特征。将该公式扩展到多维空间中的求和分部算子方法，包括笛卡尔和曲线网格。利用算法化构造推导多个新的熵保持格式。

Result: 获得了非保守双曲系统熵保持三点法的完整特征，建立了从有限体积方法到SBP框架的自然扩展。推导出理想气体内部能量方程的压缩欧拉方程和色散浅水模型等新熵保持格式。数值实验验证了所提格式的鲁棒性和精度。

Conclusion: 提出的熵保持波动形式简单易解释，允许算法化构建非保守双曲系统的熵保持方法。该方法在有限体积和SBP框架下均有效，能自然扩展到多维和曲线网格，并保持良好平衡性等额外性质。

Abstract: Many entropy-conservative and entropy-stable (summarized as entropy-preserving) methods for hyperbolic conservation laws rely on Tadmor's theory for two-point entropy-preserving numerical fluxes and its higher-order extension via flux differencing using summation-by-parts (SBP) operators, e.g., in discontinuous Galerkin spectral element methods (DGSEMs). The underlying two-point formulations have been extended to nonconservative systems using fluctuations by Castro et al. (2013, doi:10.1137/110845379) with follow-up generalizations to SBP methods. We propose specific forms of entropy-preserving fluctuations for nonconservative hyperbolic systems that are simple to interpret and allow an algorithmic construction of entropy-preserving methods. We analyze necessary and sufficient conditions, and obtain a full characterization of entropy-preserving three-point methods within the finite volume framework. This formulation is extended to SBP methods in multiple space dimensions on Cartesian and curvilinear meshes. Additional properties such as well-balancedness extend naturally from the underlying finite volume method to the SBP framework. We use the algorithmic construction enabled by the chosen formulation to derive several new entropy-preserving schemes for nonconservative hyperbolic systems, e.g., the compressible Euler equations of an ideal gas using the internal energy equation and a dispersive shallow-water model. Numerical experiments show the robustness and accuracy of the proposed schemes.

</details>


### [140] [A bilinear inverse problem with forward operator inaccuracy applied to neonatal atlas-based diffuse optical tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18980)
*Aada Hakula,Pauliina Hirvi,Nuutti Hyvönen*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文提出使用主成分分析(PCA)建模前向算子不准确性，将其转化为双线性张量逆问题，并开发了两种优化算法和吉布斯采样方法，应用于新生儿弥散光学层析成像中解剖图谱不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性逆问题在实际应用中很常见，但前向算子通常基于对研究系统的近似构建。处理逆问题中前向算子的不准确性是一个研究较少的问题，特别是在医学成像等领域，如弥散光学层析成像中解剖图谱与个体解剖结构不匹配导致的灵敏度剖面或雅可比矩阵不准确问题。

Method: 假设有一组候选前向算子矩阵，使用主成分分析(PCA)建模它们相对于均值的变异。将原始线性问题与包含的前向算子不准确性结合成双线性张量逆问题，提出了两种优化算法和吉布斯采样方法来近似求解该问题。

Result: 将算法应用于新生儿弥散光学层析成像，处理29-44周胎龄范围内使用图谱头部解剖模型而非个体解剖模型时存在的灵敏度剖面或雅可比矩阵不准确性。在模拟血流动力学活动重建中，报告了空间定位和对比度噪声比的视觉和数值改进。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一种处理逆问题中前向算子不准确性的新方法，通过PCA建模算子变异并将其转化为双线性张量问题，在新生儿弥散光学层析成像应用中显示出重建质量的改进，为解决医学成像中解剖模型不匹配问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Linear inverse problems are highly common in practical real-world applications from industry to medical imaging. The forward operator is often built on some approximations of the studied system. Handling inaccuracies in the forward operator in the context of inverse problems is a relatively unstudied problem. In this work, we assume that we have a set of candidate forward operator matrices and suggest principal component analysis (PCA) for modeling their variation from the mean. We combine the original linear problem with the included forward operator inaccuracy into a bilinear tensor inverse problem and present two optimization algorithms and Gibbs sampling for approximately solving the problem. As a real-world test case, we apply the algorithms to account for the inaccuracy that is present in the sensitivity profiles or Jacobian matrices in diffuse optical tomography when an atlas-based model of the head anatomy is used instead of the subject's own anatomical model in neonates over a wide range of gestational ages (29--44 weeks). We report visual and numerical improvements in the spatial localization and contrast-to-noise-ratio in reconstructions of simulated hemodynamic activity.

</details>


### [141] [Solving Maxwell's Equations with Mimetic Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19056)
*Johnny Corbino*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种用于求解一维和二维麦克斯韦方程的拟态有限差分方法，该方法使用满足离散扩展高斯散度定理的拟态算子，实现紧凑且物理一致的电磁计算


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种基于拟态算子的有限差分方法，以更物理一致的方式求解麦克斯韦方程，克服传统FDTD方法在某些方面的局限性

Method: 引入满足离散扩展高斯散度定理的拟态算子，构建紧凑的电磁计算框架，使用Mimetic Operators Library Enhanced (MOLE)实现，并在一维和二维问题中验证

Result: 成功实现了一维正弦波与有耗介质板的相互作用模拟，以及二维高斯脉冲与单轴完美匹配层吸收边界条件的数值计算

Conclusion: 拟态有限差分方法为计算电磁学提供了一种紧凑且物理一致的数值框架，能够准确模拟电磁波与复杂介质的相互作用

Abstract: We present a mimetic finite-difference approach for solving Maxwell's equations in one and two spatial dimensions. After introducing the governing equations and the classical Finite-Difference Time-Domain (FDTD) method, we describe mimetic operators that satisfy a discrete analogue of the extended Gauss divergence theorem and show how they lead to a compact, physically consistent formulation for computational electromagnetics. Two numerical examples are presented: a one-dimensional sinusoidal wave interacting with a lossy dielectric slab, and a two-dimensional Gaussian pulse with Uniaxial Perfectly Matched Layer (UPML) absorbing boundary conditions. All implementations use the Mimetic Operators Library Enhanced (MOLE).

</details>


### [142] [A conservative, discontinuous Galerkin, tracer transport scheme using compatible finite elements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19075)
*Timothy C. Andrews,Thomas M. Bendall*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于地球物理流体方程兼容有限元模型的标量示踪剂保守输运方案，通过求解示踪剂密度（混合比乘以干密度）的保守输运方程，确保连续和离散方程中的质量守恒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于混合比的对流输运方程在离散化时难以保持质量守恒，需要开发一种能够确保示踪剂质量严格守恒的输运方案，特别是在Charney-Phillips垂直交错网格中处理不同变量配置时。

Method: 采用保守输运方程求解示踪剂密度（混合比乘以干密度），而非传统的混合比对流方程；使用间断Galerkin输运方案保持离散守恒性；支持两种Charney-Phillips垂直交错配置（共位或交错）；开发质量守恒限制器确保非负性。

Result: 新方案在连续和离散层面均能保持示踪剂质量守恒，确保混合比恒定的一致性；通过终结器玩具化学和湿上升气泡测试验证了方案在物理项耦合和水分物种质量守恒方面的准确性。

Conclusion: 该保守输运方案成功解决了地球物理流体模型中示踪剂质量守恒问题，为兼容有限元框架提供了可靠的示踪剂输运方法，特别适用于需要严格质量守恒的应用场景。

Abstract: This paper outlines a conservative transport scheme for scalar tracers within a compatible finite element model for geophysical fluid equations. Instead of using the advective transport equation for a mixing ratio, a conservative transport equation is solved for the tracer density of the mixing ratio multiplied by the dry density. This ensures mass conservation in the continuous equations, which can be preserved in the discrete equations with a discontinuous Galerkin transport scheme. Our method is designed to work for two placements of the mixing ratio in a Charney-Phillips vertical staggering: either co-located with the dry density or vertically staggered from it. The new scheme is designed to conserve the tracer density and ensure consistency by maintaining a constant mixing ratio. Additionally, a mass-conserving limiter is developed to ensure non-negativity in the co-located configuration. Tests with terminator toy chemistry and a moist rising bubble show the use of the new transport scheme with physics terms and its ability to accurately model mass conservation of moisture species in a dynamical core setup.

</details>


### [143] [Reduced order computation of 2D elastodynamic Green's functions in layered soil using a low-rank tensor approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19080)
*Zainab Farooq,Amar Pashov,Pieter Reumers,Stijn François,Geert Degrande*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于贪婪塔克近似的降阶建模策略，用于高效计算弹性动力学格林函数，显著降低计算和内存需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在参数研究或边界元计算中，评估多个源-接收位置、频率和材料属性的弹性动力学格林函数计算量大且内存密集，需要高效的降阶建模方法。

Method: 采用贪婪塔克近似策略，通过基于适当广义分解的交替最小二乘法逐步构建格林张量的低秩表示，使用Petrov-Galerkin公式提高收敛性和近似精度。

Result: 该方法将多维张量表示为一系列一维基函数和紧凑核心，显著减少了内存需求。在土壤层和分层半空间案例中验证了有效性，并与直接刚度法进行了比较。

Conclusion: 提出的降阶建模策略能够高效处理弹性动力学格林函数的计算，适用于包含多种参数组合的复杂场景，为参数研究和边界元计算提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: The evaluation of elastodynamic Green's functions across numerous source-receiver locations, frequencies, and material properties, particularly in the context of parametric studies or boundary element computations, is computationally demanding and memory intensive. This paper presents a reduced order modeling strategy based on the Greedy Tucker Approximation (GTA), which incrementally constructs a low-rank representation of the Green's tensor through rank-one enrichments obtained via a Proper Generalized Decomposition (PGD)-type alternating least squares procedure. A Petrov-Galerkin formulation is employed to improve convergence and approximation accuracy. The resulting multi-dimensional tensor, expressed in terms of one-dimensional basis functions and a compact core, achieves substantial reductions in memory requirements. The methodology is demonstrated for two cases: a soil layer on rigid bedrock and a layered halfspace. Different separable dimensions are considered to capture various combinations of source and receiver configurations, frequencies, and material parameters. Results are validated against those obtained with the direct stiffness method and computation times and memory requirements are compared.

</details>


### [144] [Numerical Considerations for the Construction of Karhunen-Loève Expansions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.19108)
*Cosmin Safta,Habib N. Najm*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该报告研究了构建二阶随机过程Karhunen-Loève展开(KLE)的数值方面，包括Fredholm积分方程离散化、SVD等价性、解析基准测试以及高维复杂几何的数值实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究KLE数值构造中的关键问题：离散化策略、积分规则和样本数量对结果的影响，为模型驱动和数据驱动的KLE构建提供一致的数值框架。

Method: 1) 推导Fredholm积分方程离散化与加权样本矩阵SVD的代数等价性；2) 使用指数和平方指数协方差核作为解析基准；3) 在一维、二维不规则网格和三维环形域上测试数值方法；4) 分析特征值估计收敛性和KL系数分布收敛性。

Result: 1) 证明了Fredholm特征解与加权样本矩阵SVD的代数等价性；2) 建立了数值一致性和准确性评估框架；3) 特征值估计和KL系数分布随样本数增加收敛到理论目标；4) 在复杂几何中展示了离散化策略、积分规则和样本数的相互影响。

Conclusion: KLE数值构造需要仔细考虑离散化策略、积分规则和样本数量的相互作用，Fredholm方法与SVD方法在代数上等价，为模型驱动和数据驱动的KLE构建提供了统一的数值框架。

Abstract: This report examines numerical aspects of constructing Karhunen-Loève expansions (KLEs) for second-order stochastic processes. The KLE relies on the spectral decomposition of the covariance operator via the Fredholm integral equation of the second kind, which is then discretized on a computational grid, leading to an eigendecomposition task. We derive the algebraic equivalence between this Fredholm-based eigensolution and the singular value decomposition of the weight-scaled sample matrix, yielding consistent solutions for both model-based and data-driven KLE construction. Analytical eigensolutions for exponential and squared-exponential covariance kernels serve as reference benchmarks to assess numerical consistency and accuracy in 1D settings. The convergence of SVD-based eigenvalue estimates and of the empirical distributions of the KL coefficients to their theoretical $\mathcal{N}(0,1)$ target are characterized as a function of sample count. Higher-dimensional configurations include a two-dimensional irregular domain discretized by unstructured triangular meshes with two refinement levels, and a three-dimensional toroidal domain whose non-simply-connected topology motivates a comparison between Euclidean and shortest interior path distances between the grid points. The numerical results highlight the interplay between the discretization strategy, quadrature rule, and sample count, and their impact on the KLE results.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [145] [Borsuk-Ulam type theorem for Stiefel manifolds and orthogonal mass partitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18550)
*Oleg R. Musin*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 将Borsuk-Ulam定理推广到Stiefel流形，用于保证在给定m个测度下存在k个相互正交的超平面，其中任意n个超平面能将每个测度等分为2^n份


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在欧几里得空间中，对于给定的多个测度，如何保证存在相互正交的超平面系统，使得这些超平面能够以特定方式等分所有测度。这扩展了经典的测度等分问题，增加了正交性约束

Method: 将Borsuk-Ulam定理推广到Stiefel流形，利用拓扑方法推导存在性条件。Stiefel流形是正交标架的空间，适合处理正交超平面问题。通过拓扑不变量和映射度分析，建立存在正交超平面系统的充分条件

Result: 得到了保证存在k个相互正交超平面的维度d的下界条件，其中任意n个超平面能将每个测度等分为2^n份。当n=k时，结果与文献[11]的界一致，但增加了超平面相互正交的更强结论

Conclusion: 成功将Borsuk-Ulam定理推广到Stiefel流形，为具有正交性约束的测度等分问题提供了拓扑证明方法，扩展了经典结果的应用范围

Abstract: A generalization of the Borsuk-Ulam theorem to Stiefel manifolds is considered. This theorem is applied to derive bounds on $d$ that guarantee--for a given set of $m$ measures in $\mathbb{R}^d$--the existence of $k$ mutually orthogonal hyperplanes, any $n$ of which partition each of the measures into $2^n$ equal parts. If $n=k$, the result corresponds to the bound obtained in [11], but with the stronger conclusion that the hyperplanes are mutually orthogonal.

</details>


### [146] [Cohomology of dilute Temperley--Lieb algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18704)
*Andrew Fisher,Daniel Graves*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文证明了稀Temperley-Lieb代数的(上)同调在所有正度数上消失


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀Temperley-Lieb代数是Temperley-Lieb代数的变体，源于统计力学中可解晶格模型的研究。研究其同调性质有助于理解这些代数结构及其在物理模型中的应用。

Method: 作者采用代数同调理论的方法，对稀Temperley-Lieb代数的同调结构进行分析和证明。具体技术细节未在摘要中详细说明，但涉及代数的同调计算。

Result: 主要结果是证明了稀Temperley-Lieb代数的(上)同调在所有正度数上消失，即H^n(A)=0对所有n>0成立。

Conclusion: 稀Temperley-Lieb代数具有平凡的同调结构，这一结果深化了对这类代数变体的理解，并为相关统计力学模型的研究提供了代数基础。

Abstract: Dilute Temperley--Lieb algebras are variants of Temperley--Lieb algebras arising in statistical mechanics in the study of solvable lattice models. In this paper we prove that the (co)homology of dilute Temperley--Lieb algebras vanishes in all positive degrees.

</details>


### [147] [On Topological André-Quillen homology of Eilenberg-MacLane spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.18932)
*Cyril Barlasov*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 计算Eilenberg-MacLane谱（如HZ和HZ/p^n）的拓扑André-Quillen同调，扩展了Basterra-Mandell对HF_p的工作


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于Dundas、Lindenstrauss和Richter的工作，计算Eilenberg-MacLane谱的拓扑André-Quillen同调，特别是HZ和HZ/p^n的情况。HF_p的情况已在Basterra-Mandell未发表工作中解决，后由Brantner和Mathew在谱划分李代数背景下完善。

Method: 采用类似于Cartan计算Steenrod代数的方法，但更为简洁。基于Dundas-Lindenstrauss-Richter的工作框架，计算拓扑André-Quillen同调，并特别处理相对于HZ基的计算。

Result: 成功计算了HZ和HZ/p^n等Eilenberg-MacLane谱的拓扑André-Quillen同调，提供了相对于HZ基的具体计算结果，扩展了先前仅针对HF_p的工作。

Conclusion: 该方法有效计算了Eilenberg-MacLane谱的拓扑André-Quillen同调，提供了一种比Cartan计算Steenrod代数更简洁的途径，并建立了与谱划分李代数理论的联系。

Abstract: Based on the work of Dundas, Lindenstrauss and Richter we compute the topological André-Quillen homology with reduced coefficients for Eilenberg-MacLane spectra such as $H\mathbb{Z}$ and $H\mathbb{Z}/p^n$. The case of $H\mathbb{F}_p$ was settled in an unpublished work of Basterra and Mandell, which was refined later by Brantner and Mathew in the context of spectral partition Lie algebras. Our approach is similar to Cartan's calculation of the Steenrod algebra, and eventually shorter. We also present some computations relative to the $H\mathbb{Z}$ base.

</details>
